### [1] [Edge-cusped compactons from gradient-power nonlinear dispersion: exact transitions and singular spectral structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00552)
*Francisco R. Villatoro*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文引入一个守恒型双指数色散方程族（非线性通量指数 p>1，梯度色散指数 r>0），在保留显式行波理论的同时，将紧致化、自由边界正则性与线性化谱结构三种机制分离。该族在 r=1 时退化为绝对 Rosenau-Hyman 方程。文章识别出两条独立相变曲线 pr=1（区分无穷尾孤立波与紧子）和 r(p-1)=2（区分正则自由边界紧子与边缘尖点紧子），并通过数值实验连接精确结构与逼近、动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧子（compacton）现象中，紧致支撑、自由边界正则性、线性化谱结构与数值逼近误差常相互纠缠，缺乏一个能独立调节各机制的模型。作者构造双指数族以分离这些机制，同时保持可解析的行波理论与显式谱结构，为在同一退化色散模型中研究几何奇异性、弱可容许性、谱结构与数值正则性提供理想试验平台。

Method: 构造守恒型双指数色散方程族，以 p 控制非线性通量、r 控制梯度色散；推导显式行波解、振幅-速度律与宽度-振幅依赖关系；解析确定两条相变曲线 pr=1 与 r(p-1)=2；对 r≥1 情形将线性化约化为奇异 Sturm-Liouville 问题，经紧子适配变量替换化为 Jacobi-超几何型特征值方程，求两条显式谱序列；开展 Fourier 尾衰减、投影误差、Fourier-Galerkin 时间积分与 Jacobi-Galerkin 谱诊断等数值实验。

Result: 相变曲线 pr=1 区分无穷尾孤立波与紧子；r(p-1)=2 区分正则自由边界紧子与斜率发散的边缘尖点紧子，后者仍为分布解且自由边界与波峰处均无奇异测度。r≥1 时线性化经紧子适配变量替换后为 Jacobi-超几何型特征值方程，具两条显式谱序列、恰一个负特征值与一维平移核。数值上，Fourier 尾与投影误差速率与自由边界指数预测一致；带填充 Fourier-Galerkin 时间积分可使尖点波相干传播且相位漂移小；结构适配 Jacobi-Galerkin 诊断在全生成元的截断点谱中未检出实部。

Conclusion: 该双指数色散族提供了一个显式可解的试验平台，使紧子的几何奇异性、弱可容许性、线性化谱结构与数值正则性能够在单一退化色散模型内统一研究，并揭示了控制紧致化、自由边界正则性与谱结构的两条独立相变机制。

Abstract: We introduce a conservative two-exponent dispersive family, with $p>1$ the nonlinear-flux exponent and $r>0$ the gradient-dispersion exponent, that separates the mechanisms governing compactification, free-boundary regularity and linearised spectral structure while retaining an explicit travelling-wave theory. The family reduces to the absolute Rosenau-Hyman equation when $r=1$. The deformation leaves the amplitude-speed law unchanged, but makes the width amplitude dependent except at $r=1$. Two independent transition curves emerge: $pr=1$ separates infinite-tail solitary waves from compactons, whereas $r(p-1)=2$ separates compactons with a regular free boundary from edge-cusped compactons with divergent slope. The latter remain distributional solutions, with no singular measures at either the free boundary or the crest. For $r\ge1$, the linearisation factors through a singular Sturm-Liouville operator whose natural Friedrichs realisation is solvable: after a compacton-adapted change of variable, the eigenvalue equation is of Jacobi-hypergeometric type, with two explicit spectral sequences, exactly one negative eigenvalue and a one-dimensional translation kernel. Numerical experiments connect these exact structures to approximation and dynamics. Measured Fourier tails and projection errors agree with the rates predicted by the free-boundary exponent, while padded Fourier-Galerkin time integration propagates the cusped waves coherently with small phase drift. A structure-adapted Jacobi-Galerkin diagnostic of the full generator finds no detectable real part in the tested truncated point spectra. The family therefore provides an explicit testbed in which geometric singularity, weak admissibility, spectral structure and numerical regularity of compactons can be studied within a single degenerate dispersive model.

</details>


### [2] [Discrete PT-symmetric solitons on branched lattices: Vertex states, stability, and vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00774)
*M. Akramov,A. Fayzullaev,O. Tojakhmadova,T. Akhmadjanov*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 研究PT对称四边星形图上的离散非线性薛定谔方程，从反连续极限延拓构造出一类由分支几何支持的离散涡旋态，揭示了其携带电流、具有独特稳定窗口等性质，并讨论了电路实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分支结构引入了在一维晶格中不存在的顶点自由度，从而从根本上改变非线性局域化行为。既有研究多集中于一维链或环状晶格，对分支图（如星形图）中拓扑局域态的演化规律，尤其是在PT对称（平衡增益与损耗）条件下的行为，尚缺乏系统认识，因此需要研究分支几何对局域态及其稳定性的影响。

Method: 以PT对称的四边星形图（four-edge star graph）为模型平台，采用离散非线性薛定谔方程描述系统。通过从反连续极限（anticontinuum limit）进行延拓（continuation）来构造局域解，分析其拓扑荷、在平衡增益与损耗下的电流特性，并考察线性稳定性，得到稳定窗口；最后提出基于电路的物理实现方案。

Result: 识别出一类具有固定拓扑荷的离散涡旋态，该态由分支几何所支持；从反连续极限延拓得到的涡旋态在平衡增益与损耗存在时演化为携带电流的态，并表现出常规分支所不具备的稳定性窗口；同时给出了该模型的可行电路实现。

Conclusion: 分支几何可支持具有固定拓扑荷的离散涡旋态，并赋予其在PT对称条件下携带电流与独有的稳定窗口，说明分支结构是调控非线性局域化的有效自由度，也为在电路等可操控平台上实验验证提供了途径。

Abstract: Branching fundamentally modifies nonlinear localization by introducing vertex degrees of freedom absent in one-dimensional lattices. We investigate localized states in a PT-symmetric four-edge star graph described by the discrete nonlinear Schrödinger equation. We identify a discrete vortex state with a fixed topological charge, supported by the branching geometry. Constructed by continuation from the anticontinuum limit, the vortex evolves into a current-carrying state in the presence of balanced gain and loss and exhibits stability windows absent for the conventional branches. A possible electric-circuit realization of the proposed model is discussed.

</details>


### [3] [The role of root allometry and root overlap in vegetation pattern formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01730)
*Jelle van der Voort,Ricardo Martinez-Garcia,Arjen Doelman*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该研究从显式植被-水系统出发，推导出含双积分结构的非局部标量植被模型，揭示了根系异速生长与根系重叠导致的两类图灵失稳机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非局部植被模型多用单一卷积项描述资源竞争，忽略了根系异速生长（根系空间范围随地上生物量增加）与根系重叠引起的竞争效应，可能影响对植被斑图形成条件的判断。

Method: 建立空间显式的双组分植被-水系统，植物通过横向扩展的根系吸水，根系异速生长由随地上生物量密度增大而扩展的相互作用核表示；在快水动力学极限下消去水变量，得到非局部标量植被动力学模型，并对其进行图灵失稳分析。

Result: 模型约化表明，考虑根系重叠自然导致标量模型中出现双积分结构，区别于常用的单卷积竞争项。识别出两种驱动图灵失稳的机制：其一要求根系生长快于死亡率；其二依赖竞争压力区的形成，即邻近高生物量密度区域施加的累积竞争压力抑制植被生长。根系异速生长促进两类失稳机制，而根系重叠抑制竞争压力区的出现。

Conclusion: 根系异速生长和根系重叠都是研究植被斑图形成时需要考虑的重要特征，二者对图灵失稳具有相反的调控作用。

Abstract: We investigate how root allometry and below-ground root competition due to root overlap affect the conditions for pattern formation in non-local scalar vegetation models. We start from a spatially explicit two-component vegetation-water system in which plants acquire soil water through their laterally extended root systems, with root allometry represented by an interaction kernel whose spatial extent increases with the above-ground biomass density. In the limit of fast water dynamics, we eliminate the water variable, resulting in a non-local scalar model for the vegetation dynamics. This reduction reveals that accounting for root overlap naturally leads to a double integral structure in the scalar model, in contrast to the single convolution term commonly used to model non-local competition for resources. For the resulting scalar model, we identify two distinct mechanisms that can drive Turing instabilities. The first instability mechanism requires root growth to outpace mortality, while the second mechanism relies on the development of competitive pressure zones, in which vegetation growth is impeded by the cumulative competitive pressure exerted by neighbouring areas with higher biomass density. Root allometry promotes the occurrence of both instability mechanisms, while root overlap inhibits the emergence of competitive pressure zones. Hence, our results show that both root allometry and root overlap are important features to consider when studying vegetation pattern formation.

</details>


### [4] [Stability and instability analysis of resonance-induced nonlinear bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01875)
*Jackson C. Turner,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究紧支撑势下的一维聚焦立方NLS-GP方程，针对由线性算子的透射/散射共振所诱导的非线性束缚态，建立了其在分岔点附近的轨道稳定性与不稳定性的精确判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（turner2026resonance）证明线性算子的纯虚零点（透射共振）或下半平面纯虚极点（散射共振）会生成共振诱导的非线性束缚态分支，且这些态在严格正L^2激发阈值处分岔，区别于由点谱（上半平面极点）在零L^2范数处分岔的普通非线性束缚态。但这类共振诱导态的稳定性尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以紧支撑势H_V=-∂_x^2+V(x)的反射系数共振为出发点，分析共振诱导非线性束缚态在分岔点邻域的扰动演化，通过构造相应的谱与轨道稳定性分析框架，给出轨

Result: 得到共振诱导非线性束缚态在分岔点附近轨道稳定性/不稳定性的精确判据。作为推论：当i) 对应线性（散射或透射）共振频率足够小，且ii) 相应的线性共振本征模严格为正时，共振诱导非线性态是轨道稳定的。并辅以数值模拟探索理论未覆盖的区域以及稳定/不稳定态附近的长时间动力学。

Conclusion: 共振诱导非线性束缚态的轨道稳定性可由共振频率大小与线性共振本征模的正性来判定，扩展了零范数分岔束缚态的稳定性理论，并通过数值模拟揭示了更大参数范围及长时间演化行为。

Abstract: We study the focusing one-dimensional cubic nonlinear Schrödinger / Gross--Pitaevskii equation (NLS-GP), where the potential of the underlying linear Schrödinger operator, $H_V=-\partial_x^2+V(x)$, is compactly supported. In \cite{turner2026resonance} the authors proved that purely imaginary zeros (transmission resonances) or purely imaginary poles in the lower half plane (scattering resonances) of a reflection coefficient of $H_V$ seed branches of {\it resonance-induced nonlinear bound states}, and that these states bifurcate at a strictly positive $L^2(\mathbb R)$ excitation threshold. This is in contrast to nonlinear bound states which bifurcate at zero $L^2$-norm from point spectra of $H_V$ (corresponding to poles in the upper half plane). In this paper we establish precise criteria for the nonlinear orbital stability and instability of the resonance-induced states near the bifurcation point. A corollary is that resonance-induced nonlinear states are orbitally stable if i) the underlying linear (scattering or transmission) resonance frequency is sufficiently small, and ii) the corresponding linear resonance eigenmode is strictly positive. Numerical simulations are presented to explore regimes not accessible to our theory, and to explore the large time dynamics for initial conditions near stable and unstable resonance-induced nonlinear bound states.

</details>

### [5] [Beyond Lie Algebras: Lie-Wedge Stratification and Pure-State Stabilizability in Single-Channel Qubit Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00046)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入"李楔"（Lie wedge）刻画开放量子系统中可逆控制与不可逆耗散的区别，对无漂移单量子比特系统（双向哈密顿控制 + 至多一个由无迹跃迁算符生成的独立耗散器）给出十三类结构分层，并用该分层判定楔中是否存在具有全局吸引唯一纯态的生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李代数只能描述控制生成元如何组合，但在开放系统中会抹去可逆控制与不可逆耗散之间的本质区别；作者希望用保留该信息的李楔结构，刻画开放量子控制系统的控制能力。

Method: 研究无漂移单量子比特系统，含双向哈密顿控制与至多一个独立驱动的耗散器（由无迹跃迁算符生成）。利用生成代数与李楔几何进行分类，证明系统落入十三种结构分层；控制代数与四个信道性质共同决定所属分层，楔中的可逆方向恰为相干控制方向。

Result: 证明系统存在十三种结构分层；在每一楔中可逆方向恰为相干控制方向。据此判定楔中是否存在具有唯一全局吸引纯态的生成元：其中五个分层的可稳定性依赖额外信道数据；一个特殊分层中单个控制旋转的耗散方向不足，但楔中两个此类方向取平均可实现稳定化。

Conclusion: 李楔能揭示生成李代数所遗漏的控制能力，表明在开放量子系统中必须区分可逆控制与不可逆耗散才能准确刻画可实现的控制目标。

Abstract: Lie algebras describe how control generators combine, but in open systems they erase the distinction between reversible control and irreversible dissipation. Lie wedges retain this information. We study drift-free single-qubit systems with bidirectional Hamiltonian controls and at most one independently actuated dissipator generated by a traceless jump operator. In this work, we prove that they fall into thirteen structural strata defined by the generated algebra and its Lie-wedge geometry. The control algebra and four channel properties determine the stratum; in each wedge, the reversible directions are precisely the coherent controls. We then use the stratification to determine whether a wedge contains a generator with a unique globally attractive pure state. In five strata, stabilizability depends on additional channel data. In one exceptional stratum, no single control-rotated dissipative direction suffices, but averaging two such directions in the wedge yields stabilization. Lie wedges therefore reveal control capabilities that the generated Lie algebra misses.

</details>


### [6] [Sharp Target-Domain Certificates for Quantum-Kernel Advantage under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00080)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Asier González-Santocildes,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出无假设的锐利识别区间，量化固定量子候选相对经典核族在标签偏移下的有限批次预测优势，并给出零一损失的闭式部分标签更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子预测优势的评估通常依赖已知目标标签，在标签偏移与未审标签不完整时结论不可靠。论文旨在去除这一标签假设，分离协议有效性、目标预测不可或缺性与量子相关性。

Method: 推导任意有界损失下、未审标签可任意补全时的锐利识别区间，对零一精度给出精确闭式部分标签更新；在八个安全偏移上以115个经典核为参照，并用回顾性审计与前瞻性验证（预定义判据、任务-分类器中位数、效应符号、特征门控）进行实证。

Result: 八个偏移下零标签上端点相对115个经典核为0.002–0.088；回顾性审计将每个端点降至0.010，仅需0–33/500个标签，涵盖纠缠-ZZ模型。前瞻性验证中两个合格任务的四个任务-分类器中位数需0–3个标签（总体中位数0，最大76），20个实现效应全为负；第三个任务未通过特征门控。协议对照可反转表观优势，有限样本噪声可提高预测区分度但不带来有用优势。

Conclusion: 该区间框架在无标签假设下给出量子预测优势的严格识别，审计结果表明显著优势可被少量标签消除，强调需区分协议有效性、目标预测不可或缺性与量子相关性。

Abstract: Quantum predictive advantage under shift usually requires known target labels. We derive the assumption-free sharp identified interval for the finite-batch advantage of a fixed candidate over the best member of a prespecified fixed classical-kernel family under any bounded loss and unrestricted completions of the unaudited labels; zero-one accuracy has exact closed-form partial-label updates. Across eight security shifts, zero-label upper endpoints against 115 classical kernels span 0.002-0.088. Retrospective auditing reduces every endpoint to 0.010 with 0-33 of 500 labels, including entangling-ZZ models. Prospective corroboration on two eligible tasks met predefined criteria: four task-classifier medians require 0-3 labels (overall median 0; maximum 76), and all 20 realized effects are negative; a third task fails its feature gate. Protocol controls reverse an apparent advantage, while finite-shot noise can increase predictive distinctness without useful advantage. The framework separates protocol validity, target predictive indispensability, and quantum relevance.

</details>


### [7] [Exact Posterior Prediction from Product Haar Measurements and a Randomized-Mesh Maximum-Likelihood Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00117)
*Naqueeb Ahmad Warsi,Ayanava Dasgupta,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知有限维纯量子态在Haar POVM单拷贝测量下的预测问题，给出后验均值的确切永久式表示、极小极大性与有限样本相对熵保证，并通过有限射影网格参考实验得到更尖锐的渐近尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在观测副本经单拷贝Haar POVM测量后，如何预测一个未观测副本的未知有限维纯量子态。现有基于固定可分离观测的贝叶斯预测与精细乘积数据向规则射线型实验的衔接尚需精确刻画，且希望在不假设确定性有限样本唯一性的前提下获得熵损失与后验纯度的样本复杂度保证。

Method: 在量子相对熵损失下取全秩后验均值作为贝叶斯预测态，通过结果Gram矩阵子式的永久式精确求值并证明其正性与归一化；基于固定结果建立决策规则的极小极大性。利用后验均值的Hilbert-Schmidt投影与精确分析的线性反演竞争者，证明有限样本后验纯度界与相对熵保证。为连接精细乘积数据与规则射线型实验，对有限射影网格抽取一个随机方向并在整个样本中保持，应用选定的有限字母MLE，通过嵌套恢复网格均匀恢复精细Fisher信息。

Result: 得到后验均值的确切永久式表示及正性、归一化性质，证明固定结果下决策规则的极小极大性；获得有限样本后验纯度界与相对熵保证，样本数达O((d²/ε)log(d/ε))。通过有限网格参考实验，选定MLE的Haar平均逆Fisher系数为ā_k，网格细化后ā_k趋近d-1，从而给出ε最优的主导相对熵与后验重叠界，得到更尖锐的固定维渐近尺度(d/ε)log(d/ε)。

Conclusion: 精细后验可由有限射影网格上的规则有限字母实验逼近，其选定MLE的Haar平均逆Fisher系数随网格细化趋于d-1，由此获得ε最优的相对熵与后验重叠界及(d/ε)log(d/ε)的固定维渐近尺度。该渐近尺度不作为统一有限样本保证，且未使用确定性有限样本MLE唯一性或三角网格调度。

Abstract: We study prediction of one unmeasured copy of an unknown finite-dimensional pure quantum state after independently measuring the observed copies with the one-copy Haar POVM. For the resulting fixed separable observation, the Bayes predictive state under quantum relative-entropy loss is the full-rank posterior mean. We evaluate it exactly through permanents of minors of the outcome Gram matrix, prove positivity and normalization of the permanent representation, and establish minimaxity among decision rules based on these fixed outcomes. Using Hilbert--Schmidt projection of the posterior mean and an exactly analyzed linear-inversion competitor, we prove a finite-sample posterior-purity bound and an explicit relative-entropy guarantee with sample count $O((d^2/ε)\log(d/ε))$. Using the exact collective benchmarks proved in a companion paper, we also obtain entropy and posterior-purity comparisons. Our proof uses a family of suitably tuned qubit semigroups to transform a general log-Sobolev entropy bound into supporting lines of Wootters' function. Matching this bound with the entanglement of formation also determines the exact cost for a broader class of two-qubit states. For qudit isotropic states, we derive another general lower bound from the full depolarizing $2\to 3$ norm. This bound substantially improves on the PPT-relative entropy of entanglement and nearly matches the entanglement of formation, leaving a gap of at most $2\%$ of $\log d$ that vanishes as $d$ grows. We derive its sampling variance, compare it with Gaussian random-direction smoothing, and formulate the resulting stochastic dynamics through a Fokker--Planck equation. Abstract: Quantum state tomography provides a foundation for characterizing state preparation and predicting measurement outcomes. We characterize the minimax expected trace norm risk of quantum state tomography over classes with polynomial spectral decay under randomized nonadaptive single copy measurements. Under an explicit sufficient block size condition for the two layer periodic Clifford ensemble, we establish statewise expected trace norm oracle inequalities for operator norm minimum distance and measurement weighted projected least squares, with bounds that depend on each state's actual spectral tail, while the estimators require no prior knowledge of the state's structural characteristics, including its rank, eigenbasis, or spectral structure. Over these polynomial spectral decay classes, both estimators attain the minimax expected trace norm rates up to constants. As a direct consequence, the same estimators attain the minimax rate over every fixed rank constrained class, while remaining adaptive to the unknown structural characteristics of the state. The bound is sharp in dimension and holds for arbitrary test states, including those entangled across blocks. Covariance concentration and positivity yield adaptive oracle bounds, while a shared rotation information argument on spectral tail packings establishes matching lower bounds. We also provide polynomial time reconstruction algorithms, numerical comparisons, and Lean formalizations of all labeled theoretical results.

For fixed input, unitary realizations are unique up to simultaneous unitary conjugation. We address two questions.

The output decomposes into two trivial and two reduced Burau summands, and an $n(n-1)$-dimensional summand isomorphic to Long's reduced construction, generically irreducible for $n=3,4$. If $a$ or $c$ is irrational, every generator is non-Clifford in every finite-dimensional Weyl-Heisenberg frame; if $1,a,c$ are linearly independent over $\mathbb{Q}$, every generator is entangling for every nontrivial bipartition. Abstract: Secret sharing is ubiquitous throughout cryptography. On the other hand, we give a novel obstruction we call the Clique Path obstruction, which applies to arbitrary access structures. For thresholds, it shows that QSS is impossible whenever $t\leq (n-1)/k$. Our upper and lower bounds exactly match for $k=2$. Our results clarify the limitations of uncertainty-based resolutions and the causal trade-offs faced by superluminal extensions of relativity.

Abstract: Local-unitary (LU) equivalence asks whether two quantum states differ only by independent changes of basis on their qubits. For graph states, whether this relation can be decided in polynomial time has remained open for over a decade. Building on Claudet and Perdrix's quasipolynomial algorithm, we replace the enumeration of vertex subsets by a compact system of constraints generated from pairs and triples. The remaining graph transformation is found by solving linear equations over the binary field. These new steps cost $\widetilde O(n^5)$ bit operations; the inherited graph preprocessing sets the overall bound. We also count the local-Clifford (LC) classes of graph states within any LU class: their number is a power of two, computable within the same bound. For any given graph state, this decides whether single-qubit Clifford gates reach every graph state in its LU class, and supplies a counterexample when they do not. Abstract: The Hamiltonian of a natural quantum system is usually determined indirectly, by proposing microscopic models and comparing their predicted observables with experiment. A more direct approach is to extract information about the Hamiltonian from measurements on the system itself. In many settings, however, direct access to the full system is unavailable and measurements can be performed only through controllable probes, as in quantum sensing and boundary spectroscopy. Existing Hamiltonian learning protocols under such restricted access typically require a special symmetry, a known parametric form, or controlled operations beyond free evolution. We introduce Quantum Krylov Learning (QKL), a restricted-access framework that avoids these requirements. QKL uses single temperature probe autocorrelators and their operator Krylov structure to extract model-independent spectral information about the unknown system. Rather than fitting a parametrized Hamiltonian, it reconstructs the operator Krylov Jacobi matrix representing the dynamical modes accessible to the probe. The method assumes that the unobserved system is at, or near, infinite temperature. Under this condition, the Jacobi matrix can be reconstructed directly from the autocorrelation of a probe observable, without oracle access to the Hamiltonian. The required operations are free evolution under the unknown $H$, together with probe state preparation and measurement. The learning core therefore requires no symmetry, no parametric model, and no controlled-unitary gates. We show a Lieb--Robinson bound for the operator Krylov chain and derive a data-driven criterion for the required observation window. We also establish an end-to-end sample complexity guarantee: under generic conditions on the spectral gaps and weights, the number of measurement shots required to achieve spectral precision $\varepsilon$ is polynomial in $1/\varepsilon$.

The query specifies what is computed; the evaluation plan, semiring, and representation of intermediate factors determine the cost.

Over a formal phase semiring, compressed intermediate factors recover rank-width-based simulation for compatible quadratic phase representations. For Clifford circuits, affine-quadratic factors are closed under multiplication and marginalization and remain polynomial in size, yielding polynomial-time exact amplitude computation without any bounded-width assumption.

This parameter combines local cut-rank with separator symmetry through exact tree-tensor representations. We give a constructive evaluation bound and exhibit a circuit family with bounded tensor layout symmetry width but unbounded phase-graph rank-width and circuit line-graph treewidth. These results establish representation-aware FAQ evaluation as a common algorithmic foundation for classical simulation and a systematic route to new tractable regimes.

The selected candidates initialize independent learned sequential BP continuations from the same decoder state. Since these continuations are independent, they can be executed in parallel, so testing several candidates mainly increases parallel hardware resources rather than sequential decoding latency. Abstract: The task of learning the Hamiltonian interactions governing a quantum system, given samples of its thermal (or 'Gibbs') states, is a foundational question at the intersection of quantum learning theory and many-body physics. We leverage the stationarity test to address the following applications:
  1. To give the first learning algorithm for the underlying interaction graph, i.e. structure learning, of lattice Hamiltonians at all temperatures, given copies of their Gibbs states.
  2. To give the first learning algorithm for the coefficients of a lattice Hamiltonian, given only copies of its thermal metastable states, modeled as the "local minima" of the free energy.

Abstract: Measuring part of a many-body wave function generates a projected ensemble of pure quantum states on the unmeasured subsystem. Our results establish the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble as a universal description of deep thermalization in free-fermion systems.

Abstract: Measurement incompatibility is a fundamental quantum resource that enables advantages in many quantum information tasks, including cryptography and communication. However, unavoidable interactions between a system and its environment can degrade or even completely destroy measurement incompatibility; such a process is referred to as a measurement-incompatibility-annihilating channel. Recently, the capability of noisy quantum dynamics to preserve measurement incompatibility has been characterized within the resource theory of measurement incompatibility preservability. We demonstrate that while pre-filtering operations can strengthen measurement incompatibility preservability, they cannot activate it from an incompatibility-annihilating channel, establishing a no-go theorem. Furthermore, we explicitly demonstrate that an incompatibility-annihilating channel can be stochastically activated via post-filtering operations. Our results provide a practical framework for exploiting measurement incompatibility in quantum information processing.

Abstract: Barren plateaus hinder training of parameterized quantum circuits by making loss gradients exponentially small in the number of qubits. For angle-encoded product states, we show how the input distribution affects output variation through algebraic input purity, i.e. the input's overlap with the circuit's dynamical Lie algebra. With the specified readouts and Haar or exact group 2-design sampling, matchgate circuits on $n$ qubits retain output variance of order $1/n$ for every pure product input. The off-diagonal family instead has zero output on computational-basis inputs at any depth and for every parameter choice. Independent uniform angles yield mean variance of the same order as matchgates. A count of diagonal Pauli strings identifies these zero-output endpoints. For any fixed dataset of nonzero inputs with at least $2n$ coordinates, we construct a classical preprocessing certificate. A shared random rotation is accepted only when the dataset's mean purity passes a computable threshold. This certifies mean output variance of order $1/n$ for the off-diagonal family before circuit execution, with a constant expected number of trials. Binary and ternary weighted encodings also recover the independent-angle mean purity from one uniform scalar input. Numerical experiments examine training behavior and extensions to larger algebras. The guarantee concerns output variance averaged over inputs, while successful learning also depends on the task and information preserved by the encoding.

</details>

Abstract: Indefinite causal order is known to offer enhancements in quantum metrology, notably including an unbounded advantage in the measurement of a geometric phase of a harmonic oscillator. This advantage, however, is specific to the infinite dimensional setting, and its finite dimensional analogue remains elusive, with recent findings suggesting that advantages in finite dimensions may be fundamentally limited to bounded constant factors. Here we show that, in fact, arbitrarily large advantages arise for finite dimensional systems in the finite sample regime. Specifically, we establish an unbounded separation between definite and indefinite causal order in the estimation of a geometric phase associated to two sets of $N$ displacements generated by discrete position and momentum operators on a $d$-dimensional quantum system: for any given constant $R$, there exist values of $N$ and $d=Ω(N^2)$ such that a strategy with indefinite order uses an initial probe with $R$ times less energy than the probe required by every strategy with definite order achieving the same mean squared error, whenever the number of measurement shots $ν$ is bounded as $ν=\mathcal{O}(\exp(πd/16)/\mathrm{poly}(d))$. In other words, indefinite order offers an energy saving that grows arbitrarily large with the parameters of the problem. To prove this result, we establish an approximate Weyl relation for discrete Gaussian wavepackets, which is of independent technical interest.

</details>

Abstract: The Pauli twirl of amplitude damping (AD) is generalized amplitude damping at infinite temperature: it keeps the contraction of AD and removes its non-unital term, so comparing the two in variational circuits isolates the zero-temperature bias, which acts mainly through the scale of the features. For random parameters, features under AD settle on a floor, which at strong damping is set by the last layer, has a closed form, and at fixed $T_1$ falls with temperature as $\tanh(\hbarω/2k_BT)$; under the twirls they shrink by a constant factor per layer, up to eight qubits. A trainable output scale removes most of the resulting accuracy differences, leaving AD ahead of its twirls by at most about three percentage points in our simulations; what it removes reappears as a cost in measurement shots: trained and tested with $10^3$ shots per image, a four-qubit classifier under AD at $p=0.3$ stays within 1.5 points of noiseless accuracy, while the twirled classifiers lose up to 33. At weaker damping the separation depth grows roughly as $(np)^{-1}\ln(1/p)$. The damping direction is a gauge when the damping follows complete entangling layers and the circuit boundaries are trainable; in an eigensolver it becomes physical inside a decomposed two-qubit gate.

</details>

Result: (1) 逐点协变性保证每条轨迹在无系统-环境耦合时精确复现目标逻辑，闭帧反例证明仅端点补偿会导致一阶仪器误差；(2) 全区间独立Pauli共轭可在边界对角化误差历史，但经典标签仍保留环境诱导关联，由精确静态浴解证实；(3) 在有界中心耦合与混合条件下导出全自适应输出记录的全变差误差界，并证明固定粒子扇区中轨迹平均cPT系数在有限非高斯环境及具时间可积协方差的高斯浴（含未截断Drude谱）下收敛；数值上通过输出概率、算符条件互信息及双粒子系数在量子比特与热Drude浴中验证理论。

Abstract: Traditional discrete randomized compiling (RC) tailors errors with random control frames at logical-gate boundaries, but fails to capture intra-instrument control evolution or persistent system-environment coupling. In memory-bearing environments, errors correlate across times, not just single gates. We build continuous-process randomized compilation (CPRC) in the continuous process tensor (cPT) framework, unifying random unitary control trajectories, pointwise-covariant finite-duration instruments, and environmental dynamics on one time axis. We study four protocols: fixed Pauli refresh, bounded Cayley paths, unitary Brownian motion, Ornstein-Uhlenbeck driving. We obtain three key results: (1) Pointwise covariance ensures each trajectory exactly reproduces the target logic without system-environment coupling; a closed-frame counterexample proves endpoint-only compensation causes first-order instrument errors. (2) Independent Pauli conjugations over full intervals diagonalize error histories at boundaries, but classical labels retain environment-induced correlations, confirmed by an exact static-bath solution. (3) We derive a total-variation error bound for full adaptive output records under bounded centered coupling and mixing conditions. We prove convergence of trajectory-averaged cPT coefficients in fixed particle sectors for finite non-Gaussian environments and Gaussian baths with time-integrable covariance, including the uncut Drude spectrum. We numerically validate the theory in a qubit model via output probabilities, operator conditional mutual information, and all two-particle coefficients for both qubit and thermal Drude baths. This work extends RC from discrete circuits to continuous-time memory-bearing quantum processes, offering a unified framework, quantitative tools, implementable schemes, and benchmarks for non-Markovian error tailoring.

</details>

Method: 从源于系统-热浴相互作用的细致平衡主方程（Lindbladian）的亚稳态出发学习相互作用。提炼Gibbs态学习背后的算法机制与结构条件，并将其完整推广到亚稳态。

Result: 实现了在系统尺寸和精度上近最优的样本复杂度与计算复杂度，并完整地将Gibbs态学习的机制扩展至亚稳态。

Conclusion: 深化了亚稳态的概念，建立了有限温度学习的统一框架，为从更物理现实的亚稳态输入进行量子相互作用学习提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Learning quantum interactions from finite-temperature many-body systems is a central task in emerging quantum platforms. Recently, the problem of learning from lattice quantum Gibbs states has found rigorous, efficient protocols. Nevertheless, exact Gibbs states, as the input premise, are in fact computationally intractable to prepare and may not faithfully represent generic finite-temperature quantum systems. In contrast, a system coupled to a heat bath can be stuck at an approximate stationary state (metastable state) long before it truly equilibrates. Here, we formulate a physically and algorithmically consistent alternative: learning from such metastable states of detailed-balanced master equations (Lindbladians) arising from system-bath interactions. We distill the algorithmic mechanism and structural condition underlying Gibbs-state learning and extend it in full to metastable states, attaining nearly optimal sample and computational complexity (in the system size and the precision). More broadly, we sharpen notions of metastability and develop a unified framework for finite-temperature learning.

</details>

Method: 提出一种新颖的混合量子-经典从头算分子动力学算法，通过核-电子轨道（NEO）方法纳入核量子效应。该算法在量子计算机上使用变分量子本征求解器（VQE）评估基态能量和力，同时以经典方式更新分子几何结构。

Abstract: Nuclear quantum effects are critical for describing proton transfer and hydrogen bonding, but their incorporation into quantum chemistry calculations is often computationally prohibitive on classical hardware. A promising alternative are quantum computers due to their linear scaling in the space requirements with system size. We introduce a novel hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for ab-initio molecular dynamics that incorporates nuclear quantum effects via the nuclear-electronic orbital method. The proposed algorithm evaluates ground state energies and forces on the quantum computer using a variational quantum eigensolver, while the molecular geometries are updated classically. We validate our approach through simulations of $\text{H}_2$, $\text{H}_2\text{O}$ and the Zundel ion $\text{H}_5\text{O}_2^+$, comparing the simulated vibrational spectra with experimental data. Upon inclusion of nuclear quantum effects, the proton shuttling movement in the Zundel ion becomes effectively barrierless, and errors in the simulated frequencies improve significantly. Employing compact hardware-efficient ansatz circuits we achieve results comparable to the more accurate UCCSD ansatzes, which hints towards the feasibility of executing our algorithm on near-term quantum devices.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性是物理学基本原理，但以往检验通常需要反向时间演化或不可信假设。作者希望仅依赖前向量子操作与可信设备，构建可操作、可量化的时间反演对称性检验，并揭示其与纠缠之间的定量联系。

Result: 证明任意经典自适应协议需 Ω(min{2^{n/2}, 2^{n-e}}) 次查询，其中 e=min{e_s,e_m}；最大纠缠探测与 SWAP 测量可将查询次数降为常数；对任意固定兼容探测与测量给出测试并建立其查询复杂度与对数纠缠负度的关系，在高纠缠区通过优化探测与测量达到下界标度。

Conclusion: 结果建立了纠缠与时间反演对称性之间的定量联系，桥接了量子信息科学与基础物理两个核心概念，并表明纠缠可显著降低检验时间反演对称性的查询代价。

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry is a fundamental principle of physics describing the invariance of physical laws under reversal of the direction of time. We formulate a Bell-inequality-like test of this antiunitary symmetry using only forward access and trusted quantum operations: entanglement converts temporal input--output relations into measurable spatial exchange symmetry. For $n$-qubit unitary dynamics, we prove that reliably distinguishing the time-reversal-symmetric circular ensembles from Haar-random dynamics requires $Ω(\min\{2^{n/2},2^{n-e}\})$ queries for any classically adaptive protocol. Here, $e=\min\{e_{\mathrm s},e_{\mathrm m}\}$ with $e_{\mathrm s}$ and $e_{\mathrm m}$ representing the probe and measurement logarithmic entanglement negativities, respectively. Maximally entangled probes and SWAP measurements reduce this cost to a constant number of queries. Furthermore, we develop a time-reversal symmetry test for arbitrary fixed, compatible probes and measurements, relate its query complexity to their logarithmic negativities, and match the lower-bound scaling in the high-entanglement regime by optimizing the probe and measurement. Our results establish a quantitative connection between entanglement and time-reversal symmetry, bridging two central concepts in quantum information science and fundamental physics.

</details>

Abstract: This paper investigates the performance scalability and adversarial robustness of Quantum Machine Learning (QML) models deployed on noise-model-based fake simulators. We conduct a dual-phased study, first benchmarking the computational throughput of Qiskit's Aer simulation engine across varying hardware architectures, and second, evaluating the effectiveness of Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) attacks and adversarial retraining strategies under realistic noise conditions. Our results quantify the runtime tradeoffs and scaling behavior for medium-scale simulations (projected up to 8 qubits) and demonstrate that high adversarial-to-benign retraining ratios (50/50) are essential for achieving practical model robustness for 4-qubit classifiers under realistic noise conditions.

</details>

Abstract: Actual quantum key distribution network nodes may be vulnerable to side-channel attacks such as electromagnetic leakage. In this study, we measured electromagnetic emissions from a prototype quantum key distribution device implementing a time-bin decoy-state BB84 protocol with Y and Z bases. We measured the electromagnetic spectra and waveforms at distances of 0 m and 3 m from the transmitter and receiver inside an anechoic chamber. The results showed that the transmitter was the primary source of electromagnetic leakage, and distinct differences were observed between the quantum states generated at the third harmonic of the 1241.6 MHz clock.

TL;DR: Lindblad耗散（ℓ光子损耗，ℓ>2）可恢复Bose-Hubbard系统的算符范数Lieb-Robinson界，并给出几乎弹道式的界，从而实现与初态一致（uniform in the input state）的局域信道近似、热力学极限与高效数字量子模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭玻色格点系统一般不存在与初态无关、对算符范数一致成立的Lieb-Robinson界，其信息传播速度可随局域玻色占据数发散，导致局域近似、热力学极限与数字模拟等应用无法对任意输入态统一成立（这与量子自旋系统形成对比）。本文旨在探究局域耗散是否能重建这样的界，使玻色系统获得类似自旋系统的可控性。

Method: 1) 研究对象为耗散Bose-Hubbard模型，由含在位ℓ光子损耗（ℓ>2）的Lindbladian描述；2) 分析耗散对高占据格点的快速排空作用，将状态在局域粒子矩的Sobolev型尺度上正则化；3) 建立局部粒子矩随时间演化的界——该界在时间趋于零时发散，但在足够低阶矩上仍可积；4) 借助这些矩界推导算符范数下的(几乎弹道式)Lieb-Robinson界；5) 将方法推广至cat-code耗散，并对码空间初态给出算符范数意义下、对总体积一致成立的局域绝热近似。

Result: - 证明局域耗散可恢复算符范数Lieb-Robinson界，并为主结果建立了几乎弹道的Lieb-Robinson界；- 得到在足够低阶局域粒子矩上时间near-zero不可积性可控（可积）的矩界；- 对cat-code耗散，证明码空间初态下局域绝热近似对总体积一致成立；- 由上述结果导出对输入态一致成立的局域信道近似、热力学极限以及高效数字量子模拟。

Conclusion: 局域耗散（ℓ>2光子损耗）将原本因高占据而导致信息速度发散的封闭玻色系统正则化，使其在输入态一致意义下具备与量子自旋系统相同的可控性；这些应用（局域信道近似、热力学极限、高效数字量子模拟）因此对任意输入态均统一成立，从而区别于封闭Bose-Hubbard系统。

Abstract: Closed bosonic lattice systems do not generally admit operator norm Lieb--Robinson bounds uniformly in the initial state. The reason is that the information propagation velocity can grow with the local boson occupancy, which can be macroscopically large. Here, we show that local dissipation restores an operator-norm Lieb--Robinson bound. We consider the dissipative Bose--Hubbard model described by a Lindbladian operator with on-site $\ell$-photon loss, $\ell>2$ and, for our main result, we establish an almost-ballistic Lieb-Robinson bound. Dissipation rapidly depletes highly occupied sites, thus regularizing the state on the Sobolev-type scale of local particle moments. The resulting moment bounds diverge as time approaches zero but remain integrable near zero at sufficiently low orders. We extend these ideas to treat cat-code dissipation and, for initial states in the code space, we prove a local adiabatic approximation uniform in the total volume. Further consequences include local channel approximation, a thermodynamic limit, and efficient digital quantum simulation. The point is that these applications are now available \textit{uniformly in the input state}, as for quantum spin systems, but in contrast to closed Bose--Hubbard systems.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出基于GNN的量子纠错解码器PolyMechanon，仅以探测错误模型（DEM）为输入，将DEM表示为探测器—错误机制—逻辑可观测量的三分图，实现跨稳定子码与噪声模型的通用解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错解码器（如MWPM、BP+OSD）通常针对特定码型与噪声模型设计，泛化性差、需重新设计；且部分解码器解码时间随物理错误率增长，难以满足实时解码需求。

Method: 以量子码本身作为数据计算输入特征，而非依赖设计选择；将DEM构建为探测器、错误机制与逻辑可观测量构成的三分图，用GNN解码；仅需在DEM上训练，DEM变化即可解码新码与新噪声模型。

Result: 旋转表面码上优于相关MWPM，逻辑失败减少最多25%；高码率qLDPC码上小码持平BP+OSD、大码超越（[[130,4,6]]减少16%失败）；解码时间不随物理错误率变化，[[130,4,6]]上单GPU每shot约几毫秒，BP+OSD单CPU核为几十毫秒；跨码族单一模型在图类码上优于非相关MWPM，其他码上差距在10%以内，但对未见码（尤其更大码）泛化较差；概率输出支持置信度后选择，表面码上逻辑错误率降低一个数量级以上且保留超90% shots。

Conclusion: DEM作为统一接口使单一GNN架构原则上可解码任意可表达为DEM的稳定子码与噪声模型，无需重新设计解码器；固定计算图具备实时解码潜力，概率输出支持后选择，但跨未见码泛化仍是局限。

Abstract: We present POLYMECHANON, a graph neural network (GNN) decoder for quantum error correction whose only input is the detection error model (DEM) of a quantum code under a given noise model. We represent the DEM as a tripartite graph of detectors, error mechanisms and logical observables, where every input feature is computed by using the quantum code as data rather than design choice. In this way, the same architecture decodes in principle any stabiliser code, under any noise model that can be expressed as a detection error model, once trained on the DEM. We test this approach along four directions. Firstly, on the rotated surface code the decoder outperforms correlated MWPM under both phenomenological and circuit-level noise, with up to $25\%$ fewer logical failures. Secondly, on a family of high-rate qLDPC codes it matches BP+OSD on the smaller codes and surpasses it on the larger ones, with up to $16\%$ fewer logical failures on $[\![130,4,6]\!]$. Thirdly, its decoding time does not depend on the physical error rate, whereas that of BP+OSD grows with it: on $[\![130,4,6]\!]$ its effective cost per shot is of the order of a few ms on a single GPU against a few tens of milliseconds for BP+OSD on a single CPU core, and its fixed computational graph makes it a candidate for real-time decoding on neutral-atom processors. Finally, a single model trained across codes of different families outperforms uncorrelated MWPM on the graph-like codes seen during training and stays within $10\%$ of BP+OSD on the others, while it tends to fail to generalize to unseen codes, particularly larger ones. Its probabilistic output enables confidence-based post-selection, lowering the logical error rate by more than an order of magnitude on the surface code while keeping more than $90\%$ of the shots. Since only the DEM changes, new codes and noise models can be decoded without redesigning the decoder.

</details>


### [79] [Mentor-Initiated Asymmetric Bidirectional Quantum Teleportation Protocol for Arbitrary Qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01684)
*Sugata Adhya,Benchao Yang*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种导师发起（mentor-initiated）的双向非对称量子隐形传态协议，突破了现有方案中Bob到Alice只能传输Bell型两量子比特态的限制，允许传输任意两量子比特态，并给出了基于标准量子门的纠缠资源构造及Qiskit量子线路实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双向非对称量子隐形传态方案通常要求Bob传给Alice的两量子比特态被限制为Bell型形式 α|00⟩+β|11⟩，泛化能力受限。本文旨在解除这一限制，使协议在导师发起框架下支持任意两量子比特态的传输。

Abstract: Electric-field noise in microfabricated surface ion traps contributes to gate errors in trapped-ion quantum computers, but this noise has been challenging to predict. TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于批量梯度下降（BGD）的贝尔不等式量子值优化器，可在GPU上高效处理超过千级输入/输出的贝尔不等式，相较see-saw方法显著加速，并给出相应的经典值高效计算方法。

Method: 提出基于批量梯度下降的贝尔不等式量子值优化器，在可行量子策略空间上进行可微搜索：状态与投影测量由无约束变量生成；贝尔表达式通过直接张量收缩求值，避免构造规模过大的贝尔算子。该形式使并行随机重启代价低廉，天然适配GPU。同时通过GPU枚举策略或将优化表述为混合整数线性规划来高效计算经典值。

Result: 在多种贝尔不等式族上与see-saw方法对比，取得显著加速；GPU实现的BGD优化器可在数分钟内优化实验中超过一千个输入或输出的贝尔不等式；对这类大规模不等式也能高效计算经典值。

Conclusion: 该优化器可作为贝尔实验及由多输入/输出贝尔不等式建模的多智能体协调问题（如高频交易、分布式系统）的数值评估工具。

Abstract: Exploring the world of Bell inequalities requires numerical methods that scale effectively with the number of parties, inputs, and outputs. Such methods can be used to study large-scale Bell inequalities beyond the reach of analytic methods, to design experiments for violating more complicated Bell inequalities, and to enable applications of Bell inequality violation. Such applications include randomness generation and multi-agent coordination with restricted communication. However, current general purpose optimizers such as the see-saw method struggle to handle Bell inequalities with only a dozen inputs and outputs. We introduce an optimizer for the quantum value of a Bell inequality based on batched gradient descent (BGD). More precisely, our BGD optimizer is a differentiable search over feasible quantum strategies in which states and projective measurements are generated from unconstrained variables. The Bell expression is evaluated by a direct tensor contraction without forming the prohibitively large Bell operator. This formulation makes parallel random restarts inexpensive and is thus naturally implemented by a GPU. We evaluate our optimizer against the see-saw method and find a significant speedup for a wide variety of Bell inequality families. Implemented on a GPU, our BGD optimizer can optimize Bell inequalities in our numerical experiments with more than a thousand inputs or outputs within minutes. For these large-scale Bell inequalities, we also efficiently compute the classical value by enumerating possible strategies using a GPU or expressing the optimization as a mixed-integer linear program. Our optimizers can serve as numerical evaluation tools for Bell experiments and multi-agent coordination problems modeled by Bell inequalities with many inputs and outputs, a common feature of real-world scenarios such as high frequency trading and distributed systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 首次探测统计呈现两个由测量探测时间与幺正电路动力学弛豫时间尺度竞争所设定的regime：低频监测给出几何型首次探测统计，而高频监测引入的相关性导致偏离几何行为；返回概率由被监测子区域的大小控制。

Conclusion: 该工作对局域测量backaction如何重塑相互作用量子多体系统的探测统计给出了罕见的精确刻画，且其局域协议可在数字量子模拟器上通过局域mid-circuit测量直接验证。

Abstract: We study the interplay between local stroboscopic measurements and the unitary quantum circuit dynamics of the deterministic Floquet-quantum East model. We derive exact analytical results for the probability of first detecting a subset of qubits back in their initial state. This is achieved via a renewal equation linking the first-detection probability to the subsystem Loschmidt echo, which encodes the effective open dynamics of the monitored qubits. The resulting statistics exhibit two regimes set by the competition between the measurement probing time and the relaxation timescale of the unitary circuit dynamics: while infrequent monitoring leads to geometric first-detection statistics, frequent monitoring induces correlations that manifest as deviations from this geometric behavior. These results provide a rare exact characterization of how local measurement backaction reshapes detection statistics in an interacting quantum many-body system. In our local protocol, return probabilities are controlled by the size of the monitored subregion and can therefore be accessed on digital quantum simulators via local mid-circuit measurements.

</details>


### [83] [Less precise but less noisy: local circuits for momentum-space state preparation and measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01704)
*Etienne Granet,Henrik Dreyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Quantinuum H2量子计算机上，紧束缚链基态制备存在临界系统尺寸N，超过该尺寸后绝热演化在同门数和同线路深度下达到比FFT显著更低的能量。作者将FFT的高噪声敏感性归因于其高动量分辨率（可分辨1/N的动量），这需要实空间长程耦合，从而更快传播误差。地方性物理线路（如绝热演化）动量分辨率较粗但误差传播更慢。作者还提出一种动量测量方案，精度较低但成本更低、噪声更小，在谱函数测量上性能优于FFT。工作强调在门数和线路深度之外降低量子算法噪声敏感性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法通常以门数或线路深度为优化目标，但作者观察到在实际量子硬件上，即使门数和深度相同，不同算法的噪声敏感性差异显著，因此需要将噪声敏感性作为独立于门数和深度的关键优化维度。

Method: 在Quantinuum System Model H2量子计算机上进行紧束缚链基态制备实验，对比绝热演化与Fermionic Fourier Transform（FFT）在相同门数和相同线路深度下的表现；分析FFT高精度（动量分辨率为1/N）导致实空间长程耦合、进而加快误差传播的机制；并提出一种成本更低、噪声更小的动量测量方案用于谱函数测量。

Result: 发现存在临界系统尺寸N，超过该尺寸后绝热演化在同门数和同深度下达到显著低于FFT的能量；FFT因高动量分辨率需要长程耦合而噪声敏感性更高；提出的动量测量方案虽精度低于FFT，但成本更低、噪声更小，在Quantinuum H2上谱函数测量性能优于FFT。

Conclusion: 对于物理应用，高动量分辨率很少被需要，通常值得以低噪声敏感性来换取。工作强调在门数和线路深度之外，降低量子算法的噪声敏感性同样重要，物理局域线路因误差传播更慢而具有优势。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are usually optimized for gate count or circuit depth. We find on Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer that for a tight-binding chain ground state preparation, there is a system size $N$ beyond which the adiabatic evolution reaches significantly lower energies than the Fermionic Fourier Transform (FFT), with the same number of gates, and with the same circuit depth. We attribute this high noise sensitivity of the FFT to its high precision, being able to distinguish momenta by $1/N$. This high resolution in momentum space requires long-range couplings in real space, which propagates errors faster. In contrast, although local and physical circuits such as the adiabatic evolution have a coarser momentum resolution, they also propagate errors more slowly. For physical applications, high momentum resolution is rarely required and is often worth trading for low noise sensitivity. We also introduce a momentum measurement scheme that although less precise than FFT, is less costly and less noisy. We show that it achieves better performance than FFT for spectral function measurement on Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer. Our work emphasizes the importance of reducing the noise sensitivity of quantum algorithms, beyond the number of gates or circuit depth.

</details>


### [84] [Near-optimal quantum query lower bounds on bipartiteness and expansion testing in the bounded-degree graph model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01752)
*Chandrima Kayal,Sayantan Sen,Dániel Szabó*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了有界度数模型下二部性测试与扩展性测试的量子查询复杂度近似紧下界 Θ̃(N^{1/3})，完全刻画了这两个问题的量子查询复杂度（至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二部性与扩展性测试是图性质测试的经典问题，经典复杂度为 Θ̃(√N)。Ambainis、Childs 和 Liu (2011) 开启量子设定研究，给出 Õ(N^{1/3}) 上界及扩展性测试 Ω̃(N^{1/4}) 下界，但二部性测试是否存在指数级量子优势悬而未决，且此后十五年无实质进展。

Method: 沿用多项式方法，但引入经过归约与主问题相关联的中间问题，并对所产生多项式进行更精细的分析。

Result: 证明了二部性测试和扩展性测试均具有近似紧的 Ω̃(N^{1/3}) 量子查询下界，与已知上界匹配。

Conclusion: 完全刻画了这两个问题的量子查询复杂度（至多对数因子），否定了二部性测试存在指数级量子优势的可能。

Abstract: In this work, we study bipartiteness and expansion testing, two canonical problems in graph property testing in the bounded-degree model through the lens of quantum query complexity. In the classical setting, it is known that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{N})$ queries are necessary and sufficient for both these testing problems (Goldreich and Ron, 1999, 2000 & 2002), where $N$ denotes the number of vertices of the input graph. Due to their significance, (Ambainis, Childs, and Liu, 2011) initiated the study of these problems in the quantum setting and designed quantum algorithms for bipartiteness and expansion testing that perform $\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$ queries, showing a polynomial speedup. They also proved that $\widetildeΩ(N^{1/4})$ queries are necessary for expansion testing, but the possibility of an exponential quantum advantage for bipartiteness testing remained open. Despite significant effort, there has been no improvement in these results in the last decade and a half. In this work, we prove essentially tight $\widetildeΩ(N^{1/3})$ quantum query lower bounds for both bipartiteness and expansion testing, thereby completely characterizing the quantum query complexity of these problems up to polylogarithmic factors. While our proofs use the polynomial method similarly to Ambainis, Childs, and Liu, we use intermediate problems that we relate to the main problems via reductions, and perform a more precise analysis of the resulting polynomials, leading to the near-optimal lower bounds.

</details>


### [85] [Efficient quantum phase estimation with adaptive entanglement-assisted Hadamard test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01772)
*Hengzhun Chen,Benchi Zhao,Yingzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应纠缠辅助Hadamard测试（AEHT）算法，通过迭代精化参考相位实现逐步增强的相位放大，并抑制不完美本征态制备带来的系统偏差，在设备重启计数度量下优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠辅助Hadamard测试（EHT）通过放大相位信号来估计量子相位，但其放大能力根本上受限于参考相位的精度，导致在高精度区间效率低下。此外，在不完美本征态制备场景下，传统EHT估计量子相位会产生不可避免的系统偏差。因此需要一种能突破参考相位精度限制并抑制偏差的高效方法。

Method: 提出自适应纠缠辅助Hadamard测试（AEHT）算法，迭代地精化参考相位，使放大强度随迭代逐步增强；针对不完美本征态制备场景，利用AEHT抑制传统EHT中不可避免的系统偏差；在物理实现层面，采用设备重启计数（device-restart count）而非采样次数（shot count）来度量量子相位估计的成本。

Result: 数值实验证实，在该设备重启计数度量下，所提出的AEHT相较于传统方法更为有效，能够充分释放大纠缠态的放大能力。

Conclusion: AEHT为在近期量子处理器上高效估计高精度量子相位提供了一种可行方法。

Abstract: The entanglement-assisted Hadamard test (EHT) is a practical method for estimating a quantum phase by amplifying the phase signal. However, the feasible amplification is fundamentally limited by the accuracy of the reference phase, such that the method is inefficient in the high-precision regime. In this work, we propose an algorithm, called adaptive entanglement-assisted Hadamard test (AEHT), that iteratively refines the reference phase, enabling progressively stronger amplification as the iteration goes by. We further consider the imperfect eigenstate preparation scenario, where a systematic bias is unavoidable when estimating the quantum phase with the conventional EHT. Such a bias can be suppressed by the proposed AEHT. Moreover, taking physical implementation into consideration, we adopt device-restart count to measure the cost of quantum phase estimation, rather than shot count. The numerical experiments confirm the effectiveness of the proposed AEHT compared with conventional methods under this measure. By unlocking the full amplification power of large entangled states, this work offers an efficient method to estimate high-precision quantum phase on near-term quantum processors.

</details>


### [86] [QUFIG: GNN-Based Prediction of Quantum Fault Injection Vulnerabilities with Gate-Level Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01777)
*Shihan Zhao,Qiying Li,Ben Dong,Qian Wang,Yuntao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QUFIG，将量子电路门级故障分析建模为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题，用基于电路DAG的GNN预测每个门-故障对的脆弱性分数并排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件规模扩大与可访问性提升带来新的可靠性与安全挑战，例如云量子计算平台的运行时故障注入攻击。现有方法无法以门级精度识别脆弱点，也难以适应运行时环境。

Method: 将门级故障分析形式化为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题；采用基于电路DAG的GNN骨干网络，预测每个门对每类注入故障的脆弱性分数（定义为该门-故障对对电路保真度的影响），再按分数对门-故障对排序。

Result: 在QASMbench与HamLib MaxCut上的实验表明，QUFIG以少于随机和基于深度启发式的检查次数恢复高影响脆弱门-故障对；在保持有效故障识别的同时，需检查的门数减少2.9%–19.8%。

Conclusion: QUFIG可在有限保真度预算下高效定位门级脆弱点，帮助量子电路设计者更高效地识别漏洞并施加针对性防御。

Abstract: The growing scale and accessibility of quantum hardware exposed new reliability and security challenges in the quantum computing workflow, such as the run-time fault injection attacks in cloud-based quantum computing platforms. However, existing works fail to identify vulnerabilities with gate-level precision or adapt to run-time environments. In this work, we formulate gate-level fault analysis as a learning-guided prioritization problem under restricted fidelity budgets. The framework uses a circuit-DAG-based GNN backbone to predict the vulnerability score of each gate to each type of injected fault, defined as the impact of the gate-fault pair on circuit fidelity. The gate-fault pairs are then ranked by their vulnerability score. Experiments on QASMbench and HamLib MaxCut show that QUFIG recovers high-impact vulnerable gate-fault pairs with fewer inspections than random and depth-based heuristics. Our results show that QUFIG can reduce the number of gates requiring inspection by 2.9--19.8% while maintaining effective fault identification, allowing quantum circuit designers to identify vulnerabilities and apply targeted defenses more efficiently.

</details>


### [87] [Non-Markovian effects on informational steady states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01782)
*Jacob Werner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在连续监测的碰撞模型框架下研究了非马尔可夫效应对信息稳态(ISS)的影响，发现稳态单次测量信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息稳态(ISS)产生于连续测量获得信息的速率与先前测量信息向环境流失的速率相平衡之时。尽管ISS已在马尔可夫情形下得到研究，但记忆效应对其形成及稳态性质的影响仍知之甚少。

Method: 在连续监测的碰撞模型框架下建模环境，将环境表示为一系列双量子比特辅助粒子(ancilla)，分析系统-辅助粒子相互作用参数与辅助粒子-辅助粒子相互作用参数的影响。

Result: 对该特定模型而言，稳态下每次测量的信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关；数值结果进一步刻画了系统-辅助粒子及辅助粒子-辅助粒子相互作用参数如何同时影响非马尔可夫度与稳态信息增益。

Conclusion: 揭示了连续监测碰撞模型中记忆效应对信息稳态的作用机制，为非马尔可夫条件下ISS的形成与性质提供了新认识。

Abstract: Informational steady states (ISSs) arise when the rate at which information is gained from continuous measurements is exactly balanced by the rate at which information from earlier measurements is lost to the environment. While ISSs have been studied in Markovian settings, relatively little is known about how memory effects influence their formation and steady-state properties. In this work, we investigate non-Markovian effects on ISSs within the framework of continuously monitored collision models. We model the environment as a sequence of two-qubit ancillas and find that, for this specific model, the steady-state information gain per measurement is negatively correlated with the degree of non-Markovianity. Numerical results further characterize how the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction parameters influence both the degree of non-Markovianity and the steady-state information gain.

</details>


### [88] [Learnt Attacks on Quantum Key Distribution under Channel Noise and Device Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01792)
*Marcel Mordarski,Benjamin Gras,Abdelrahman Shehata,Daniel Budina,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子密钥分发中自适应窃听的价值量化问题，将其建模为受约束马尔可夫决策过程并给出上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD链路基于静止信道的安全性分析配置，但实际器件在重校准间会产生信道漂移；对于无法改变信道自身噪声、只能跟随漂移的自适应窃听者，其收益尚未被量化。

Method: 将自适应窃听建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程：每轮攻击者选择一个电路，噪声水平服从Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，终止条件为每个轮次块的预算约束；联合搜索门结构与旋转角，得到紧凑的离散动作集，并扩展至未知模板的噪声模型（含振幅阻尼信道）；利用强化学习学习攻击策略。

Result: 在双边去极化噪声下的设备无关E91中，强化学习攻击者将Holevo信息从最优固定电路的0.135提升至零检测下0.348，达上界的98%；在漂移比特翻转信道的BB84中，其保真度超过保守的噪声索引规则0.024，达上界的99%；静止噪声下，基不对称带来的收益在平均与逐基错误率约束间改变符号。

Conclusion: 自适应窃听的收益由最优固定电路与动态规划上界界定；从随机门序列出发的搜索可恢复解析克隆器与集体攻击密钥率，并从上方满足Winick-Lütkenhaus-Coles目标下界。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) links are provisioned from security analyses of stationary channels, whereas the devices that determine the channel drift between recalibrations. Whether an eavesdropper who cannot alter the channel's own noise gains by following that drift has not been quantified. Adaptive eavesdropping is posed here as a constrained Markov decision process in which the attacker selects one circuit per round while the noise level follows an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process and the abort condition is a budget over each block of rounds. The value of adaptation is bounded by the best fixed circuit and a dynamic-programming upper bound. The actions are learnt attacks. Whereas Decker et al. trained a parametrised circuit on a fixed gate template against a fixed channel, here the gate structure and rotation angles are searched jointly. This yields circuits compact enough to form a discrete action set, extending the construction to noise models lacking a known template, including the amplitude damping channel. On device-independent E91 under bilateral depolarising noise, a reinforcement-learning attacker raises her Holevo information from $0.135$ for the best fixed circuit to $0.348$ at zero detection, $98\%$ of the upper bound. On BB84 under a drifting bit-flip channel, she exceeds a conservative noise-indexed rule by $0.024$ in fidelity, reaching $99\%$ of the upper bound. Under stationary noise, the attacker's gain from basis asymmetry changes sign between an averaged and a per-basis error-rate constraint. The search, started from random gate sequences, recovers the analytical cloners and the collective-attack key rate, and meets the lower bound of the Winick--Lütkenhaus--Coles objective from above.

</details>


### [89] [Collision models: Markovian and Non-Markovian impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01814)
*Jacob Werner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类“马尔可夫杂质模型”（Markovian impurity models）的碰撞模型，用于研究信息回流被周期性阻断的开放量子系统，并基于BLP度量分析了其非马尔可夫性的临界转变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞模型是研究开放量子系统与非马尔可夫性的灵活框架。作者受信息回流被周期性阻断的物理情景启发，希望构造一类新的碰撞模型来刻画这种机制。

Method: 提出马尔可夫杂质模型（互补地引入非马尔可夫杂质模型）；采用Breuer-Laine-Piilo（BLP）非马尔可夫性度量，证明p-杂质模型非马尔可夫则(p+1)-杂质模型亦非马尔可夫，从而存在马尔可夫到非马尔可夫的临界p；并通过数值模拟考察临界参数对系统-辅助子及辅助子-辅助子相互作用强度的依赖。

Result: 证明了非马尔可夫性随杂质数的单调蕴含关系，并由此确认临界p的存在；数值模拟表明，虽有例外，但增大任一种相互作用强度通常会降低临界p；当辅助子-辅助子相互作用足够弱时，即使无杂质系统也保持马尔可夫动力学。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫杂质模型为刻画信息回流被周期性阻断的开放量子系统提供了新工具，其非马尔可夫性存在由杂质数控制的临界转变，且该临界点受相互作用强度调控；作者还简要提出了互补的非马尔可夫杂质模型。

Abstract: Collision models provide a flexible framework for studying open quantum systems and introducing non-Markovianity. We present a novel class of collision models, which we term Markovian impurity models, motivated by physical scenarios in which information backflow is periodically blocked. Using the Breuer-Laine-Piilo (BLP) measure of non-Markovianity, we show that if a $p$-impurity model is non-Markovian, then the corresponding $(p+1)$-impurity model is also non-Markovian, implying the existence of a critical $p$ at which the process transitions from Markovian to non-Markovian. Numerical simulations are used to investigate how this critical parameter depends on the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction strengths. We find that, altough exceptions exist, increasing either interaction strength generally lowers the critical $p$, while sufficiently weak ancilla-ancilla interactions lead to a regime in which the dynamics remain Markovian even in the absence of impurities. Finally, we briefly introduce the complementary non-Markovian impurity model.

</details>


### [90] [QPI-DeepONet-MAC: A Scalable and Stable Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01824)
*Said Lantigua,José Valencia,Gilson Giraldi,Renato Portugal,Jonas Maziero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出混合量子-经典架构QPI-DeepONet-MAC，通过乘加耦合将参数化量子电路融入物理信息DeepONet，理论上证明其通用算子逼近能力，并给出梯度界、可训练性判据、传感器下界及量子相干正则化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用算子学习求解参数化偏微分方程是AI与物理建模交叉的核心难题。PI-DeepONet虽能引入控制方程，但在高维下优化困难；量子扩展虽增强表达能力，却受贫瘠高原限制而难以训练。目标是构建兼具表达能力、可训练性与稳定性的量子增强物理信息算子学习框架。

Method: 提出QPI-DeepONet-MAC：将参数化量子电路以乘法-加法耦合（MAC）方式整合进PI-DeepONets，融合经典表示与量子期望值。分析上证明通用算子逼近能力；推导量子参数梯度界，给出避免贫瘠高原的显式条件与可训练性缩放判据；推导达到目标精度所需传感器数量下界；并设计基于量子相干性与态保真度动态的信息正则化方案。

Result: 理论上证明该架构保持通用算子逼近能力；建立量子参数梯度上界，明确避免贫瘠高原的条件与可训练性缩放准则；给出传感器数量下界，将采样需求与输入函数正则性、域维数及PDE动力学增长直接关联；信息正则化可防止训练中相干性损失并保持量子结构。

Conclusion: 上述结果共同为量子增强物理信息算子学习奠定理论基础，使QPI-DeepONet-MAC成为求解参数化PDE的可扩展、稳定框架。

Abstract: General operator learning for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) is a fundamental challenge at the intersection of artificial intelligence and physics-based modeling. Physics-informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) incorporate governing equations into learning, but face optimization difficulties in high dimensions. Quantum extensions offer additional representational capacity, yet their trainability can be hindered by barren plateaus. Here, we introduce QPI-DeepONet-MAC, a hybrid quantum-classical architecture that integrates parameterized quantum circuits into PI-DeepONets through a multiplicative-and-additive coupling (MAC) of classical representations and quantum expectation values. We prove analytically that the proposed architecture retains universal operator approximation capabilities. Furthermore, we derive bounds on quantum parameter gradients, establishing explicit conditions to avoid barren plateaus alongside a scaling criterion for trainability. Additionally, we derive a lower bound on the number of sensors required for a target accuracy, directly linking sampling requirements to input function regularity, domain dimension, and PDE dynamical growth. Finally, we propose an informational regularization scheme based on quantum coherence and state fidelity dynamics to prevent coherence loss and preserve quantum structure during training. Together, these results establish a theoretical foundation for quantum-enhanced physics-informed operator learning and position QPI-DeepONet-MAC as a scalable, stable framework for solving parametric PDEs.

</details>


### [91] [Experimental realization of Wheeler's delayed-choice experiment with dual selections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01832)
*Xiaowan Yang,Xinglei Yu,Liangsheng Li,Xinzhi Zhao,Tianle Zheng,Chengjie Zhang,Chuan-Feng Li,Guang-Can Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于纠缠光子实现双选择延迟选择实验，通过路径编码同时控制马赫-曾德尔干涉仪中两个分束器的存在与缺失，揭示单光子的波粒行为，并讨论两分束器处于量子叠加时光子呈现波粒量子叠加态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wheeler延迟选择实验用于展示波粒二象性，但以往量子版本实验中通常仅控制第二个分束器的存在或缺失。作者希望进一步扩展该框架，通过对干涉仪中两个分束器同时进行控制（双选择），更深入地检验波粒行为及其量子叠加特性。

Method: 采用基于纠缠光子的实验方案，在马赫-曾德尔干涉仪中利用路径编码同时控制两个分束器的存在与缺失，测量不同分束器配置下单光子的波粒行为；并进一步分析两个分束器处于存在与缺失的量子叠加时的情况。

Result: 实验揭示了单光子在不同双分束器配置下的波粒行为，并在两分束器处于量子叠加时，观察到光子处于波粒量子叠加态。

Conclusion: 该工作将延迟选择实验从单一分束器控制推广到双分束器双选择控制，验证并拓展了波粒二象性在量子叠加配置下的表现。

Abstract: Wheeler's delayed-choice experiment demonstrates wave-particle duality of particles using different experimental configurations of a Mach-Zehnder interferometer. In a quantum version of this experiment, the wave-particle behavior of photons can be observed by controlling presence or absence of the second beam splitter. Here, we implement a delayed-choice experiment with dual selections based on entangled photons, experimentally controlling both of two beam splitters in the Mach-Zehnder interferometer, where the presence and absence of the second beam splitter are simultaneously controlled through path encoding. Our experiment reveals the wave-particle behavior of single photons under different configurations of two beam splitters. We further discuss the scenario when two beam splitters are in a quantum superposition of being present and absent, photons will be in a wave-particle quantum superposition state.

</details>


### [92] [Toward Optimal Circuit Depth for Geometrically Local Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01839)
*Zhenyu Shen,Yusen Wu,Penghui Yao,Xiao Yuan,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究格点量子多体哈密顿量的数字量子模拟电路复杂度，构造了深度为 O(T log(nT/ε))、门数为 O(nT log(nT/ε)) 的最近邻量子电路，将原有 polylog 开销降为单对数；并证明均匀 XXZ 模型需深度 Ω(log n / log log n)，达到近似最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格哈密顿量模拟的常规方法（如 Lieb-Robinson + Trotter/乘积公式）会产生较高的多项式对数（polylog）深度开销。作者认为空间局域性应使数字模拟保留底层动力学的并行性，因此希望在保证精度 ε 的前提下降低电路深度，并刻画其下界以判断是否最优。

Method: 基于 Lieb-Robinson 分解，将时间演化划分为对数尺度的空间分块；在每个分块内使用常数阶乘积公式，并引入一个校正项（correction）来抵消其误差，从而实现指数精度。校正项的实现深度与分块直径成正比，不引入超出空间局域性所需数量级的额外渐近深度。下界方面，针对均匀 XXZ 哈密顿量构造一个无条件电路深度下界论证。

Result: 对 n 量子比特、有限程、固定维格点上的含时演化 T≥1 情形，构造深度 O(T log(nT/ε))、门数 O(nT log(nT/ε)) 的最近邻电路，将 Haah-Hastings-Kothari-Low 的 polylog 开销降为单对数。同时证明均匀 XXZ 情形在固定演化时间及足够小的固定误差下，任意最近邻电路深度需 Ω(log n / log log n)，与上界 O(log n) 仅差二重对数因子。

Conclusion: 空间局域性同时控制高精度模拟的代价，使格点哈密顿量数字模拟达到近似最优的电路深度，澄清了局域性与精度在决定量子动力学电路复杂度中的作用，并为空间受限的量子计算提供更普遍的原则。

Abstract: Understanding the dynamics of interacting quantum systems is a central goal of quantum simulation. For geometrically local Hamiltonians, locality suggests that digital simulation should retain much of the parallelism of the underlying dynamics. For a target accuracy $ε$, we construct a nearest-neighbor quantum circuit that simulates the dynamics of an $n$-qubit, time-independent, finite-range Hamiltonian with normalized local interaction strength on a fixed-dimensional lattice for time $T\ge1$, with circuit depth $O(T\log(nT/ε))$ and gate count $O(nT\log(nT/ε))$. This reduces the polylogarithmic overhead of Haah, Hastings, Kothari, and Low to a single logarithm. On logarithmic-scale blocks from the Lieb--Robinson decomposition, a shallow but highly accurate implementation is essential for reducing the circuit depth. Our key observation is that spatial localization also controls the cost of high precision. In each block, the error of a constant-order product formula is compensated for by a correction, yielding exponential accuracy. The correction can be implemented with depth proportional to the block diameter, introducing no additional asymptotic depth scale beyond that required by spatial localization.
  Furthermore, we prove an unconditional circuit-depth lower bound for uniform XXZ Hamiltonians: at fixed evolution time $T$ in the stated regime and sufficiently small fixed error $ε$, any nearest-neighbor quantum circuit simulating these dynamics requires depth $Ω(\log n/\log\log n)$. This matches our $O(\log n)$ upper bound up to a doubly logarithmic factor, establishing near-optimal circuit depth for lattice Hamiltonian simulation in this regime. Together, these results clarify the role of locality and precision in determining the circuit complexity of quantum dynamics and suggest broader principles for spatially constrained quantum computation.

</details>


### [93] [Trapdoored Clifford Operators and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01848)
*Minki Hhan,Hojune Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"带陷门的Clifford算子分布",其样本与均匀随机Clifford计算不可区分,但在持有陷门时可近线性时间采样与实现;并应用于加速随机Clifford协议及矩阵/Clifford问题的最坏到平均归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机n量子比特Clifford算子因Clifford群规模庞大,采样与实现的复杂度近乎二次,阻碍了随机Clifford在randomized benchmarking、classical shadows、量子认证等中的应用,故需一种在密码学意义上可"绕过"该障碍的构造。

Method: 在learning parity with noise(LPN)假设的一个变体下,构造带陷门的Clifford算子分布:样本与均匀随机Clifford计算不可区分,但已知陷门时可近线性时间采样与实现;支持对Pauli标签的快速tableau作用以便经典模拟,并可优化为polylog深度实现。途中还构造了有限域上的带陷门矩阵,支持矩阵及其逆的高效乘法。基于这些构造给出更快的随机Clifford协议。

Result: 得到近线性时间采样/实现、polylog深度的带陷门Clifford分布;构造出支持高效正逆乘法的有限域带陷门矩阵,解决了Vaikuntanathan-Zamir[SODA'26]遗留的公开问题;给出更快的随机Clifford协议;并在矩阵与Clifford问题(迭代矩阵乘法、Clifford电路综合)的最坏到平均归约上展示应用。

Conclusion: 证明了批处理Clifford电路的困难性:对多个寄存器施加同一Clifford的综合问题至少与最坏情况矩阵乘法一样难,即使综合仅对一小常数比例的随机Clifford成功;该结论可推广到由通用量子电路进行的近似实现。

Abstract: Random Clifford operators have numerous applications in quantum computing, including randomized benchmarking, classical shadows, and quantum authentication. However, sampling and implementing uniformly random $n$-qubit Clifford incur near-quadratic complexity due to the size of Clifford group.
  We introduce a cryptographic way to overcome these barriers: trapdoored Clifford operator distributions whose samples are computationally indistinguishable from uniformly random Cliffords, yet implementing them can be much faster given the trapdoor. We construct a distribution of trapdoored Clifford operators whose elements can be sampled and implemented in near-linear time under a variant of the learning parity with noise assumption. Our constructions allow fast tableau action on Pauli labels for classical simulation, and also can be optimized to admit polylogarithmic-depth implementation. Along the way, we construct trapdoored matrices over finite fields that support efficient multiplication by both a matrix and its inverse, resolving an open question left by Vaikuntanathan and Zamir [SODA'26].
  We use these constructions to obtain faster protocols based on random Cliffords. We also explore their applications to the worst-case to average-case reductions for matrix and Clifford problems including the iterated matrix multiplication and Clifford circuit synthesis. In particular, we show the hardness of batching Clifford circuits: synthesizing circuits that apply the same Clifford to multiple registers is at least as hard as worst-case matrix multiplication, even when synthesis succeeds on a small constant fraction of random Cliffords. This extends to approximate implementations by general quantum circuits.

</details>


### [94] [Quantum Optimal Transport Barycenters: Existence, Duality, and Gaussian Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01855)
*Augusto Gerolin,Zhiyi Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了量子最优传输（QOT）重心框架，证明了存在性与对偶性，并针对高斯输入态建立了全局刚性定理：若至少一个高斯输入为忠实态，则2-量子Wasserstein重心在所有量子态中唯一且为高斯态；忠实性是充分非必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Wasserstein重心的理论推广到量子情形，统一处理量子态与量子信道两种表述下的2-量子Wasserstein重心，并回答核心问题——高斯输入态是否迫使重心本身为高斯且唯一（高斯刚性）。

Method: 在可分Hilbert空间上对一般（可能无界）传输代价建立QOT重心框架及其存在性与对偶性；通过选取与Caglioti–Golse–Mouhot–Paul及De Palma–Trevisan的2-量子Wasserstein距离相对应的典范二次代价算子，统一处理量子态与量子信道两种表述；对高斯输入，将重心问题化为协方差矩阵上的有限维凸优化，并借助协方差互补松弛下的状态重构原理，把最优协方差的唯一性提升为完整量子态的唯一性。

Result: 重心问题存在高斯极小元，可约化为协方差矩阵上的有限维凸优化。通过协方差互补松弛的状态重构原理，证明全局刚性定理：只要至少一个高斯输入为忠实态，则重心在所有量子态中唯一且必为高斯态。忠实性为充分非必要条件：纯态族仍确定唯一重心，而部分纯非忠实高斯输入可能允许多个重心。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子态提供了与量子Wasserstein重心相对应的QOT重心理论，统一了量子态与量子信道框架，并通过状态重构原理解决了"最优协方差唯一不等于量子态唯一"的关键困难，给出了2-量子Wasserstein高斯刚性的一般性结论及忠实性条件的充要性刻画。

Abstract: We develop a Quantum Optimal Transport (QOT) barycenter framework for quantum states, as an analog of Wasserstein barycenters~\cite{AguCar}, and establish existence and duality results for a broad class of possibly unbounded transport costs on separable Hilbert spaces. Our framework provides, in particular, a unified treatment of $2$-quantum Wasserstein (QW) barycenters in both the quantum-state and quantum-channel formulations by specializing to the canonical quadratic cost operators associated with the $2$-quantum Wasserstein distances of Caglioti--Golse--Mouhot--Paul~\cite{caglioti2021towards,Golse-Mouhot-Paul:2016} and De Palma--Trevisan~\cite{DPaTre19}.
  The central results concern Gaussian input states and, in particular, Gaussian rigidity: whether Gaussian input states force the 2-QW barycenter itself to be Gaussian and uniquely determined when the minimization is taken over all quantum states. We first show that the barycenter problem admits a Gaussian minimizer and reduces to a finite-dimensional convex optimization problem over covariance matrices.
  The main difficulty is that uniqueness of the optimal covariance does not, in general, imply uniqueness of the underlying quantum state. We bridge this gap through a state-reconstruction principle under covariance complementary slackness that upgrades uniqueness of the optimal covariance to uniqueness of the full quantum state, and thereby prove a global rigidity theorem: if at least one Gaussian input is faithful, then the barycenter is unique among all quantum states and is necessarily Gaussian. Faithfulness is sufficient but not necessary: families of pure inputs still determine a unique barycenter, whereas partially pure nonfaithful Gaussian inputs may admit multiple barycenters.

</details>


### [95] [Approximation theorems for fermionic Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01860)
*Amir-Reza Negari,Farzin Salek,Zoltán Zimborás,Aram Harrow,Patrick Hayden,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在Majorana协方差矩阵层面建立了费米子高斯态"充分分布相互作用导致有效平均场描述"的定量版本，核心是协方差矩阵的可容许性条件诱导的两点关联定量单配性(monogamy)约束，并据此导出三类结果：高次图上二次费米哈密顿量的乘积态近似、有限费米高斯de Finetti定理、以及指数聚类下的条件互信息衰减与可恢复性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体物理中"充分分布的相互作用或关联导致有效平均场描述"是反复出现的原理，但对费米子高斯态此前缺乏直接定量刻画，尤其在协方差矩阵层面缺少统一的关联约束工具。作者希望以Majorana协方差矩阵可容许性为出发点，给出一个统一原理，同时解释高次图哈密顿量近似、de Finetti型交换性、以及空间态可恢复性这三类看似独立的现象。

Method: 核心观察：费米子协方差矩阵的可容许性条件对两点关联施加定量单配性约束——固定区域仅拥有有界的协方差"预算"可分配给众多不相交区域。作者直接在该协方差层面展开论证并应用于三个方向：(1) 对高次有限简单图上的二次费米哈密顿量，构造基态能量与有限温度自由能的乘积态近似，D-正则图误差为O(D^{-1/2})；(2) 证明有限费米高斯de Finetti定理，k份在分级费米拷贝置换下n-可交换的高斯态，与其单份边缘之积的迹范数距离为O(k/n)，无限可交换则精确为乘积结构；(3) 对具有指数聚类互信息的空间费米高斯态，证明跨越分隔缓冲区的条件互信息按O(r^{-1})衰减，并借助可恢复性得到O(r^{-1/2})迹范数近似。

Result: (1) 高次图上的二次费米哈密顿量获得基态能量与自由能的乘积态近似，D-正则图误差O(D^{-1/2})；(2) 有限高斯de Finetti定理成立，n-可交换k份高斯态距单份边缘之积O(k/n)，无限可交换蕴含精确乘积结构；(3) 指数聚类空间高斯态的条件互信息跨缓冲区以O(r^{-1})衰减，并由可恢复性给出O(r^{-1/2})迹范数近似。

Conclusion: 费米子高斯系统的乘积近似、de Finetti近似与可恢复性估计均可直接由协方差层面的结构导出，表明协方差矩阵的单配性约束是统一而有效的分析工具。

Abstract: A recurring principle in many-body physics is that sufficiently distributed interactions or correlations lead to an effectively mean-field description. For fermionic Gaussian states, we establish a quantitative version of this principle directly at the level of Majorana covariance matrices. The main observation is that the admissibility condition for fermionic covariance matrices imposes a quantitative monogamy constraint on two-point correlations: a fixed region has only a bounded covariance budget to distribute among many disjoint regions. We use this principle in three directions. First, for quadratic fermionic Hamiltonians on finite simple graphs of high degree, we obtain product-state approximations to both the ground-state energy and the finite-temperature free energy, with error of order \(D^{-1/2}\) for \(D\)-regular graphs. Second, we prove a finite fermionic Gaussian de Finetti theorem: a \(k\)-copy Gaussian state that is \(n\)-exchangeable under graded fermionic copy permutations is \(O(k/n)\)-close in trace norm to the product of its one-copy marginal. In particular, infinite exchangeability within the Gaussian class implies exact product structure. Third, for spatial fermionic Gaussian states with exponentially clustering mutual information, we prove that the conditional mutual information across a separating buffer decays as \(O(r^{-1})\), and hence, by recoverability, the state admits an \(O(r^{-1/2})\) trace-norm approximation by a recovered state. These results show that product and de Finetti approximations and recoverability estimates for fermionic Gaussian systems can all be derived directly from covariance-level structure.

</details>


### [96] [Fourier Symmetrization for Geometric Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01874)
*Letao Wang,Abdel Lisser,Sreejith Sreekumar,Zeno Toffano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文用傅里叶表示研究量子傅里叶模型(QFM)中对称性对表达能力的影响:对称性将频谱组织成轨道并合并系数,在2-design条件下给出系数量方差等式及单层近似2-design的偏差上界(比已有界指数级更紧),并据此提出无需辅助比特或额外深度的随机化编码,在两类PDE的QPINN任务上取得最低平均误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何量子机器学习将对称性引入量子模型,但对称性如何塑造其表达能力、以及如何指导有效模型设计仍缺乏理解。论文旨在通过QFM的傅里叶表示回答这一问题,并解决实现对称化模型通常需要辅助比特或量子twirling额外电路深度的问题。

Method: 以QFM的傅里叶表示为框架:对称性把频谱组织为轨道,并将轨道内傅里叶系数求和为对称化系数;当可训练层构成独立精确2-design时,证明各对称化系数的方差等于其轨道内系数方差之和;对含ε-近似2-design可训练层的单层QFM,给出偏离该恒等式的界。进一步分析超八面体群导致的轨道增长对对称化系数表达能力消失的缓解作用,并证明在初等阿贝尔2-群上对称化得到纯多元Chebyshev多项式基函数。提出随机化编码,无需辅助比特与量子twirling的额外深度即可实现不变模型。

Result: 在精确2-design下得到对称化系数方差等于轨道内系数方差之和的恒等式;对单层ε-近似2-design QFM给出偏差上界,其单个傅里叶系数的界可较已有界指数级更紧;超八面体群示例显示轨道增长可缓解对称化系数的表达能力消失;初等阿贝尔2-群对称化对应纯多元Chebyshev基。在二维屏蔽Poisson方程与稳态粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程上,基于硬边界约束的QPINN中,精确对称化与随机化编码分别取得两个基准的最低平均误差。

Conclusion: 对称性通过轨道结构显著影响QFM的频谱与表达能力,2-design框架为系数量方差与偏差提供可控的理论刻画;随机化编码是一种无需辅助比特或额外电路深度即可实现不变QFM的可行方案,并在PDE求解的QPINN基准上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Geometric quantum machine learning incorporates symmetry into quantum models, but how symmetry shapes their expressivity and guides effective model design remains insufficiently understood. We address this question through the Fourier representation of quantum Fourier models (QFMs). Symmetry organizes the frequency spectrum into orbits and sums the Fourier coefficients within each orbit into a symmetrized coefficient. When QFM trainable layers form independent exact 2-designs, the variance of each symmetrized coefficient equals the sum of the coefficient variances in its orbit. For single-layer QFMs with $\varepsilon$-approximate 2-design trainable layers, we bound the deviation from this identity. The resulting bound for individual Fourier coefficients can be exponentially tighter than an existing bound. The hyperoctahedral group provides an example of orbit growth that can mitigate vanishing expressivity of the symmetrized coefficients. Symmetrization of QFMs over an elementary abelian 2-group also yields pure multivariate Chebyshev polynomial basis functions. We introduce randomized encoding, which implements invariant models without ancilla qubits or the additional circuit depth for quantum twirling. We evaluate the models as quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) on two-dimensional screened Poisson and stationary viscous Hamilton-Jacobi equations. Under hard boundary constraints, QPINNs using exact symmetrization and randomized encoding achieve the lowest mean errors in the two benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [97] [Floquet-Universal Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01878)
*Emilio Onorati,Harriet Apel,Michael M. Wolf,Toby Cubitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了 Floquet 模拟理论：用周期性驱动哈密顿量合成时间无关哈密顿量，证明集合 S 可 Floquet 模拟 Lie(S) 中任意哈密顿量，并给出 Floquet 通用哈密顿量的构造性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类比模拟与数字哈密顿量模拟分别对应时间无关哈密顿量与完全可控时间依赖量子电路两种动力学机制。时间周期哈密顿量代表介于两者之间的中间机制，但其模拟理论尚不完善，因此作者希望建立统一的 Floquet 模拟理论。

Method: 作者采用周期性驱动的哈密顿量来合成时间无关哈密顿量，证明任意相互作用集合 S 都能 Floquet 模拟其李代数 Lie(S) 中的所有哈密顿量，从而构造性地刻画 Floquet 通用哈密顿量。构造仅使用 O(1) 的局部相互作用强度及其比例。

Result: 该构造避免了类比模拟中不切实际的多尺度局部相互作用强度；对于 k-local 格点哈密顿量等重要类别，所需驱动频率范围随系统规模多项式增长。此外还导出多项复杂性理论结果，包括 Floquet 物理中自然物理性质与量的 BQP-完全性与 QMA-困难性结论。

Conclusion: Floquet 模拟提供了介于类比与数字模拟之间的可行中间路径，仅需 O(1) 局部相互作用强度即可实现通用模拟，并对复杂性与量子引力等相关领域产生直接复杂性理论意义。

Abstract: Analogue and digital Hamiltonian simulation represent two distinct approaches to making one quantum system replicate the physical properties of another, with consequences ranging from practical applications to complexity theory and quantum gravity. They are rooted in two distinct regimes of quantum dynamics: time-independent Hamiltonians for the former; fully controllable time-dependent quantum circuits in the latter.
