Time-periodic Hamiltonians represent an intermediate regime. In this work, we establish a theory of Floquet simulation where we use periodically driven Hamiltonians to synthesise time-independent ones.
  We show that a set of interactions $S$ can Floquet-simulate every Hamiltonian in the Lie algebra $\mathrm{Lie}(S)$, and consequently we give a complete and constructive characterisation of Floquet-universal Hamiltonians that are able to produce any target Hamiltonian. Notably, our construction uses only $O(1)$ local interaction strengths and ratios thereof, avoiding the impractical multi-scale local interaction strengths often required in analogue simulation via time-independent Hamiltonians. Instead, the construction requires a range of driving frequencies which scales polynomially with system size for important classes such as $k$-local lattice Hamiltonians. This has immediate complexity-theoretic implications, including numerous BQP-completeness and QMA-hardness results for natural physical properties and quantities studied in Floquet physics.

</details>


### [98] [Exponential quantum advantages for decoded quantum interferometry in the streaming setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01902)
*Kewen Wu,Guangxu Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在流式模型下研究解码量子干涉（DQI）算法的空间优势，通过对最优多项式交（OPI）问题的推广（基于Hermite插值与Hasse导数），首次无条件证明了DQI在内存资源上的量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI已被证明在时间维度上对OPI问题具备量子优势，但空间（内存）同样是核心资源，尤其是逻辑比特的容错开销巨大。作者希望回答：DQI是否在内存上也具备量子优势，并且能否无条件地证明这一点。

Method: 在流式（streaming）设置下，考虑OPI的自然推广：用Hermite插值和Hasse导数刻画的问题——寻找一个低次多项式，使其尽可能多地满足关于其取值和导数的约束，并分析该问题在量子与经典算法下的空间复杂度。

Result: 量子效率：DQI算法的变体可产出满足93%约束的多项式，且仅需单遍读取输入流、使用多对数空间、每次流条目的计算时间为多对数。经典难度：任何满足76%约束的经典算法，即使允许多项式次遍数和无限时间，仍需多项式空间。结果给出了可调参数的完整权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 该结果首次无条件地证明了DQI在内存资源上的量子优势，并由此推出DQI对原始OPI问题具备可证明的量子优势。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a polynomial-time quantum algorithm introduced by Jordan et al. (Nature 2025). For a natural optimization problem, known as optimal polynomial intersection (OPI), it achieves approximation guarantees in regimes where all known classical algorithms require exponential time.
  Besides time, space is another central resource: storing and manipulating a massive input can be very challenging, especially when logical qubits carry substantial fault-tolerant implementation overhead. This motivates the following question: does DQI yield quantum advantages in memory, and can we prove it unconditionally?
  We give an affirmative answer to this question in the streaming setting. In particular, we consider a natural generalization of OPI using Hermite interpolation and Hasse derivatives, which asks for a low-degree polynomial satisfying as many constraints on its values and derivatives as possible. As a concrete example, we show
  [Quantum efficiency.] An adaptation of the DQI algorithm produces a polynomial satisfying $93\%$ of the constraints; moreover, it only reads the input stream in one pass, uses polylogarithmic space, and has polylogarithmic computation time per stream entry.
  [Classical hardness.] Any classical algorithm that produces an answer satisfying just $76\%$ of the constraints requires polynomial space, even if it can read the input stream with polynomially many passes and can use unlimited time.
  Our result provides a complete tradeoff curve for the tunable parameters, and implies that DQI has provable quantum advantages for the original OPI problem.

</details>


### [99] [Loss-tolerant distributed lattice surgery using fusion networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01923)
*Felix Burt,Richard Meister,Sheng-Ku Lin,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Michael Hanks,Roberto Bondesan,M. S. Kim,Kin K. Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用ZX演算变换构建混合症状提取协议，将其应用于分布式旋转表面码格点手术，证明多种协议在无局域噪声时可达50%界面擦除阈值，并将擦除阈值转化为光子损耗阈值、分析亚阈值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过网络连接物质型量子处理单元（QPU）是实现可扩展容错量子计算机的有前景途径，但光子链路中的噪声（光子损耗、概率性线性光学操作）本质上强于且不同于局域QPU，因此需要在网络化QPU接口处设计对损耗和概率性光子操作鲁棒的分布式逻辑操作，以补充电路型纠错。

Method: 采用ZX演算（ZX calculus）变换构造混合症状提取（syndrome extraction）协议，并将其应用于分布式旋转表面码的格点手术（lattice surgery）；构建了使用融合贝尔对的电路型格点手术以及使用线性簇态的混合协议；相对直接贝尔对接口几何，计算接口距离变化；分析截断资源态下的阈值下降，并刻画资源态错误、局域电路噪声和融合擦除下的可纠正区域；随后将擦除阈值转换为光子损耗阈值，并用融合增强（fusion boosting）探测亚阈值性能。

Result: 在局域噪声缺失时，若干协议达到50%的接口擦除阈值，包括使用融合贝尔对的电路型格点手术和使用线性簇态的混合协议。相对直接贝尔对接口几何，混合协议将merge观测量接口距离从d+1提升至2d+1，并将perpendicular观测量接口距离从⌊(d+1)/2⌋恢复至d。计算了截断资源态导致的阈值下降，并映射了资源态错误、局域电路噪声与融合擦除下的可纠正区域；将擦除阈值换算为光子损耗阈值。在电路与资源态错误率为10^-3时，局域错误大体掩盖了接口距离优势；当局域噪声降至10^-4及以下时，线性链协议随擦除减小改善更快。

Conclusion: 混合症状提取协议（尤其基于线性簇态者）可显著提升接口距离并保持高擦除容限，从而在低局域噪声区制下有效发挥接口距离增强的优势，为网络化QPU的容错接口提供可行方案；但需保持局域噪声足够低（约10^-4及以下）否则局域错误会掩盖该优势。

Abstract: Networking matter-based quantum processing units (QPUs) offers a promising route to scaling fault-tolerant quantum computers. This requires distributed logical operations to be performed across photonic links, where noise is characteristically different from and stronger than in local QPUs owing to photon loss and probabilistic linear-optical operations. Measurement- and fusion-based quantum computing are designed to be robust against loss and probabilistic photonic operations, suggesting they could complement circuit-based error correction at the interface between networked QPUs. We use ZX calculus transformations to construct hybrid syndrome extraction protocols and apply them to distributed rotated surface code lattice surgery, demonstrating that several protocols attain a $50\%$ interface-erasure threshold when local noise is absent, including circuit-based lattice surgery using fused Bell pairs and hybrid protocols using linear cluster states. Relative to a straight Bell-pair interface geometry, the hybrid protocols increase the merge-observable interface distance from $d+1$ to $2d+1$ and restore the perpendicular-observable interface distance from $\lfloor(d+1)/2\rfloor$ to $d$. We calculate how the threshold decreases when using truncated resource states and map correctable regions under resource-state errors, local circuit noise, and fusion erasure. We then convert these erasure thresholds into photon-loss thresholds and probe subthreshold performance using fusion boosting. At circuit and resource-state error rates of $10^{-3}$, local errors largely mask the interface-distance advantage. As local noise is reduced to $10^{-4}$ and below, the linear-chain protocols improve more rapidly with decreasing erasure, suggesting that interface distance enhancements are effective in low local noise regimes.

</details>


### [100] [Avoiding Exponentially Large Groups with Open Quantum System Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01932)
*Jihong Cai,Advith Govindarajan,Marius Junge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用开放量子系统进行态制备的新方法：在(n+3)量子比特上识别出一个维度为poly(n)的小李群G，结合环境中的偏迹与态制备，可从任意固定输入态逼近任意量子态；并给出实现密度矩阵空间传递性及信道通用性的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统态制备需在n量子比特的完整酉群上操作，代价高。作者希望借助开放量子系统的全部能力，用更小的群和更经济的操作实现通用态制备与信道模拟。

Method: 在(n+3)量子比特上识别一个由局部Pauli串李代数导出的小李群G，其李代数维度为poly(n)；通过反复使用偏迹、环境中的态制备与群操作来逼近目标态；识别单个开放系统magic相互作用哈密顿量，其酉群与开放系统量子技术结合可在密度矩阵空间上实现传递性；用更大环境逼近n量子比特上的所有信道。

Result: 从任意固定输入态出发，借助上述小群与开放系统操作可逼近任意量子态；单个magic相互作用哈密顿量的酉群结合开放系统技术可实现对全体密度的传递性；使用更大环境可实现n量子比特信道的通用性；并由此得到廉价态/密度/信道与昂贵态/密度/信道的新图景。

Conclusion: 开放量子系统结合小李群可显著降低态制备与信道模拟的资源需求，所识别的局部Pauli串李代数小群为量子态、密度矩阵与信道建立了一种新的成本分类框架。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to state preparation by embracing the full power of open quantum systems. Instead of working with the whole unitary group in $n$ qubits, we identify a small Lie group $G$ in $(n+3)$ qubits. The dimension of its Lie algebra is $\text{poly}(n)$. Every state can be approximated starting from any fixed input state by repeatedly using partial trace and state preparation in the environment alongside group operations. Moreover, we also identify a single (open system magic) interaction Hamiltonian whose unitary group can be combined with open system quantum technology mentioned above to achieve transitivity on the space of all densities. Similarly, we show that all channels on $n$ qubits can be approximated by using a larger environment, achieving channel universality. The interesting small groups we identify are derived from Lie algebras of local Pauli strings and lead to a new landscape of cheap and expensive states, densities, and channels.

</details>


### [101] [Optimal query complexity for fractional quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01940)
*Anthony Yuezhang Liu,Adam Wesołowski,Jayne Thompson,Mile Gu,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了分数次量子查询问题的查询复杂度下界，与该问题已知的QSVT上界匹配，从而确定其最优查询复杂度为Θ(1/δ log(1/ε))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定未知酉算符U=e^{iH}的oracle访问，分数次查询问题研究实现非整数幂U^t=e^{itH}（0<t<1）所需的查询次数，其中谱与分支割之间有间隙δ。QSVT给出了O(1/δ log(1/ε))的上界，但该复杂度的最优性尚不明确，需要匹配的下界来确认。

Method: 作者将任意N次查询电路归约为用次数不超过O(N)的三角多项式逼近e^{itθ}的问题，并结合Remez不等式进行下界分析；此外还给出一种基于构造线性泛函（该泛函湮灭逼近空间）的替代下界证明。

Result: 证明了匹配下界Ω_τ(1/δ log(1/ε))，因此分数次查询问题的最优查询复杂度为Θ_τ(1/δ log(1/ε))，表明已知QSVT构造渐近最优；替代方法给出对δ一致的Ω_τ(log(1/ε))下界。

Conclusion: 分数次量子查询问题的最优查询复杂度被确定为Θ_τ(1/δ log(1/ε))，说明QSVT构造在渐近意义下不可改进。

Abstract: Given oracle access to an unknown unitary $U=e^{iH}$ , the fractional query problem asks how many queries are required to implement a noninteger power $U^t=e^{itH}$, $0<t<1$, when the spectrum is separated from the branch cut by a gap $δ$. Quantum singular value transformation gives an upper bound of $O\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ queries for approximation error $\varepsilon$. We prove a matching lower bound for arbitrary query algorithms. Our argument reduces any $N$-query circuit to the approximation of $e^{itθ}$ by a trigonometric polynomial with degree bounded by $O(N)$, together with Remez inequality. This allows us to establish the lower bound of $Ω_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$. Consequently, the optimal query complexity for fractional query problem is $Θ_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$, showing that the known QSVT construction is asymptotically optimal. We also give an alternative lower bound proof based on constructing a linear functional that annihilates the approximant space, yielding a $Ω_τ\!\left(\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ bound uniform to $δ$.

</details>


### [102] [Universality Sacrifices Reliability in Classical-Quantum Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01941)
*Kaito Watanabe,Masahito Hayashi,Takaya Matsuura,Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究经典-量子信道的通用编码可靠性问题，证明了通用编码无法普遍达到信道已知情形下的最优可靠性，并分别用量子Rényi散度刻画了信道已知与通用情形下的最优错误指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信道中通用码可同时达到容量与最优高码率可靠性，作者希望探究这一性质在经典-量子信道中是否依然成立，即通用编码是否会因不知道输出系统上的酉旋转而损失可靠性。

Method: 构造一族经典-量子信道证明固定编码方案无法达到信道已知的最优可靠性；针对酉不变解码器推导可靠性逆向界；并构造与信道无关的编解码对，给出可达的通用可靠性界。

Result: 证明经典信道中通用性与最优可靠性兼容的性质在经典-量子信道中一般失效；酉不变解码器的逆向界可严格小于信道已知最优错误指数；所构造的通用编解码对在高码率区间的可达界与逆向界吻合，从而刻画了最优通用可靠性。

Conclusion: 信道已知与通用情形下的错误指数分别由Petz Rényi散度和sandwiched Rényi散度决定；二者对可交换输出相同、对不可交换输出不同，这解释了通用性在经典中保持最优可靠性而在量子中会造成损失的原因，揭示了通用经典-量子信道编码的根本可靠性代价。

Abstract: Universal channel coding enables communication without a complete description of the channel. For classical channels, universal codes can attain both capacity and the optimal high-rate reliability. We show that this compatibility fails for classical-quantum channels in general; that is, the optimal reliability in the channel-aware scenario is not always achievable with universal coding due to the ignorance of the unitary rotation of the output system. We exhibit a family of classical-quantum channels for which one cannot achieve the channel-aware optimal reliability by a fixed coding scheme. We further derive a converse bound on the reliability for unitary-invariant decoders, a natural assumption for the universal coding scheme, that can be strictly smaller than the optimal channel-aware error exponent. Conversely, we construct a channel-independent encoder-decoder pair and establish a universally achievable bound on the reliability that matches this converse bound in the high-rate regime, thereby characterizing the optimal universal reliability. Specifically, the channel-aware and universal exponents are governed by the Petz and sandwiched Rényi divergences, respectively. These divergences coincide for commuting outputs but differ for noncommuting ones, explaining why universality preserves optimal reliability classically but can reduce it quantumly. Our results showcase the fundamental reliability cost of performing the classical-quantum channel coding task universally.

</details>


### [103] [Hardware-Efficient Ground-State Preparation using Variational Imaginary-Time Majorana Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01954)
*Federico Santona,Manuel G. Algaba,Aeishah Ameera Anuar,Anna M. Wernbacher,Prachi Sharma,Fedor Šimkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 VIME（变分虚时马约拉纳演化）经典预训练算法，将 OVQITE 扩展至分子电子结构，并配合压缩的 c-tUPS 拟设，以实现资源高效的量子化学基态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期容错量子化学需要紧凑的基态制备电路，而现有方法（如 ADAPT-VQE 类变分马约拉纳演化）在强关联体系下电路资源消耗大、精度受限。

Method: 1) VIME：基于单一编译的马约拉纳传播（MP）代理图，对 OVQITE 进行经典预训练；2) 开发压缩平铺酉积态（c-tUPS）拟设，同时降低经典模拟与量子态制备成本。

Result: 在强关联钌配合物 TLD-1411 上，最大 52 量子比特的活性空间计算达到化学精度（对比 DMRG），能量误差比 ADAPT-VMPE 约小 400 倍；在至七并苯（60 量子比特）的强多参考并苯系列中，VIME 精度匹配或优于 ADAPT-VMPE，CNOT 数最多减少 26 倍，双量子比特深度最多降低 167 倍。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了压缩变分拟设的虚时经典预训练是通向资源高效分子基态制备的有前景路径。

Abstract: Compact ground-state preparation circuits are essential for early fault-tolerant quantum chemistry. We introduce Variational Imaginary-time Majorana Evolution (VIME), a classical pre-training algorithm that extends operator-projected variational quantum imaginary-time evolution (OVQITE) to molecular electronic structure using a single compiled Majorana-propagation (MP) surrogate graph. We pair VIME with a compressed tiled Unitary Product State (c-tUPS) ansatz that we develop to reduce both classical simulation and quantum state-preparation costs. In the strongly correlated ruthenium complex TLD-1411, active-space calculations requiring up to 52 qubits achieve chemical precision against DMRG reference energies, with energy errors approximately $400\times$ smaller than those from ADAPT-VQE-based variational Majorana-propagation (ADAPT-VMPE). Across the strongly multireference acene series up to heptacene (60 qubits), VIME matches or improves on the MP-reported energy accuracy of the ADAPT-VMPE comparison points with up to $26\times$ fewer CNOTs and $167\times$ lower two-qubit depth. These results establish imaginary-time classical pre-training of compressed variational ansätze as a promising route to resource-efficient molecular ground-state preparation.

</details>


### [104] [Fully tolerant product state testing and closest product state learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01979)
*Zongbo Bao,Jonas Helsen,Tuyen Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知 $n$-qudit 态 $\rho$ 与乘积态接近性的容忍测试，给出所需副本数与 $n$ 无关的高效算法；并利用随机染色与分块谱投影技术，显著改进最近乘积态学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定未知多体量子态是否接近乘积态、以及学习最优乘积态，是量子态层析与量子性质测试中的核心问题。已有方法通常依赖随系统规模增长的副本数或计算开销，难以高效扩展，因此需要设计副本数 $n$-无关的容忍测试器，并降低最近乘积态学习对副本数的依赖。

Method: 1) 对未知态采用随机染色，将 $[n]$ 划分为 $q$ 个部分，证明总能找到一种划分，使与该划分下最近乘积态重叠的平方至多增加 $O(1/q)$ 的加性因子；2) 由此把原问题归约为 $q$ 方（局部维度可能增长）的容忍测试；3) 结合分块谱投影与 Harrow & Montanaro 乘积态检验的自然 $k$-副本推广，构造高效容忍测试器；4) 学习方面，采用 qudit 版 Bakshi 等高保真乘积态学习算法，并结合基于 Werner 最优克隆信道的采样技术。

Result: 测试问题获得时间高效且副本数与 $n$ 无关的算法。学习问题给出一个使用 $\widetilde{O}((nd)^2)2^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^8)}$ 个未知态副本、输出 $\varepsilon$-近似最优乘积态的算法，显著改进了最近乘积态学习。

Conclusion: 随机染色与分块谱投影是统一且有力的技术工具：既可用于乘积态度量下的容忍测试，也可用于最近乘积态学习，在副本复杂度与计算效率上均取得改进。

Abstract: We address the problem of testing whether an unknown $n$-qudit state $ρ$ is $a$-close to a product state or $b$-far away from any product state, as measured in terms of state overlap. We provide a time-efficient algorithm to solve this problem that requires an $n$-independent number of copies of the unknown state. Our random coloring argument shows that for any unknown state there always exists a partition of $[n]$ into $q$ parts such that the square of the overlap with the closest product state with respect to this partition is only an additive factor $O(1/q)$ larger. This reduces the problem to tolerant testing of $q$ parties, with potentially growing local dimensions. Combining this insight with blockwise spectral projection arguments we can show that the natural $k$-copy generalization of Harrow & Montanaro's product state test provides an efficient tolerant tester.
  We use the same random coloring and blockwise spectral projection techniques to obtain a substantially improved algorithm for closest product state learning. In particular we give an algorithm that takes in $\widetilde{O}\big((nd)^2\big)\, 2^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^8)}$ copies of the unknown state and produces an $\varepsilon$-approximately optimal product state. The key technical components of this learner are a qudit variant of the Bakshi et al. high-fidelity product state learning algorithm and a sampling technique based on Werner's optimal cloning channel.

</details>


### [105] [On the Metastability of the Mean-Field Interchange Model for Local Dimension $\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01987)
*Sergio Escobar,Lin Lin,Michael Ragone,Kevin D. Stubbs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究平均场交换模型（局域维数 d≥3、单格点广义泡利耦合）在 Davies 动力学下的热化行为，利用 Schur-Weyl 对偶将生成元分解为杨图上的经典 Markov 链，证明动力学相图由两个特征逆温 β_min(d) 与 β=d 刻画，静态一阶相变位于其间；在区间外谱隙以系统尺寸的多项式下界约束，区间内谱隙随 n 指数消失，表明低温亚稳导致的指数慢化完全源于杨图链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温下量子系统常因亚稳相存在而热化缓慢，但已有迹象表明进一步降温反而可能恢复多项式时间热化。作者旨在刻画这种非单调热化行为的机制，并给出严格的谱隙分析：弄清慢化发生在哪个温度区间、由何种自由度主导。

Method: 以平均场交换模型为对象（局域维数 d≥3，单格点广义泡利耦合），考察其 Davies 生成元。核心工具是 Schur-Weyl 对偶：将生成元分解到对称性分块，其中一块等价于杨图上的经典 Markov 生成元；对剩余各对称性扇区证明其谱隙为 Ω(n^{-1})(即多项式下界)，从而把整体谱隙的特征归结为杨图链的谱性质。

Result: 动力学相图由两个特征逆温 β_min(d) 与 β=d 确定，静态一阶相变恰好落在二者之间。当温度位于区间 (β_min(d), d) 之外（即更低或更高温）时，Davies 生成元的谱隙有系统尺寸 n 的逆多项式下界；而当温度处于该区间内时，谱隙随 n 指数消失，呈现亚稳性。由此判定指数慢化全部来源于杨图链。

Conclusion: 该工作表明低温热化并非单调变慢：越过一阶相变区后继续降温可恢复多项式时间热化，形成"快—慢—快"的动力学相图。方法上，Schur-Weyl 分解把量子耗散动力学约化为杨图上的经典 Markov 链，为分析平均场量子模型的谱隙与亚稳性提供了可推广的框架。

Abstract: Many quantum systems are believed to thermalize slowly at low temperatures due to the existence of metastable phases. However, there are cases where further cooling can restore polynomial-time thermalization: we demonstrate that the Davies dynamics of the mean-field interchange model with local dimension $d\geq 3$ and single-site generalized Pauli couplings exhibits this behavior. The dynamical phase diagram is beholden to two distinguished inverse temperatures, $β_{\mathrm{min}}(d)$ and $β=d$, with the static first-order phase transition lying between them. At low and high temperatures surrounding the interval $(β_{\mathrm{min}}(d),d)$, the spectral gap of the Davies generator is bounded below by an inverse polynomial in system size $n$. Meanwhile, in this interval, the gap vanishes exponentially in $n$, indicating metastability. The proof uses Schur-Weyl duality to decompose the Davies generator into a classical Markov generator on Young diagrams, and the restriction to the remaining symmetry sectors whose gap is $Ω(n^{-1})$. Thus, exponential slowdown arises entirely from the Young diagram chain.

</details>


### [106] [Geometry of Knill-Laflamme Coefficients for Pauli Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01992)
*Baisong Sun,Ningping Cao,Yiu Tung Poon,Bei Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Knill-Laflamme系数向量构成的联合高阶数值范围几何,证明算子表示的多重度足以将活跃期望数据提升为精确标量压缩,并由此刻画系数范围的凸性、连通性及签名谱的区间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knill-Laflamme系数通过误差算子到码空间的标量压缩刻画精确量子纠错检测,但对固定码维数下可达系数向量所形成的几何结构,以及算子表示、码维数与检测约束如何影响该几何,尚缺乏系统理解。

Method: 将可达系数向量视为联合高阶数值范围,定义其欧氏范数像为签名谱;通过引入"活跃期望体"与"表示多重度"概念,证明当每个被占据块的多重度至少为码维数乘以对应活跃态秩时,存在从活跃期望数据到精确标量压缩的提升。随后将该框架分别应用于交换Pauli族与子系统稳定子码,并构造母哈密顿量实现精确检测码,最后用三量子比特例子分析非交换检测约束。

Result: 证明表示多重度充分时系数范围等于完整活跃期望体,因而凸且连通,签名谱为闭区间;交换Pauli族在公共本征空间简并充分时给出多胞形,子系统码中受保护逻辑子系统提供多重度空间;实例显示增大码维数可使范围收缩、坍缩或变空,增大多重度可将Bloch球面变为实心球;三量子比特例子中额外非交换检测约束将四面体范围约化为其重心与四顶点,签名谱不连通。

Conclusion: 该工作为Knill-Laflamme系数几何提供了统一的结构性框架,揭示了多重度与码维数对系数范围几何的控制作用,并激励在结构化误差模型下进一步建立系数范围连通性与签名谱区间性的更精细判据。

Abstract: Knill-Laflamme coefficients characterize exact quantum error detection through scalar compressions of error operators to the code space. For prescribed Pauli observables and a fixed code dimension, attainable coefficient vectors form a joint higher-rank numerical range, whose Euclidean norm image is the signature spectrum. We study how operator representation, code dimension, and detection constraints govern this geometry. Our main theorem identifies representation multiplicity as sufficient to lift active expectation data to exact scalar compressions: a lift exists whenever each occupied block has multiplicity at least the code dimension times the rank of the corresponding active state. When multiplicity suffices to realize all active states, the coefficient range is the full active expectation body and is therefore convex and connected, with a closed interval as its signature spectrum. This framework unifies commuting Pauli families, where sufficient common-eigenspace degeneracy yields polytopes, and subsystem stabilizer codes, where protected logical subsystems supply multiplicity spaces for noncommuting gauge observables. Explicit examples show how increasing the code dimension can make a range shrink, collapse, or become empty, while increasing multiplicity can turn a Bloch sphere into a filled ball. In the subsystem setting, parent-Hamiltonian constructions realize exact detecting codes as degenerate ground spaces, selecting distinguished vectors and continuous paths within these ranges. A final three-qubit example shows additional noncommuting detection constraints reduce a tetrahedral range to its barycenter and four vertices, producing a disconnected signature spectrum. The results provide a structural framework for Knill-Laflamme coefficient geometry and motivate sharper criteria for connected coefficient ranges and interval signature spectra under structured error models.

</details>


### [107] [Operational transformation rates of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02025)
*Giulia Mazzola,Renato Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文放宽量子态多拷贝的iid假设，考虑实验上无法与张量幂区分的资源态集合，借助de Finetti型论证证明该操作集合可渐近表示为几乎iid态，并引入纠缠成本与蒸馏等渐近转化率的操作类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常用张量幂描述量子态的多份拷贝，但从操作角度看，实验者无法排除未知位置的不完美或拷贝间关联，因此iid假设先验上不成立，需要更一般的框架。

Method: 放宽iid假设，定义与张量幂态在操作上本质上不可区分的一整类资源态；运用de Finetti型论证分析其渐近结构。

Result: 证明该操作意义上的态集合可渐近地用几乎iid态表示；并由此引入纠缠成本、纠缠蒸馏等渐近转化率的操作类比。

Conclusion: 提出一个开放问题：这些操作类比定义的渐近转化率是否与iid情形下的对应速率一致。

Abstract: Typically, multiple copies of a quantum state are described by a tensor power of that state. However, from an operational point of view, an experimentalist cannot, in general, rule out imperfections at unknown positions or correlations between copies, so assuming independent and identically distributed (iid) copies is not justified a priori. In the following, we relax the iid assumption by considering a whole set of resource states that are fundamentally indistinguishable from a tensor power state, and in that sense look like many copies of the quantum state to the experimentalist. Using a de Finetti-type argument, we show that this operational set of states can asymptotically be represented by almost-iid states. As an application, we introduce operational analogues of asymptotic transformation rates, such as entanglement cost and distillation. This raises the question of whether these rates coincide with their iid counterparts.

</details>


### [108] [(A Variant of) Clifford Circuit Synthesis is NP-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02029)
*Luna Lima Keller,Richard Kueng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最优Clifford电路综合的复杂度，证明该问题是NP难的，与已知启发式方法（如归约到3-SAT）形成匹配的硬度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优电路综合是量子与经典硬件设计的核心问题。经典版本在启发式与严格硬度方面已有充分理解，但量子最优电路综合的认识仍很有限，尤其缺乏关于最优Clifford综合的硬度刻画。

Method: 证明分为两步：(i) 将3-正则图上的3-边着色归约到仅含CZ门的电路综合问题；(ii) 证明额外可用的基本Clifford门（特别是Hadamard、phase和CNOT）不能带来最优电路深度的进一步改进。

Result: 最优Clifford综合的一个变体是NP难的，即判定某个Clifford酉算子是否能在规定深度内实现是NP完全的。

Conclusion: 该工作加深了对Clifford电路复杂度理论的理解：其模拟与等价性检查属于P，而判定Clifford酉算子能否在给定深度内实现则是NP完全的。

Abstract: Optimal circuit synthesis is the problem of finding the shortest-depth circuit representation of a given functionality with respect to a pre-specified elementary gate set. This is a central problem in both quantum and classical hardware design. While the classical version is very well understood -- both in terms of heuristics and rigorous hardness assertions -- much less is known about optimal quantum circuit synthesis.
  We focus on optimal Clifford circuit synthesis, for which various heuristics are known, e.g. via reduction to 3-SAT. Our main result supplies a matching hardness result: a variant of optimal Clifford synthesis is NP-hard. The proof proceeds in two parts: (i) reduce 3-edge colorability on 3-regular graphs to a circuit synthesis problem that only involves CZ gates, (ii) prove that the availability of additional elementary Clifford gates -- most notably: Hadamard, phase and CNOT -- cannot lead to further improvements of the optimal circuit depth. Our work sharpens the complexity-theoretic understanding of Clifford circuits: simulation and equivalence checking are in P, whereas deciding whether a Clifford unitary admits an implementation within a prescribed depth is NP-complete.

</details>


### [109] [Sparse Hamiltonian simulation with optimal dependence on the maximum column Euclidean norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02030)
*Zecheng Li,Chunhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种模拟d-稀疏Hermitian哈密顿量的量子算法，查询复杂度为O(tΛ√d + √d log(2/ε))，消除了Low算法中的次多项式开销，并在多种参数下达到最优下界，同时应用于稀疏量子线性系统求解与黑盒酉算子实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low [STOC 2019] 的稀疏哈密顿量模拟算法存在次多项式开销，尤其对稀疏度d的依赖包含额外的次多项式因子，限制了效率；同时黑盒酉算子实现（Berry 与 Childs [QIC 2012]）的查询复杂度下界问题尚未解决。本文旨在消除这些开销、匹配已知下界并解决公开问题。

Method: 假设已知最大列欧几里得范数‖H‖_{1→2}的上界Λ，给出基于稀疏oracle查询的量子模拟算法；也可用谱范数上界替代Λ。通过减少查询次数至O(tΛ√d + √d log(2/ε))，并使用1-和2-qubit门数量线性于查询规模。将该模拟技术应用于求解d-稀疏量子线性系统，并在标准稀疏及态制备访问下实现黑盒酉算子。

Result: 在tΛ≥1/2时，模拟误差ε使用O(tΛ√d + √d log(2/ε))次稀疏oracle查询；当d>1且tΛ≥log(2/ε)时达到最坏情况下界。求解d-稀疏量子线性系统（‖A‖≤1, ‖A^{-1}‖≤κ）需O(κ√d polylog(κ/ε))次查询。黑盒酉算子（每行/列至多d个非零元）实现使用O(√d log(2/ε))次查询，常数误差下达到最优，任意N×N酉算子为Θ(√N)次查询。

Conclusion: 本文提出的量子算法去除了Low算法的次多项式开销，以加法对数精度项替代，并在广泛参数范围内匹配最坏情况查询下界；应用于稀疏量子线性系统和黑盒酉算子实现，解决了Berry与Childs提出的公开问题，实现了门高效且查询最优的酉算子模拟。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for simulating a $d$-sparse Hermitian Hamiltonian $H$, assuming a known upper bound $Λ$ on its maximum column Euclidean norm $\|H\|_{1\to2}$. For $tΛ\ge1/2$, simulation with operator-norm error $ε$ uses \[
  O\!\left(tΛ\sqrt d+\sqrt d\log(2/ε)\right) \] sparse-oracle queries. This removes the subpolynomial overhead in Low's algorithm [STOC 2019], replacing it with an additive logarithmic precision term. For $d>1$ and $tΛ\ge\log(2/ε)$, the bound matches the worst-case lower bound. A known spectral-norm upper bound may also be used in place of $Λ$. The number of 1- and 2-qubit gates is linear in the query scale, up to oracle costs and polynomial overhead in the input bit lengths and logarithmic precision parameters.
  As applications, we obtain $O(κ\sqrt d\,\mathrm{polylog}(κ/ε))$ queries for solving $d$-sparse quantum linear systems with $\|A\|\le1$ and $\|A^{-1}\|\leκ$, under standard sparse and state-preparation access. We also give a gate-efficient implementation of black-box unitaries with at most $d$ nonzero entries per row and column using $O(\sqrt d\log(2/ε))$ queries, given sparse access to the unitary and its adjoint. At constant error, the query bound is optimal and yields $Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries for arbitrary $N\times N$ unitaries, resolving the open question on black-box unitary implementation posed by Berry and Childs [QIC 2012].

</details>


### [110] [Adaptivity is all you need: Optimal stabilizer learning using just single-copy measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02031)
*L. Bittel,J. Eisert,W. Gong,A. A. Mele,L. Schatzki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明自适应单拷贝Clifford测量可将稳定子态学习的样本复杂度从Ω(n²)降至Θ(n)，与贝尔采样的最优复杂度匹配，并推广至有界T门Clifford电路制备的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子态在量子纠错、基准测试和经典模拟中处于核心地位，但其可学习性存在显著差距：两拷贝贝尔测量需Θ(n)份，而非自适应单拷贝测量需Ω(n²)份。作者希望探究自适应性是否能完全弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出多项式时间的自适应算法，仅使用Θ(n)次单拷贝Clifford测量即可学习任意n量子比特稳定子态；并基于相同思想构造样本最优的单拷贝容忍测试器，以及在k量子比特量子内存下达到最优测试权衡Θ(n-k+1/ε)。

Result: 自适应单拷贝测量达到与贝尔采样相同的Θ(n)最优样本复杂度；获得样本最优单拷贝容忍测试器；在k比特量子内存下达到最优测试权衡Θ(n-k+1/ε)（不忠实度为ε）；稳定子零度至多r的态（含有限T门Clifford电路制备态）可用O(n2^r)次单拷贝测量学习。

Conclusion: 自适应性完全弥合了单拷贝与两拷贝测量在稳定子态学习上的样本复杂度差距，并将该机制推广到非精确稳定子态，为量子态学习与测试提供了统一且样本最优的框架。

Abstract: Stabilizer states are central to quantum computing, underlying quantum error correction, benchmarking, and efficient classical simulation. Yet their learnability exhibits a striking gap: an $n$-qubit stabilizer state can be learned from $Θ(n)$ copies using two-copy Bell measurements, whereas non-adaptive single-copy measurements require $Ω(n^2)$ copies. Here we show that adaptivity completely closes this gap. We give a polynomial-time adaptive algorithm that learns an arbitrary $n$-qubit stabilizer state from $Θ(n)$ single-copy Clifford measurements, matching the optimal sample complexity of Bell sampling without any multi-copy measurements. The same ideas yield a sample-optimal single-copy tolerant tester and, with $k$ qubits of quantum memory, the optimal testing tradeoff $Θ(n-k+1/\varepsilon)$ at infidelity $\varepsilon$. Finally, we show that this adaptive mechanism extends beyond exact stabilizer states: states of stabilizer nullity at most $r$, including states prepared by Clifford circuits with a bounded number of $T$ gates, can be learned using $O(n2^{r})$ single-copy measurements.

</details>


### [111] [Nonlocality without entanglement for multipartite quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02032)
*Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了乘积测量的非局域性（无纠缠非局域性）及其在LOCC下的可区分性问题，揭示了测量层面存在有别于态层面的非局域现象，并给出了层级结构、方向性、一般化构造与光学实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 态层面的"无纠缠非局域性"已被广泛研究，但测量（quantum process）层面是否存在类似现象、其结构如何，尚不清楚；同时LOCC区分测量的能力边界与协议层级缺乏系统性刻画。

Method: 构造并分析双量子比特乘积测量的联合测量与LOCC区分对比；比较自适应与非自适应LOCC协议；分析双侧启动方向对可区分性的影响；构造n量子比特乘积测量对，使其全局可完美区分但在任意二分下即使允许各方任意联合量子操作也无法完美区分；并设计基于量子过程无纠缠非局域性的数据隐藏光学方案。

Result: 证明存在一对双量子比特乘积测量，联合访问时可完美区分，但在LOCC下不能完美区分；发现自适应LOCC可严格优于受限非自适应协议（用六个双分体qutrit乘积测量佐证）；LOCC可区分性本质上是方向性的；构造出全局可完美区分却在任意二分下不可完美区分的n量子比特乘积测量对；并给出基于基本光学元件的数据隐藏实现。

Conclusion: 测量层面存在"无纠缠非局域性"这一类比现象，且在极简设置中即出现；LOCC区分测量呈现自适应层级与方向依赖性，并可推广到任意二分不可区分的强非局域构造，具备光学可实现的数据隐藏应用潜力。

Abstract: We show that a pair of two-qubit product measurements can be perfectly distinguished when accessed jointly, yet cannot be perfectly distinguished when their local components are distributed between distant parties restricted to local quantum operations and classical communication (LOCC). This establishes a measurement analogue of nonlocality without entanglement that, unlike its state-based counterpart, emerges in a remarkably simple setting. We further uncover a hierarchy of LOCC discrimination strategies: adaptive protocols, in which the probe used at one site may depend on classical information obtained from another, can strictly outperform restricted non-adaptive protocols, as demonstrated by a set of six bipartite qutrit product measurements. We also show that LOCC distinguishability of bipartite measurements can be intrinsically directional, depending on which party initiates the protocol. More generally, we construct pairs of $n$-qubit product measurements that are globally perfectly distinguishable but cannot be perfectly distinguished across any bipartition, even when all parties on either side of the bipartition are allowed arbitrary joint quantum operations. Finally, we show how quantum data hiding based on quantum-process nonlocality without entanglement can be realized using elementary optical components.

</details>


### [112] [Complexity and Applications of Nearest Stabilizer Product State Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02037)
*Daniel Grier,Hakop Pashayan,Luke Schaeffer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在稳定子乘积态上的最优化问题：给定n量子比特稳定子态|ψ⟩和单量子比特稳定子态集合S，最大化|⟨ψ|φ₁,…,φ_n⟩|²，并对该"最近稳定子乘积态"问题给出完整复杂度分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题在变分集合S时具有多种应用价值：可用于改进某些经典模拟算法的运行时间上界、度量纠缠，以及与低秩矩阵补全的复杂度相关。因此需要对该问题的计算复杂度进行系统刻画。

Method: 通过改变单量子比特稳定子态集合S，研究最大化重叠（保真度）|⟨ψ|φ₁,…,φ_n⟩|²的优化问题。在考虑到Clifford群对称性后，将可能的集合S归类，共得到9种不同的集合，并逐一分析其复杂度。

Result: 经过Clifford群对称性归约后，共有9种不同的集合S。结果显示，除最简单的两种集合外，其余全部为NP完全的。

Conclusion: 最近稳定子乘积态问题获得了完整的复杂度分类，且绝大多数情形是NP完全的；该问题在经典模拟、纠缠度量和低秩矩阵补全等场景中具有应用意义。

Abstract: Consider the following optimization problem over stabilizer product states: given an $n$-qubit stabilizer state $\left|ψ\right\rangle$ and a set of single-qubit stabilizer states $S$, maximize $\left|\langle ψ| φ_1, \ldots, φ_n \rangle\right|^2$ over single-qubit stabilizer states $\left|φ_i\right\rangle \in S$. By varying the set $S$, we show that solutions to this problem can be useful in a variety of settings: tighter runtime bounds for certain classical simulation algorithms; measures of entanglement; and the complexity of low-rank matrix completion. Moreover, we give a complete complexity classification of this nearest stabilizer product state problem. After accounting for the symmetries in the Clifford group, there are $9$ distinct possible sets $S$, and we show that all but the two simplest of these are $\NP$-complete.

</details>


### [113] [Fermionic Gaussianity can be tested with mode-independent sample-complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02050)
*Maxwell West,Martin Larocca*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种新的通用上界技术，用于约束"远离目标态族"的态在自然2-拷贝测试下的接受概率，从而给出决策问题样本复杂度的上界；据此证明判定费米子高斯性（含Slater行列式态）可用最优的Θ(ε^{-2})份拷贝完成，且常用Bell采样测试即为最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定未知量子态是否属于或远离某给定态族是量子信息论的自然问题。此类问题通常存在自然的2-拷贝测试，对属于目标族的态总是接受，但对远离族的态，其接受概率的上界往往难以刻画，从而难以确定决策问题本身的样本复杂度。

Method: 发展一种简单的通用新技术，对任意态族上界自然2-拷贝测试在远离态上的接受概率，进而上界决策问题的样本复杂度。将该框架应用于费米子高斯态族，对著名的Bell采样测试进行样本复杂度分析；并进一步应用于Slater行列式态族。

Result: 证明判定未知纯n模态是费米子高斯态、或与所有高斯态在迹距离上至少ε-远，可用最优拷贝数Θ(ε^{-2})完成；并证明Bell采样测试对该问题是样本复杂度最优的，即便与允许任意集体测量和自适应测量的协议相比也是如此。对Slater行列式态族的类似判定问题，也能以与模态无关（mode-independent）的样本复杂度求解。

Conclusion: 该通用上界技术为2-拷贝测试与态族判定给出了有力的复杂度分析工具；其核心应用表明Bell采样对费米子高斯性检验达到最优样本复杂度，且Slater行列式态判定同样高效，凸显了框架的普适性与实用性。

Abstract: Deciding whether an unknown quantum state either belongs to, or is far from, a given family of states is a natural question of quantum information theory. In many such cases, there is a natural 2-copy test which always accepts whenever the state indeed belongs to the target family; it is often much more difficult, however, to bound the probability of acceptance for states that are far from the family. Here we develop a simple new technique for upper bounding this probability for general families, and therefore upper bounding the sample-complexity of the decision problem itself. As a particularly striking example of this framework, we show that deciding whether an unknown pure $n$-mode state is fermionic Gaussian, or at least $\varepsilon$-far in trace distance from all Gaussian states, can be accomplished using the optimal number $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ of copies. Remarkably, our analysis applies to the well-known Bell sampling procedure for testing fermionic Gaussianity, which we therefore show to be optimal, even compared to protocols making arbitrary collective and adaptive measurements. As a second example, we show that the analogous decision problem for the family of Slater determinant states can also be solved with mode-independent sample-complexity.

</details>


### [114] [Sequential Capacity of Quantum Processes with Finite Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02068)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出"顺序响应容量"指标，量化固定内部存储的量子设备在长时间运行时响应复杂度，证明容量随运行步数K呈KlogK增长，并识别逆残余相位翻转概率为限制该对数增强的相干时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究固定内部存储的量子设备在运行时间延长时，其响应复杂度能增长到何种程度，并对比量子与经典随机过程的差异。

Method: 引入"顺序响应容量"概念，即用各阶段全新运行、在给定响应概率间隔下持续区分可能过程的自适应测试阶段数；通过单可见量子比特上的时间依赖相位旋转构造测试方案，并与固定基测量的经典随机过程比较；进一步分析存储经典标签选择的相位序列在已知独立Pauli噪声下的表现，在理想控制与弱残余相位噪声条件下证明匹配的容量界。

Result: 固定系统与存储规模下，容量与运行长度、概率分辨率满足紧致定律；固定分辨率下容量以KlogK量级增长（K为每次运行的时间步数）；该构造无需额外内部存储且测试响应概率恰为0或1；相同测试下经典随机过程容量仅线性增长；残余相位噪声分析给出匹配容量界。

Conclusion: 量子设备可借助时间依赖相位旋转实现超越经典的KlogK顺序响应容量增长，但该对数增强受残余相位噪声限制，其相干时间尺度由逆残余相位翻转概率决定。

Abstract: How complex can the responses of a quantum device become as it runs longer with a fixed internal memory? We quantify this complexity through sequential response capacity: how many adaptive testing stages, each using a fresh run, can continue to separate possible processes by a prescribed gap in response probabilities. For fixed system and memory sizes, we establish a tight law relating this capacity to run length and probability resolution. At fixed resolution, the capacity grows on the order of $K\log K$, where $K$ is the number of time steps in each run. Our construction attains this growth using time-dependent phase rotations on a single visible qubit with no additional internal memory; its tests give response probabilities exactly zero or one. Under the same tests, classical stochastic processes that measure in a fixed basis at every step have only linear capacity at fixed sizes and resolution. For phase sequences selected by a stored classical label, we then quantify how known independent Pauli noise changes this logarithmic enhancement. With ideal controls and weak residual phase noise after correction, we prove matching capacity bounds at a fixed small probability gap. These bounds identify the inverse residual phase-flip probability as the coherence timescale that limits the extra logarithmic growth.

</details>


### [115] [Universal Bound and Phase Transition in Many-Body Fermionic Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02075)
*Masahiro Hoshino,Ryota Matsuda,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以魔幻Rényi熵（MRE）度量费米子非高斯性，给出其通用上界与Haar均值，证明大系统极限下典型Haar随机态达到每马约拉纳 ln(4/3) 的极大MRE密度；并在SYK模型下演化高斯态，发现由MRE密度拐点及四副本置换对称性自发破缺标志的一级相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子非高斯性是通用量子计算的重要资源，可由量子多体相互作用产生。作者希望量化非高斯性的上界、典型态行为及其增长动力学，并考察非高斯性增长是否伴随热力学量无法反映的定性变化。

Method: 以魔幻Rényi熵（MRE）为非高斯性度量：推导MRE的通用上界与Haar均值；构造协方差矩阵相同但MRE显著不同的态；在Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）哈密顿量下对高斯态进行虚时与实时演化，通过调节虚时/实时时长扫描MRE密度，并分析评估MRE所用四副本间的置换对称性。

Result: 证明典型Haar随机态在大系统极限下达到每马约拉纳 ln(4/3) 的极大MRE密度；显示相同协方差矩阵的态之间MRE可存在广泛差异；在SYK演化中，随虚时与实时时长变化，MRE密度出现拐点，同时四副本置换对称性自发破缺，标志一级相变。

Conclusion: 费米子非高斯性存在极大密度 ln(4/3)/Majorana；SYK演化中的一级相变代表非高斯性的定性变化，且该变化不体现在热自由能中，说明MRE能捕捉超越热力学描述的资源性质转变。

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity is a resource for universal quantum computation that can be generated by interactions in quantum many-body systems. Using the magic Rényi entropy (MRE) as a measure of non-Gaussianity, we derive its universal upper bound and Haar mean, proving that typical Haar-random states attain the maximal MRE density of $\ln(4/3)$ per Majorana in the large-system limit. We also show that the MRE can differ extensively between states with identical covariance matrices. To investigate how non-Gaussianity grows toward this maximal density, we evolve a Gaussian state in imaginary and real time under the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Hamiltonian. By varying the imaginary- and real-time durations, we identify a first-order transition marked by a kink in the MRE density and spontaneous breaking of the permutation symmetry among the four copies used to evaluate the MRE. This transition represents a qualitative change in the non-Gaussianity of the state that is not reflected in the thermal free energy.

</details>


### [116] [A computational phase diagram for the transverse field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02079)
*Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了横场Ising模型配分函数的经典可近似性，给出了一个基于谱宽与横场强度判据的多项式时间随机经典算法，并证明了超出该判据时近似问题是NP困难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子多体系统配分函数与Gibbs态的可计算性边界是量子复杂性理论的核心问题。横场Ising模型兼具相互作用与量子涨落，是研究经典与量子算法能力边界的理想模型，作者希望刻画其配分函数何时可被高效经典近似、何时不可近似。

Method: 作者围绕Hamiltonian H=½∑J_ij Z_iZ_j+∑h_i^z Z_i+η∑X_i展开分析。定义J的谱宽Δ(J)=λ_max(J)-λ_min(J)，以Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η≤1为判据。在该条件下构造随机化经典算法近似配分函数Z(β)=Tr(e^{-βH})，并进一步扩展到Gibbs态Pauli字符串期望值的估计；在判据失效时通过复杂性归约证明硬度。

Result: 当Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η≤1时，存在随机经典算法在关于n、β、模型参数及ε^{-1}的多项式时间内，将Z(β)近似到给定相对误差ε∈(0,1)。当Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η>1时，在exp(o(n))乘性因子内近似Z(β)是NP困难的。在可近似区间内，算法还能以任意小加性误差估计Gibbs态的Pauli字符串可观测量；当可观测量在X基下对角（P∈{I,X}^{⊗n}）时可达到任意小相对误差。

Conclusion: 论文给出了横场Ising模型配分函数可经典高效近似的精确阈值判据Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η≤1，并证明阈值之外近似问题为NP困难，从而在经典与量子算法能力之间划出清晰的复杂性边界，同时提供了估计Gibbs态可观测量的高效算法。

Abstract: We study the transverse field Ising model, defined by the Hamiltonian $H =\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i, j\in [n]} J_{ij} Z_i Z_j +\sum_{i=1}^n h_i^z Z_i + η\sum_{i} X_i$ where $J $ is the symmetric interaction matrix, and $η$ is the transverse field strength. Let $Δ(J)=λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)$ be the spectral width of $J.$ When the inverse temperature $β\geq0$ satisfies $Δ(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq1$, we give a randomized classical algorithm that approximates the partition function $Z(β)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})$ to a given relative error $ε\in(0,1)$ in time polynomial in $n$, $β$, the model parameters, and $ε^{-1}$. When $ Δ(J) \cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η > 1 ,$ we show that approximating $ Z(β)$ within an $\exp(o(n))$-multiplicative factor is $\textbf{NP}$-hard, and thus unlikely to admit an efficient classical or quantum algorithms under standard complexity theoretic assumptions. Furthermore, in the regime $Δ(J)\cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq 1,$ we provide an efficient randomized classical algorithm that approximates Pauli string observables of the Gibbs state $ ρ_β= \frac{e^{-βH}}{\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})}$ within an arbitrarily small additive error. In the special case when the observable is also diagonal in the $X$-basis, i.e. $P \in \{I, X\}^{\otimes n}$, the algorithm further achieves arbitrarily small relative error.

</details>


### [117] [Strong Simulation of 1D Quantum Circuits via Reduced Transition Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02082)
*Matilde Grassi,Stefano Carignano,Luca Tagliacozzo,Jacopo De Nardis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出扫掠RTM张量网络算法，通过联合压缩左右时间边界态，在有限相对精度下近似强模拟一维混沌砖墙线路的指定输出概率，发现所需键维数在可达时间窗口内呈亚指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态在混沌幺正线路下直接演化时，空间纠缠快速增长，严重限制张量网络模拟。本文转向更具针对性的任务——以有限相对精度近似强模拟指定输出概率，以绕开全局纠缠壁垒。

Method: 针对输入、输出比特串y与x，计算 p(x|y)=|⟨x|U(T)|y⟩|²。采用Sweeping RTM算法，一种基于约化转移矩阵(RTM)的横向张量网络收缩方法，联合压缩左、右时间边界态，并沿空间切割方向收敛输出概率，同时增大键维数。

Result: 对固定相对目标精度的一维混沌砖墙线路，数值证据表明：获得稳定估计所需的键维数在可达时间窗口内随演化时间呈亚指数增长。

Conclusion: 该工作为混沌量子线路的经典概率查询开辟了直接路径，并可潜在应用于基准测试与学习任务。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful tools for simulating quantum many-body systems, but the growth of spatial entanglement severely limits the direct evolution of pure states under chaotic unitary circuits. Here we consider a more targeted task: the approximate strong simulation of a specified output probability of a 1D chaotic brick-wall circuit at finite relative precision. Given input and output bit strings $\by$ and $\bx$, we evaluate $p(\bx|\by)=|\bra{\bx}U(T)\ket{\by}|^2$ using the Sweeping RTM algorithm, a transverse tensor-network contraction based on reduced transition matrices (RTMs). The algorithm compresses the left and right temporal boundary states jointly, seeking an output probability that converges across spatial cuts and as the bond dimension is increased. For chaotic one-dimensional brick-wall circuits at a fixed relative target precision, we find numerical evidence that the bond dimension required to obtain stable estimates grows subexponentially over the accessible time window. Our findings open a direct route to classical probability queries for chaotic quantum circuits, with potential applications to benchmarking and learning tasks.

</details>


### [118] [Stationarity as a One-Mode Constraint on Quantum Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02090)
*Itai Panas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证定态演化在能量标量性质上的"单一性"：在正则化Hartree-Fock框架中，引入单模排除公理，将正交于保留对模的协方差响应排除出终端标量能量，从而论证微观谱复杂性可与单模能量充分性相分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定态量子态即使内部响应跨越巨大希尔伯特空间，其时间演化仍只携带一个动力学相位因子和一个标量能量。作者的关切不在于绝对相位的数值或可观测性，而在于定态时间演化的"单一性"这一结构性质，并试图厘清微观谱复杂性与单模能量充分性之间的关系。

Method: 在正则化Hartree-Fock（reg-HF）中，先在未做任何单模约化之前建立与定态平均场的联系：高斯HF表示诱导的原积相对坐标高斯测度固定一个协方差，在该协方差处同时是最小Fisher信息分布、最大Shannon微分熵分布，并取Stam不等式的等号情形。库仑算符允许相容的高斯尺度分辨，位力补全将正则化对位移转换为定态相容响应。定态性导出对终端标量关联响应的单模约束，核心假设是单模排除公理。以氦原子作为最小非平凡检验，用无参数保留库仑载体与有限Z插入进行对比计算，并给出显式Bethe-Goldstone计算。

Result: 无参数保留库仑载体$-δ/e$与有限$Z$插入$8δ/9$密切再现了独立的类氦系数与能量；显式Bethe-Goldstone计算展示了宽广的非单分母返回谱，但其标量能量恰好是矩可压缩的。

Conclusion: 结果将微观谱复杂性与单模能量充分性区分开来：定态演化的标量能量只需单模响应即充分，而底层微观谱可保持高度复杂，二者并不矛盾。

Abstract: A stationary quantum state has one dynamical phase factor and one scalar energy even when its internal response spans a large Hilbert space. Our argument concerns not the value or observability of an absolute phase, but the one-ness of stationary time evolution. In regularized Hartree--Fock (reg-HF), the connection to the stationary mean field is established before any one-mode reduction: the primitive-product relative-coordinate Gaussian measure induced by the Gaussian HF representation fixes a covariance and, at that covariance, is simultaneously the minimum-Fisher-information and maximum-Shannon-differential-entropy distribution and the equality case of the Stam bound. The Coulomb operator admits a compatible Gaussian scale resolution, and virial completion converts the resulting regularized pair displacement into a stationary-compatible response. Stationarity motivates a one-mode constraint on the terminal scalar correlation response. The decisive reg-HF hypothesis is a one-mode exclusion axiom: covariance carried by response components orthogonal to the retained pair mode is excluded from the terminal scalar energy property, without asserting that the unrestricted response is one dimensional. Helium provides the minimal nontrivial test. The parameter-free retained Coulomb carrier $-δ/e$ and finite-$Z$ insertion $8δ/9$ closely reproduce independent He-like coefficients and energies, while an explicit Bethe--Goldstone calculation displays a broad non-single-denominator return spectrum even though its scalar energy is exactly moment-compressible. The result separates microscopic spectral complexity from one-mode energetic sufficiency.

</details>


### [119] [One-Way Quantum Symmetric Private Information Retrieval Protocol From A Single Database Server Using NISQ Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02093)
*Xiang Zou,H. F. Chau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于NISQ设备的单向对称私有信息检索（SPIR）协议，利用BB84态、无歧义测量与分块随机置换，在信息论安全下实现窃听者全能力时仍保密，服务器与用户仅需无长期量子存储的NISQ设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单服务器SPIR在无限制量子能力下信息论安全不可能；既有bounded/noisy-quantum-storage模型仍允许一定量子存储。作者希望给出更严格的存储限制模型，即双方均无长期量子存储且逐qubit即时测量，同时保证对全能力窃听者安全。

Method: 服务器单向发送BB84态；用户无歧义判别，获知随机子集比特。经联合随机置换分块后，用户公布其私选块的确定位置，服务器对每块这些位置做哈希并取随机奇偶校验。分块置换使同一位置集合匹配两块的概率指数小。仅需在BB84设备上增加一个含单辅助模的被动线性光学测量。证明含窃听者保密性、数据库隐私的显式尺寸规则、以及针对按规制备并诚实公布的服务器的用户隐私；扩展到decoy态弱相干脉冲。

Result: 对10^4比特数据库、数据库隐私10^-6，在容忍噪声信道下约千qubit的块即可；单光子下严格成立，含小多光子项。认证任意无记忆用户及承诺置换需更大辅助传输，用有限密钥量化。模拟显示隐私界近乎紧致。

Conclusion: 该工作表明在NISQ设备限制下（无长期量子存储、即时测量）可实现信息论安全的SPIR，且对全能力窃听者安全；仅需简单被动光学扩展，并可通过decoy态与有限密钥分析实用化。

Abstract: Symmetric private information retrieval (SPIR) lets an inquirer obtain one specific bit only, without leaking any information about its index to the database owner. With a single server, this is impossible for parties with unrestricted quantum power. We show that it becomes possible, with information-theoretic security, when the server and the user hold only noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices without long-term quantum memory and measure each qubit on arrival, while the eavesdropper remains all-powerful; this is a stricter form of the memory restriction of the bounded- and noisy-quantum-storage models. The server sends BB84 states one way; the user discriminates them unambiguously and so knows a random subset of the bits. After a joint random permutation into blocks, the user announces the conclusive locations of one privately chosen block, and the server hashes and takes a random parity of these locations in every block; the permutation makes it exponentially unlikely that one set of locations fits two blocks. We prove secrecy against the eavesdropper, database privacy through an explicit size rule for the announced set, and user privacy against a server who prepares the prescribed BB84 states and announces truthfully, exactly for single photons and up to a small multi-photon term over a tolerated noise channel. The only addition to BB84 equipment is a passive linear-optical measurement with one ancillary mode. For a $10^{4}$-bit database and database privacy $10^{-6}$, blocks of about a thousand qubits suffice over a tolerated noise channel for a user who applies the prescribed measurement; certifying an arbitrary memoryless user and committing to the permutation need larger auxiliary transmissions, which we quantify with finite keys. Database privacy extends to decoy-state weak coherent pulses, and simulations show that the bound is nearly tight.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum state preparation for weighted d-DNNF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02094)
*Steef Hegeman,Joon Hyung Lee,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于由加权 d-DNNF（确定性、可分解的伪布尔电路）描述的量子态，可以在线性时间内（忽略复数算术开销）获得生成该量子态的量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备问题要求给定量子态的描述后，高效生成计算该态的量子电路，但一般情形下这一任务在效率上存在困难，因此需要为特定结构化的态描述寻找可行且高效的构造方法。

Method: 以加权 d-DNNF（确定性、可分解伪布尔电路）作为量子态的描述形式，基于该电路结构构造对应的量子态制备电路，并分析其时间复杂度（至多相差复数算术开销）。

Result: 对加权 d-DNNF 描述的量子态，可在与描述规模呈线性时间内构造出计算该量子态的量子电路（忽略复数算术运算成本）。

Conclusion: 加权 d-DNNF 是一类支持量子态高效制备的描述形式，为量子态制备问题提供了线性时间的可行方案。

Abstract: The quantum state preparation problem is to, given a description of a quantum state, efficiently generate a quantum circuit computing the state. We show that for quantum states described by weighted d-DNNF (deterministic, decomposable pseudo-Boolean circuits) a quantum circuit computing the state can be obtained in linear time up to complex arithmetic.

</details>


### [121] [A No-Go Theorem for Order-Two Clifford Electric-Magnetic Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02097)
*Shunta Takahashi,Zhi Li,Beni Yoshida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明Z2 toric code中电-磁交换的局域保持Clifford实现不可能为二阶操作，并将结果推广到Z_N情形：奇数N存在二阶Clifford实现，偶数N不可能，揭示涌现电磁对偶的群律无需忠实提升到微观Clifford实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁对偶在任意子类型层面是二阶的，并对应Hadamard型逻辑变换。作者试图探究这种对偶能否在微观层面同样实现为二阶Clifford操作，即群律是否能忠实提升。

Method: 针对Z2 toric code，使用不依赖平移对称、不限定系统尺寸、不固定顶点-面元稳定子配对的全一般性证明，通过局部模拟电弦与磁弦不可避免的交叉（类比不可定向流形中的cross-cap）来论证。随后将问题推广至Z_N toric code进行分析。

Result: Z2 toric code中任何局域保持的Clifford电磁交换实现都不能是二阶的；对Z_N toric code，奇数N存在二阶Clifford实现，偶数N则不可能。

Conclusion: 涌现电磁对偶的群律不一定能忠实提升为其微观Clifford实现，二者在阶数结构上可以存在本质差异。

Abstract: Electromagnetic duality in the toric code has order two at the level of anyon types and acts as a Hadamard-type logical transformation on the encoded quantum information. Here, we ask whether it can likewise be realized microscopically as an order-two Clifford operation. For the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code, we prove that any locality-preserving Clifford realization of electric-magnetic exchange cannot have order two. Our proof is fully general and requires neither translation symmetry with restricted families of system sizes nor a fixed pairing between vertex and plaquette stabilizers. Geometrically, the proof locally emulates the unavoidable crossing between electric and magnetic strings, similar to a cross-cap in a non-orientable manifold. Extending the problem to the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ toric code, we find that an order-two Clifford realization exists for odd $N$, whereas it is impossible for even $N$. Our results show that the group law of an emergent electromagnetic duality need not lift faithfully to its microscopic Clifford realization.

</details>


### [122] [An exponential separation between entanglement-assisted and unassisted one-way quantum communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02099)
*Ryan Anselm,Srijita Kundu,Olivier Lalonde,Ashwin Nayak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子通信复杂度中长期悬而未决的问题：是否存在总布尔函数在纠缠辅助下只需少量通信、而无纠缠时需大量量子通信。作者给出一族总布尔函数的指数级分离：在单向模型下，有预共享纠缠时用 $O(\log n)$ 比特经典通信即可计算，但无纠缠时需 $Ω(n^{1/3})$ 量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信复杂度中一个长期问题是：纠缠能否在某类任务上大幅降低通信代价。此前仅在关系型问题、以及同时消息传递模型下的部分函数中已知此类分离，而对于总布尔函数是否存在这种分离仍未解决。

Method: 作者构造了一族总布尔函数 $f_n:\{0,1\}^n \times \{0,1\}^n \to \{0,1\}$，该函数是子群成员问题（subgroup membership problem）的特例，该问题最早由 Aaronson、Le Gall、Russell 和 Tani 在通信场景中研究。随后在单向通信模型下分析其纠缠辅助经典通信与无纠缠量子通信的复杂度下界与上界。

Result: 在有预共享纠缠时，该函数可用 $O(\log n)$ 比特的单向经典通信计算；而在无纠缠时，需要 $Ω(n^{1/3})$ 量子比特的单向量子通信。由此得到一个指数级分离。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为总布尔函数给出了纠缠辅助与无纠缠之间的指数级通信复杂度分离，解决了量子通信复杂度中的这一悬而未决问题，并表明子群成员问题是实现此类分离的有效工具。

Abstract: A longstanding question in quantum communication complexity is whether some task can be accomplished with a small amount of communication in the presence of entanglement, yet require much more quantum communication in the absence of entanglement. Separations of this nature were previously known for relational problems and, in the simultaneous message passing model, for partial functions. But it has remained unresolved whether any such separation exists for a total Boolean function. We resolve this question with an exponential separation in the one-way setting: we exhibit a family of total Boolean functions $f_n\colon \{0,1\}^n \times \{0,1\}^n \to \{0,1\}$ that can be computed with $O(\log n)$ bits of one-way classical communication given prior entanglement, but that require $Ω(n^{1/3})$ qubits of one-way quantum communication without entanglement. Our function is a special case of the subgroup membership problem, first studied in the communication setting by Aaronson, Le Gall, Russell, and Tani.

</details>


### [123] [On the pseudorandomness of simple quantum processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02100)
*Jesko Dujmovic,Jonas Haferkamp,Alexander Poremba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究Gowers关于局部随机可逆操作生成伪随机全局置换猜想在量子幺正情形下的类比，表明统计矩匹配（幺正设计）并不必然导致伪随机性，从而否定了HMMR猜想的幺正类比，并提出新的伪随机性涌现猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gowers (1996) 猜想重复组合局部随机可逆操作可产生与随机置换不可区分的全局置换，该猜想长期未解。作者转向其量子幺正类比，希望借助量子信息工具为这一经典问题提供新进展，同时厘清幺正设计与伪随机幺正之间的关系。

Method: 构造可高效采样的单比特与双比特门分布族，分析其经过T步复合后的n比特系综的统计矩性质；通过显式区分算法给出反例，并与需要高度结构化系综的更强分离构造进行对照。

Result: 第一，对任意固定t，存在可高效采样的门分布族，经T=O_t(n²log²n)步复合后得到的n比特系综是近似幺正t-设计，误差可忽略为exp(-Ω(log²n))，但存在高效量子算法仅用O_t(log²n)次查询即可将其与随机区分，否定了HMMR猜想的幺正类比。第二，给出幺正设计与伪随机幺正在多项式阶矩上更强的分离，但该反例需要高结构化的系综，与前述简单局部游走形成对比。

Conclusion: 幺正设计（即使阶数很高、误差极小）并不足以保证伪随机性，故用其建模黑洞物理中的信息扰乱需谨慎，因为最大扰乱系统仍可能保留可被高效实验探测的结构。作者据此提出关于伪随机性如何在随机量子电路等简单量子过程中涌现的新猜想。

Abstract: Can simple processes appear highly complex? Gowers (Comb. Prob. Comp. '96) conjectured that repeatedly composing local random reversible operations can yield global permutations that are indistinguishable from random. In this work, we study the unitary quantum analog of this question, in an attempt to make new progress on this longstanding conjecture.
  Our first result shows that statistical moment matching in the form of unitary designs does not generically lead to pseudorandomness---even for the simplest quantum processes: for every fixed $t$, we give an efficiently samplable family $\{ν_n\}_n$ of distributions on one- and two-qubit gates such that, after $T=O_t(n^2\log^2 n)$ independent steps, the resulting $n$-qubit ensemble is an approximate unitary $t$-design with negligible error $\exp(-Ω(\log^2 n))$, yet an efficient quantum algorithm distinguishes it from random using only $O_t(\log^2 n)$ queries. This refutes the unitary analog of the Hoory--Magen--Myers--Rackoff conjecture (ICALP '04) for permutations.
  Our second result is a stronger separation between unitary designs and pseudorandom unitaries at polynomially bounded moments; our counterexample, however, requires highly structured ensembles, in contrast with the simple local walks from before. This suggests caution when using unitary designs to model information scrambling in black-hole physics, as even maximally scrambled systems can exhibit structure which is accessible to efficient experiments. Motivated by these findings, we then propose new conjectures for how pseudorandomness can plausibly emerge within simple quantum processes, such as random quantum circuits.

</details>


### [124] [Time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02101)
*Fangqi Dong,Alex Lombardi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在随机预言机模型下证明了破解量子密码学的时间-空间下界近乎最优：对$n$量子比特二进制相位态，$T$次查询、$S$量子比特非均匀建议的敌手恢复随机密钥的概率至多为$O((T^2+\sqrt{ST})/N)$，优于后量子单向函数的$O((T^2+ST)/N)$。方法是将最优预处理攻击表示为随机矩阵的算子范数，用迹矩方法在随机预言机上求期望，并借助压缩预言机解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子单向函数的最佳已知界为$O((T^2+ST)/N)$，并在$S=N$时有平凡攻击。本文旨在探究量子密码学在预处理攻击下是否具有超越经典密码学的优势，即更少的通信量子比特能否抵御更大空间的预处理攻击。

Method: 将最优预处理攻击刻画为随机矩阵的算子范数，利用迹矩方法在随机预言机期望下对其进行界估计；这些迹矩借助压缩预言机（Zhandry, Crypto 2019）获得自然解释并加以分析。该方法可视为对Liu（Eurocrypt 2023）破解后量子密码学时间-空间权衡方法的简化与推广。

Result: 主要结果：$T$次查询、$S$量子比特非均匀建议的敌手从$n$量子比特二进制相位态恢复随机密钥的概率至多$O((T^2+\sqrt{ST})/N)$，$N=2^n$。附加结果：(1)收紧Liu对QROM中后量子PRG的分析，区分优势界为$O(T^2/N+\sqrt{ST/N})$；(2)对酉合成，将LMW24的单查询下界扩展到可进行一次任意函数查询并配合多项式多次自适应随机预言机查询（函数查询之前或之后）的敌手，并用压缩预言机解释原LMW24结果；(3)证明随机二进制相位态对空间$S$区分者的伪随机性紧界$O(\sqrt{S}/N)$。

Conclusion: 本文展示了量子密码学相对经典密码学的新优势：$n$量子比特通信即可在空间高达$N^2$（而非$N$）的预处理攻击下保持安全性。所提出的算子范数加迹矩（压缩预言机）框架简化并推广了既有时间-空间下界技术，并给出QROM后量子PRG、酉合成及二进制相位态伪随机性等多项改进结果。

Abstract: We prove near-optimal time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography in the random oracle model. Specifically, we show that a $T$-query adversary with $S$ qubits of non-uniform advice can recover a random key $k$ from the $n$-qubit binary phase state $|ψ_k\rangle \propto \sum_{x} R(k,x) |x\rangle$ with probability at most $O(\frac{T^2 + \sqrt{ST}}{N})$ for $N=2^n$. In contrast, the best known bound for post-quantum one-way functions is $O(\frac{T^2 + ST}{N})$, with a trivial attack at $S = N$. This demonstrates a new advantage of quantum cryptography over classical cryptography: $n$ qubits of communication suffice for security against preprocessing attacks with space up to $N^2$ rather than $N$. Our methodology is simple: express the optimal preprocessing attack as the operator norm of a random matrix, and bound this value in expectation over the random oracle via the trace-moment method. These trace moments have a natural interpretation using compressed oracles [Zhandry, Crypto 2019], which we then analyze. This can be viewed as a simplification and generalization of the approach of Liu [Eurocrypt 2023] for proving time-space tradeoffs for breaking post-quantum cryptography. We also prove the following results: (1) We tighten Liu's analysis of post-quantum PRGs in QROM, achieving a distinguishing advantage bound of $O(\frac{T^2}N + \sqrt{\frac{ST}N})$. (2) For unitary synthesis, we extend the one-query lower bound of Lombardi-Ma-Wright [STOC 2024] to hold against adversaries that can make one arbitrary function query along with polynomially many (adaptive) queries to the random oracle, either before or after the function query. This also interprets the original LMW24 result in terms of compressed oracles. (3) Finally, we prove a tight $O(\frac{\sqrt{S}}N)$ bound for the pseudorandomness of random binary phase states against space $S$ distinguishers.

</details>


### [125] [Unitary Schur Sampling of Qudits via Random SWAP Tests: Hunt for Antisymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02103)
*Shrigyan Brahmachari,David Jakab,Henry D. Pfister,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出仅用成对 SWAP 测试即可实现任意置换不变混合态的统一 Schur 采样，代价为 O(n·min{n,d}³·log²(n/ε)) 次随机 SWAP 测试，并应用于量子纯度放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于电路的 Schur 采样依赖相干表示论操作（如 Clebsch-Gordan 变换、广义相位估计、对称群上的量子傅里叶变换），实现复杂。作者希望找到更简单、仅依赖两量子比特/量子dit投影测量的实现方式。

Method: 核心思想是反复识别并提取最大的反对称子系统，将统一 Schur 采样转化为对反对称性的搜索，算法可解释为 Young 图的随机遍历。仅使用成对 SWAP 测试（即两量子dit在对称与反对称子空间上的投影测量），协议保持 SU(d) 不可约表示状态不变，同时在重数子系统中制备规范纯态。

Result: 在 diamond 距离下达到误差 ε，需 O(n·min{n,d}³·log²(n/ε)) 次随机 SWAP 测试；在量子纯度放大（QPA）应用中，最优纯化态可通过保留单个指定输出 qudit 并丢弃其余部分获得。

Conclusion: 统一 Schur 采样可完全由成对 SWAP 测试实现，无需相干表示论电路；该框架为置换不变信息提取提供了简洁高效的途径，并给出量子纯度放大的最优输出策略。

Abstract: Schur sampling is a fundamental primitive for extracting permutation-invariant information from many-body quantum systems and has broad applications in quantum information science. Circuit-based implementations typically rely on coherent representation-theoretic operations such as Clebsch-Gordan transforms, generalized phase estimation, or quantum Fourier transforms over the symmetric group. We show that unitary Schur sampling of arbitrary permutation-invariant mixed states on $n$ $d$-dimensional qudits can instead be implemented using only pairwise SWAP tests, namely, two-qudit projective measurements onto the symmetric and antisymmetric subspaces. Our protocol achieves error $ε$ in diamond distance using $O(n\min\{n,d\}^{3}\log^2(n/ε))$ random SWAP tests. The central idea is to repeatedly identify and extract the largest antisymmetric subsystem. This turns unitary Schur sampling into a search for antisymmetry and gives the algorithm a natural interpretation as a stochastic traversal of a Young diagram. The protocol preserves the $\mathrm{SU}(d)$-irrep state while preparing a canonical pure state in the multiplicity subsystem. As an application, we consider quantum purity amplification (QPA), in which multiple noisy copies of a pure quantum state are combined to produce a state of higher purity. We show that the optimal purified state can be obtained by retaining a single designated output qudit and discarding the rest.

</details>


### [126] [Quantum Advantage for Two-Party Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02113)
*Daniel Alabi,Emil T. Khabiboulline*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出信息论安全的量子双方汉明距离协议，在双方须输出相同估计的设定下，实现O(1)期望误差与O(n)通信量，突破经典信息论下Ω(√n)或Ω(√n/log n)的误差下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典场景下，信息论隐私协议在纯差分隐私下至少需要Ω(√n)误差，强近似差分隐私下至少需要Ω(√n/log n)误差，只有计算安全才能达到O(1)误差。作者希望探究量子通信能否在信息论层面直接实现O(1)精度，从而将量子信道视为一种隐私资源。

Method: 在Klauck的诚实、非抢占、消息保持模型中，构造O(n)通信量的量子协议，满足纯ε量子差分隐私（QDP），期望误差至多2/sinh ε + γ（任意γ>0）。核心构造包括带守卫的相干往返（guarded coherent round trip）与等格拉姆刚性原理（equal-Gram rigidity），防止诚实方保留与输入相关的互补信息。对近似(ε,δ) QDP，通过精确hockey-stick散度计算得到更小误差，并在δ=o(1/n)时保持O(1)与Ω(√n/log n)的分离。同时区分该模型与更弱的预设信道隐私（可被经典精确实现）以及完全抗保留安全（测量-中止攻击仍可行）。

Result: 量子协议在信息论意义下达到O(1)期望误差，与经典仅在计算假设下才能达到的精度相匹配；对近似QDP得到严格更小的误差；δ=o(1/n)时保持O(1)与Ω(√n/log n)的分离；证明预设信道隐私可经典精确实现，而完全抗保留安全无法抵御测量-中止攻击。

Conclusion: 量子通信可作为实现信息论O(1)误差汉明距离估计的资源，其关键在于保持非正交量子消息不被破坏，从而将“非正交消息的保持”确立为一种隐私资源。

Abstract: We introduce information-theoretically private quantum protocols for two-party Hamming distance when both parties must output the same estimate. Classically, for input length $n$, information-theoretic protocols require $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ error under pure differential privacy and $Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ error under strong approximate differential privacy, whereas computational security permits $O(1)$ error. In Klauck's honest, nonpreemptive, message-preserving model, we give an $O(n)$-communication quantum protocol with pure $\varepsilon$ quantum differential privacy (QDP) and expected error at most $\frac{2}{\sinh \varepsilon}+γ$, for every $γ>0$. For approximate $(\varepsilon, δ)$ QDP, an exact hockey-stick divergence calculation yields strictly smaller error, while preserving the $O(1)$-versus-$Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ separation for $δ=o(1/n)$. Thus, quantum communication achieves $O(1)$ information-theoretic error, matching the accuracy available classically only under computational assumptions.
  The main construction uses a guarded coherent round trip and an equal-Gram rigidity principle that prevents an honest player from retaining input-dependent complementary information. We separate this model from weaker prescribed-channel privacy, which already admits an exact classical realization, and from fully retention-robust security, against which measurement-and-abort attacks remain possible. Therefore, we identify preservation of non-orthogonal quantum messages as a resource for privacy.

</details>


### [127] [Local random quantum circuits converge to the Porter-Thomas distribution in polynomial depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02125)
*Aniruddha Sen,Nicholas Hunter-Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了多项式深度的砖墙随机电路输出分布以逆多项式速率在全变差距离下收敛到Porter-Thomas分布：对任意m≥0，深度为O(n^{2m+1}log n)的最近邻Haar随机门电路，其输出分布与Porter-Thomas分布的全变差距离不超过O(1/n^m)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Porter-Thomas统计是随机量子态及混沌多体系统输出分布的特征，在随机电路采样和量子优势实验演示中，低深度随机电路输出被认为近似服从Porter-Thomas分布，但此前缺乏严格的收敛性证明。

Method: 证明结合了三项工具：近似设计（approximate designs）给出的矩界、特征函数的解析估计，以及逆矩的局域反集中（local anticoncentration）性质。

Result: 对任意m≥0，由最近邻Haar随机门构造的深度为O(n^{2m+1}log n)的局部随机电路，其固定比特串上的输出概率分布与Porter-Thomas分布的全变差距离至多为O(1/n^m)。

Conclusion: 该工作首次严格证明了多项式深度砖墙随机电路输出分布在总变差距离下以逆多项式速率收敛到Porter-Thomas分布，为随机电路采样及量子优势实验中的Porter-Thomas假设提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Porter-Thomas statistics are a characteristic feature of the output distribution of random quantum states and, more broadly, chaotic quantum many-body systems. Convergence to Porter-Thomas plays a central role in random circuit sampling and experimental demonstrations of quantum advantage, where the output statistics of low-depth random quantum circuits are expected to be approximately Porter-Thomas, despite the absence of a rigorous proof of convergence. We show that the output distribution of polynomial-depth brickwork random circuits converges inverse-polynomially in total variation distance to the Porter-Thomas distribution. Specifically, consider the output probability distribution over a fixed bitstring of a local random quantum circuit, constructed from nearest-neighbor Haar random gates. Then, for any $m \geq 0$, the distribution corresponding to circuits of depth $O(n^{2m+1}\log(n))$ is at most $O(1/n^m)$ far in total variation distance from the Porter-Thomas distribution. Our proof uses moment bounds from approximate designs, analytic estimates for characteristic functions, and a local anticoncentration property for inverse moments.

</details>


### [128] [Spiking neural networks for streaming qubit readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02129)
*Barry M. Dillon,Aqib Javed,Jim Harkin,Patryk Dabkowski,Benjamin Lienhard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用脉冲神经网络(SNN)判别器实现超导量子比特读出，通过分块处理测量窗口实现流式、时间分辨的状态估计，精度超过匹配滤波并接近全迹ANN，同时借助量化感知训练与hls4ml综合实现FPGA实时低延迟推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器的反馈、校准与纠错需要快速准确的量子比特状态赋值；在超导平台中，频分复用读出本质上是多元任务，测量轨迹会包含串扰、弛豫事件等瞬态非理想因素，传统匹配滤波无法充分刻画，因此需要能利用时序结构且低延迟的判别方法。

Method: 引入脉冲神经网络(SNN)判别器：将测量窗口按连续时间块切分，网络逐块处理并随数据到达不断更新分类分数，而非等待读出窗口结束；同时采用量化感知训练和hls4ml综合，将模型部署到FPGA上。

Result: SNN性能优于匹配滤波判别，精度接近全迹人工神经网络；关键优势在于能随读出信号采集提供流式、时间分辨的量子比特状态估计；量化感知训练与hls4ml综合表明，每次FPGA推断更新可在下一个读出数据块到达前完成。

Conclusion: 脉冲神经网络为FPGA硬件上低延迟、实时的量子比特读出提供了有前景的途径，并对时间关键的量子控制与科学推断应用具有更广泛意义。

Abstract: Fast and accurate qubit-state assignment is essential for feedback, calibration, and error correction in quantum processors. In superconducting platforms, frequency-multiplexed readout makes this task intrinsically multivariate as measured traces can encode crosstalk, qubit-state relaxation events, and other transient nonidealities that are not fully captured by conventional matched filtering. Here, we introduce spiking neural network (SNN) discriminators for superconducting qubit readout. By processing the measurement window in successive time chunks, the networks exploit temporal structure and update classification scores as data arrive, rather than waiting until the end of the readout window. The spiking networks outperform matched-filter discrimination and approach the accuracy of a full-trace artificial neural network. Beyond reaching the performance of artificial neural networks, the key advantage of SNNs is that they provide a streaming, time-resolved estimate of the qubit state that evolves as the readout signal is acquired. Using quantisation-aware training and hls4ml synthesis, we further demonstrate that each FPGA inference update can be completed before the next readout chunk arrives. These results establish spiking neural networks as a promising route to low-latency, real-time qubit readout on FPGA hardware, with broader implications for time-critical quantum-control and scientific-inference applications.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum impurity models: easy at equilibrium, universal in motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02130)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sergey Bravyi,Anirban Chowdhury,Arkopal Dutt,Alexandru Gheorghiu,Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子杂质模型的平衡态性质可被经典算法高效近似，而时间演化则是BQP完全的，揭示了平衡与非平衡计算复杂度的鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型描述嵌入自由费米子大浴中的小型相互作用子系统，是凝聚态与量子场论中的重要模型。作者旨在刻画其基态能量、热平衡态及动力学性质的计算复杂度，以明确经典方法与量子计算在这些任务上的能力边界。

Method: 针对含n个费米子模式和常数大小杂质的哈密顿量H：(1) 经典算法近似基态能量；(2) 计算亥姆霍兹自由能及热场双态的经典描述；(3) 分析时间演化e^{-iHt}的复杂度。核心技术是利用按能标和Krylov深度组织的基底下多粒子浴激发的指数抑制。

Result: (1) 基态能量可在poly(n,1/ε)时间内以加性误差ε近似，优于此前最佳算法的准多项式时间；(2) 在逆温度β下，亥姆霍兹自由能与热场双态经典描述可在poly(n,β,1/ε)时间内以精度ε计算；(3) 对时间无关且杂质大小固定的H，模拟时间演化e^{-iHt}是BQP完全的。

Conclusion: 杂质模型的平衡性质可被经典高效近似，而时间演化可实现通用量子计算，构建出一个静止量子处理器，其程序以自由传播的费米子流形式到达。

Abstract: A quantum impurity model describes a small interacting subsystem embedded into a large bath of free fermions. Here we study the computational complexity of calculating the ground energy, thermal equilibrium, and dynamical properties of these models. Our work reveals a sharp contrast: equilibrium properties can be efficiently approximated by classical means, whereas time evolution can implement a universal quantum computation. More precisely, let $H$ be the Hamiltonian of an impurity model with $n$ fermionic modes and a constant-size impurity. We show that: (1) the ground energy of $H$ can be approximated to additive error $\varepsilon$ by a classical algorithm with runtime $\textsf{poly}(n,1/\varepsilon)$, improving on the quasi-polynomial runtime of the best previously known algorithm; (2) at inverse temperature $β$, the Helmholtz free energy and a classical description of the thermofield double state can be computed to precision $\varepsilon$ in time $\textsf{poly}(n,β,1/\varepsilon)$; (3) simulating the time evolution $e^{-iHt}$ is $\textsf{BQP}$-complete, for $H$ that is time-independent and has a fixed, constant impurity size. Our algorithms exploit exponential suppression of multi-particle bath excitations in a basis organized by energy scale and Krylov depth. Our universality construction realizes a stationary quantum processor whose program arrives in a stream of freely propagating fermions.

</details>


### [130] [Optimal transducers using symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02133)
*Benoît Dubus,Julien Ladeuze,Jérémie Roland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将表示论（对称群）方法引入Transducer框架，证明最优catalyst可取协变形式、transducer可取等型分解下的块对角形式，从而简化adversary SDP求解与酉算子构造，并据此导出搜索、振幅放大与振幅估计等原语的最优transducer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transducer是量子算法设计的有力框架：adversary SDP的可行点可直接转化为transducer，最优transduction复杂度等于adversary bound（即Las Vegas复杂度），且transducer可精确复合、避免复合时的误差控制开销。然而构造高效乃至最优的transducer仍需解adversary SDP并显式构造酉算子，难度较大。

Method: 利用状态转换问题的对称群：(1) 通过对称化论证证明最优catalyst总可选择为在对称群表示下协变；(2) 证明transducer纠缠（intertwines）两个不同的群表示，因而可在Hilbert空间的等型分解（isotypic decomposition）中取块对角形式。将Høyer-Lee-Špalek（2007）与Ambainis-Magnin-Roetteler-Roland（2011）用表示论计算adversary下界的工作扩展到最优算法的系统构造。

Result: 对多个广泛使用的量子算法原语（非结构化搜索、振幅放大、振幅估计）导出了最优transducer及其最优常数。

Conclusion: 表示论方法可同时简化最优transducer构造中的两步——adversary SDP求解与显式酉算子构造，为系统性地设计最优量子算法提供了通用途径。

Abstract: Transducers (Belovs, Jeffery and Yolcu, 2024) are a quantum computing framework describing a quantum algorithm as a unitary converting an input state into a target state using a catalyst, an auxiliary vector that is left unchanged. They are a powerful tool in quantum algorithm design, especially in the context of quantum query complexity: feasible points of the (dual) adversary semidefinite program directly translate into transducers and the optimal transduction complexity is equal to the adversary bound, i.e. the Las Vegas complexity, which is known to characterize bounded-error quantum query complexity. Moreover, contrary to bounded-error algorithms, transducers compose exactly, which limits overheads due to controlling errors in algorithms constructed by composition. Constructing efficient, let alone optimal, transducers in terms of quantum query complexity nevertheless remains a hard task since it still requires solving the adversary SDP and constructing the unitary to obtain an explicit algorithm. In this paper, we show how using the symmetry group of state-conversion problems simplifies both steps. First, using a symmetrization argument, we prove an optimal catalyst can always be chosen covariant under a representation of the symmetry group. Second, we prove that the transducer intertwines two different representations of the group and can thus be chosen block diagonal in the isotypic decomposition of the Hilbert space. Using those methods, we then derive optimal transducers, with optimal constants, for different widely used quantum algorithmic primitives, such as unstructured search, amplitude amplification and amplitude estimation. Our approach extends previous work on the use of representation theory to compute adversary lower bounds (Høyer, Lee, and {\v S}palek, 2007; Ambainis, Magnin, Roetteler and Roland, 2011) to the systematic construction of optimal algorithms.

</details>


### [131] [A Generalized quantum Stein lemma on von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02134)
*Li Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了任意von Neumann代数上、针对凸且张量稳定族的i.i.d.正态态广义量子Stein引理：在存在相对熵有限的备择态时，最坏情况II型错误指数由正则化相对熵给出，并具有强逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典量子Stein引理多局限于特定态族或有限维情形，需将结果推广到任意von Neumann代数上的凸、张量稳定族，以刻画最坏情况下的假设检验极限。

Method: 结合相对熵的hockey-stick散度积分表示、模检验界（modular testing bound）与凸极小极大论证。

Result: 在任意I型错误容忍度ε∈(0,1)下，最坏情况II型错误指数等于正则化相对熵，且满足强逆定理。

Conclusion: 在一般代数框架下建立了广义量子Stein引理，统一并推广了已有结果。

Abstract: We prove a generalized quantum Stein lemma for i.i.d. normal states against convex, tensor-stable families on arbitrary von Neumann algebras. Assuming the existence of an alternative state with finite relative entropy from the null state, we show that, at every type-I error tolerance $\varepsilon\in (0,1)$, the worst case type-II error exponent is achieved with the regularized relative entropy with a strong converse. The proof combines the integral representation of relative entropy by hockey-stick divergences, a modular testing bound, and convex minimax argument.

</details>


### [132] [Random Quantum Circuits Beyond Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02135)
*Shih-Han Hung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定量刻画了强 ε-近似酉 k-设计对单个输出概率分布的复现精度：Kolmogorov 距离界为 O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε) 且常数因子内最优；若设计在 log k+O(1) 量子比特的酉平移下不变，则依赖 k 的误差指数改善，并给出全变差界。作为应用，输出分布期望 Shannon 熵与 Haar 值之差为 O(√η)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路旨在高效复现理想随机量子演化的统计性质，近似酉设计通过与 Haar 随机酉算子的矩匹配来实现。然而，矩匹配能在多大程度上保证输出概率的完整分布精度尚缺乏定量刻画，本文即针对这一问题展开研究。

Method: 利用 Kolmogorov 距离和全变差距离作为度量，分析强 ε-近似酉 k-设计下单个输出概率分布与有限维 Porter-Thomas 分布（参数为 1 和 2^n-1 的 beta 分布）的偏差；并引入局部不变性（在作用于 log k+O(1) 量子比特的酉平移下不变）来改进估计，同时推导该精度对期望 Shannon 熵的影响。

Result: 对 n 量子比特上的每个强 ε-近似酉 k-设计，单个输出概率分布与 Porter-Thomas 分布的 Kolmogorov 距离为 O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε)，该界在常数因子意义下最优；若设计具有局部不变性，则依赖 k 的误差指数改善：Kolmogorov 距离误差为 2^{-Ω(k)}+O(ε)，全变差距离误差为 2^{-Ω(k)}+O(εlog(2/ε))。此外，若边际分布与 Haar 对应值的 Kolmogorov 距离为 η，则期望 Shannon 熵之差为 O(√η)。

Conclusion: 本文明确了矩匹配所保证的分布精度极限，并指出局部不变性是显著提升该精度的额外结构；同时给出输出分布期望 Shannon 熵的误差估计，为随机量子电路的设计与评估提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Random quantum circuits aim to efficiently reproduce the statistical properties of ideal random quantum evolution. One approach is to construct approximate unitary designs, which match the moments of Haar-random unitaries up to a prescribed order with controlled error. In this work, we establish quantitative guarantees for how accurately these designs reproduce the full distributions of individual output probabilities. We show that, for every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design on $n$ qubits, the distribution of each individual output probability is within $O\left(\sqrt{\frac{2^n}{k(k+2^n)}}+\varepsilon\right)$ in Kolmogorov distance of the finite-dimensional Porter-Thomas distribution, a beta distribution with parameters $1$ and $2^n-1$. This bound is optimal up to constant factors, implying that a substantially better uniform bound requires additional structure. We further show that local invariance yields an exponential improvement in the dependence on $k$, with guarantees also in the stronger metric of total variation: every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design invariant under unitary translations acting on $\log k+O(1)$ qubits achieves error $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$ in Kolmogorov distance and $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon\log(2/\varepsilon))$ in total variation distance. These results identify both the distributional accuracy guaranteed by moment matching and the additional structure that substantially improves it. As an application, we show that if the marginal distribution of each output probability of a strong approximate design is within Kolmogorov distance $η$ of its Haar counterpart, then the expected Shannon entropy of the output distribution differs from its Haar value by $O(\sqrtη)$.

</details>


### [133] [One-Shot any Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02137)
*Andrew C. Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意单次编码均为自纠错，并给出将任意[[n,k]] CSS QLDPC码转换为具有单次量子纠错和高效局部解码器的[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码的一般构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子低密度奇偶校验（QLDPC）码在容错量子计算中具有重要价值，但如何使其具备单次纠错能力并获得高效局部解码，同时提升错误抑制能力，是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 首先给出任意单次编码自纠错的简单证明；然后将任意[[n,k]] CSS QLDPC码变换为[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码，使其具备单次量子纠错和运行于O(log m)并行时间的局部解码器；在联合局部随机物理与测量噪声下分析阈值与错误抑制性能。

Result: 在恒定阈值p_RG>0以下，T轮纠错后的逻辑失败概率上界为O(Tn)exp[-Ω(m^α)]（α>0为常数），即阈值为p_RG、错误抑制为Ω(m^α)。若输入码本身具备阈值p_c>0和错误抑制Ω(n^β)的单次解码器，则输出码将错误抑制增强至Ω(m^α n^β)，同时保持与解码器无关的阈值p_RG。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了单次编码的自纠错性质，并提供了将一般CSS QLDPC码升级为单次纠错码的通用框架，能在保持阈值的同时实现错误抑制的乘法增强（当p_c < p_RG时为增强效果）。

Abstract: We first provide a simple proof showing that any single-shot code is self-correcting. We then show that any $[[n,k]]$ CSS quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) code can be transformed into an $[[nm,k]]$ CSS QLDPC code with single-shot (SS) quantum error correction and an efficient local decoder which runs in $O(\log m)$ parallel time. Below a constant threshold $p_{\rm RG}>0$ for joint local stochastic physical and measurement noise, the logical failure probability over $T$ correction rounds is bounded by \begin{equation*}
  O(T n)\exp[-Ω(m^α)] \end{equation*} for a constant $α>0$; we say that the \textit{threshold} is $p_{\rm RG}$ and \textit{error suppression} is $Ω(m^α)$. Moreover, if the input code is equipped with a single-shot decoder with threshold $p_c>0$ and error suppression $Ω(n^β)$, then the output code \textit{enhances} the error suppression to $Ω(m^α n^β)$, while maintaining the decoder-independent threshold $p_{\rm RG}$, and thus \textit{enhanced} if $p_c < p_{\rm RG}$.

</details>


### [134] [A provable quantum advantage for approximate optimization via decoded quantum interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02145)
*Maximilian J. Kramer,Elies Gil-Fuster,Benjamin D. M. Jones,Jens Eisert,Franz J. Schreiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在预言机模型下证明了Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI)对近似优化问题的严格量子优势：针对folded OPI任务，DQI及其改进算法可达到比任意多项式时间经典算法更高的近似比，且在码率0.3时分别取得约0.85和0.95的期望得分，而经典算法需超多项式查询才能超越0.65阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
