Motivation: DQI作为一种具有强性能保证、利用优化与编码理论对偶性的量子近似优化新范式，其核心悬而未决的问题是：它能否可证明地超越所有多项式时间经典算法。此前缺乏此类严格优势的理论证据，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在预言机设定下构造folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI)优化任务，其接受集随机选取并通过成员预言机访问。证明基于Jordan等人的DQI近似优化框架，将Yamakawa-Zhandry精确搜索预言机分离所依赖的经典下界方法推广至近似情形；并借鉴Sun-Wootters、Horinaga-Yamakawa及Jo的近期工作，构造改进版DQI算法以获得更大分离。

Result: 对folded OPI任务，证明了任意多项式时间经典算法与DQI算法可达近似比之间的严格差距；改进版DQI算法进一步实现更大量子分离。以码率0.3为例，DQI与改进算法期望得分分别约为0.85和0.95，而经典算法若要以常数概率超过0.65阈值任意固定量，需超多项式数量的成员查询。

Conclusion: 本文首次在预言机模型下给出DQI相对所有多项式时间经典算法的可证明近似优势，验证了DQI在近似优化中的量子优越性，并通过改进算法强化了该分离，为量子近似优化的理论优势提供了坚实依据。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a novel paradigm for tackling approximate optimization problems on quantum computers. This framework comes with strong performance guarantees and exploits a well-established duality between optimization and coding theory. A central question, however, is whether DQI can actually provably outperform all polynomial-time classical algorithms. In this work, we establish such an advantage in an oracle setting: we consider an optimization task called folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI), where the acceptance sets are chosen randomly and accessed through membership oracles. We establish a strict gap between the approximation ratio achievable by any polynomial-time classical algorithm and the approximation ratio achieved by the DQI algorithm. Our proof builds on Jordan et al.'s DQI framework for approximate optimization and extends the classical lower-bound method underlying Yamakawa and Zhandry's exact-search oracle separation to approximation. Building on recent developments by Sun and Wootters, Horinaga and Yamakawa, and Jo, we further show that a modified version of the DQI algorithm achieves a strictly larger gap on the folded OPI problem, yielding an even stronger quantum separation. As a concrete example, for code rate $0.3$, DQI and the modified algorithm achieve expected scores of approximately $0.85$ and $0.95$, respectively. In contrast, exceeding the classical threshold of $0.65$ by any fixed amount with constant probability on sampled instances requires super-polynomially many classical membership queries.

</details>


### [135] [Polynomial-time additive-error estimation of output probabilities for shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02146)
*Matthew Coudron,Michael J. Gullans,Jon Nelson,Joel Rajakumar,Shi Jie Samuel Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文给出了一个确定性经典算法，可在 poly(n, 1/ε) 时间内以加性误差 ε 估计常深度量子电路输出概率 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|^2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对该任务的经典模拟算法时间复杂度较高（如 n^{O(log n)}，几何局域情形为 n^{O(log log n)}，2D 几何局域电路为 n^{O(1)}），作者希望将时间复杂度降低到多项式级别，从而提升对常深度量子电路的经典模拟效率。

Method: 提出一种确定性经典算法，用于估计由有界扇入门和任意连通性构成的常深度量子电路 U 在给定输出串 x 上的概率幅平方 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|^2，目标为加性误差 ε。

Result: 算法在 poly(n, 1/ε) 时间内完成概率估计，达到加性误差 ε，改进了此前 n^{O(log n)}、几何局域情形 n^{O(log log n)} 以及 2D 几何局域电路 n^{O(1)} 的复杂度。

Conclusion: 常深度量子电路的输出概率可在多项式时间内被经典确定性算法高效估计，表明该类电路的经典可模拟性比先前认识更强。

Abstract: We give a deterministic classical algorithm that estimates $|\langle x|U|0^n\rangle|^2$ to additive error $\varepsilon$ in $\mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$ time, where $U$ is a constant-depth quantum circuit comprised of gates with bounded fan-in and arbitrary connectivity, and $x$ is an arbitrary $n$-bit output string. This improves over prior state-of-the-art algorithms that takes $n^{O(log(n))}$ time for the same task, $n^{O(log(log(n))}$ when $U$ is geometrically local, and $n^{O(1)}$ for 2D geometrically-local circuits.

</details>


### [136] [The Robustness of QAC0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02154)
*Daniel Grier,Jackson Morris,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究QAC^0电路对容错和门集合修改的鲁棒性：一方面消除并行W-test的固有误差，实现精确QAC^0模拟TC^0及计算超出AC^0[p]的布尔函数；另一方面证明QAC^0仅用广义Toffoli、S和Hadamard门即可高效近似实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAC^0通常允许非零误差，且使用任意单量子比特门。作者希望探究这些宽松条件是否必要：误差能否被消除，计算能力是否依赖于任意单量子比特门的可用性。

Method: 1) 通过在多副本情形下新颖地应用精确振幅放大，消除Grier-Morris-Wu并行W-test中的固有误差；2) 分析QAC^0在受限门集合下的表达能力，构造由广义Toffoli、S和Hadamard门组成的近似电路。

Result: QAC^0可以精确模拟TC^0（使用多项式多份经典输入副本）；对每个固定素数p，精确QAC^0（EQAC^0）能计算AC^0[p]之外的总布尔函数。此外，任意QAC^0电路都可被仅含广义Toffoli、S、Hadamard门的QAC^0电路近似，且近似电路可由原电路的经典描述高效构造。

Conclusion: QAC^0对误差和门集合限制具有鲁棒性：非零误差并非必需，且计算能力不依赖于任意单量子比特门的使用，受限门集合足以高效近似实现全部QAC^0电路。

Abstract: In this work we study the robustness of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ with respect to error tolerance and modifications to its gate-set. First, we investigate whether the non-zero error typically allowed for $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits computing Boolean functions is truly necessary. We show that the error inherent in the parallel $W$-test of \cite{grier_morris_wu} can be eliminated entirely via a novel application of exact amplitude amplification in the many-copies context. Consequently, we find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ can \textit{exactly} simulate $\mathsf{TC}^0$ with polynomially many copies of the classical input and that for every fixed prime $p$ exact $\mathsf{QAC}^0$, $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$, can compute total Boolean functions outside of $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$.
  Second, we ask to what extent the computational power of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ follows from the fact that arbitrary single-qubit gates may be used at any point in the circuit. We find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ is in fact robust to restrictions on which single-qubit gates are permitted: every $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit can be approximately implemented by a $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit consisting of just generalized Toffoli, $S$, and Hadamard gates. Moreover, this approximating circuit can be constructed efficiently from a classical description of the original circuit.

</details>


### [137] [Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians Using a Local Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02157)
*Suying Liu,Zitai Xu,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Xiaodi Wu,Yu-Xin Wang,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出仅用单个可测量量子比特，借助快速单比特控制与连通参考骨干，即可在Heisenberg极限下学习N量子比特有界度两体哈密顿量的全部O(N)个独立参数，并给出链上总查询时间Õ(N)的并行学习架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习协议通常假设能够直接测量整个系统，这在可扩展性和实验实现上构成限制。论文旨在突破这一全局测量访问假设，探索仅通过局部测量接口学习大量多体哈密顿量参数的可行性与极限。

Method: 利用快速单比特控制与连接的参考骨干，构造由量子信号处理合成的鲁棒SWAP门，将处于远处哈密顿量参数下演化的量子态相干转移到可测量量子比特，且无需对中间链路的哈密顿量参数进行预先标定；进一步设计并行学习架构。

Result: 在N量子比特链上，协议以总查询时间Õ(N)实现并行学习，并保持Heisenberg极限的精度标度；这些标度在对数因子内匹配基础精度与信息传播下界。框架可推广至任意有界度相互作用图。

Conclusion: 研究建立了通过单一局部测量接口学习大量多体哈密顿量参数的可扩展路径，表明局部测量界面足以支持对广泛的哈密顿量参数进行高效、高精度学习。

Abstract: Hamiltonian learning provides a systematic framework for reconstructing unknown quantum dynamics. However, existing protocols typically assume direct measurement access to the entire system. With fast single-qubit control and a connected reference backbone, we show that a single measurable qubit suffices to learn all $O(N)$ independent parameters of a bounded-degree two-body Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits at the Heisenberg limit. Crucially, our protocol uses robust SWAP gates synthesized by quantum signal processing, enabling coherent transfer of states evolving under distant Hamiltonian parameters to the measurable qubit. This transfer requires no prior calibration of the Hamiltonian parameters of the intermediate links. A parallel learning architecture achieves total query time $\widetilde{O}(N)$ for an $N$-qubit chain, while retaining Heisenberg-limited precision scaling. On the chain, these scalings match the fundamental precision and information-propagation lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. The framework further extends to arbitrary bounded-degree interaction graphs. Our results establish a scalable route to learning an extensive number of many-body Hamiltonian parameters through only a local measurement interface.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond Light Cones: State Preparation Complexity in Quantum Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02166)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik,Bobak T Kiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于有效profile复杂度的稠密量子p-spin哈密顿量态制备复杂度分析方法，证明近基态能量需Ω(n²/log n)个一/二比特门，并给出深度-宽度权衡、纠缠深度与MPS键维下界及magic层级阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅基于电路光锥的界无法刻画稠密p-spin系统的态制备复杂度，需要超越光锥限制的新度量来给出更强的量子电路下界。

Method: 引入有效profile复杂度（由Pauli profile的度量熵导出），结合Berta等非对称量子de Finetti定理的改编与高斯过程熵界进行分析。

Result: 均匀有界二次有效profile复杂度的类与基态能量保持正比于√n的正间隙；亚二次复杂度类在领头阶无法超越乘积态基准。近基态能量需Ω(n²/log n)个门；并得到深度-宽度权衡、纠缠深度与MPS键维下界、各固定magic层级阻碍；Clifford+T电路在o(n)个T门时相对乘积稳定子态无领头阶能量优势。

Conclusion: 有效profile复杂度是刻画稠密量子多体态制备复杂度的有力工具，可推广至含Clifford层、可丢弃辅助比特等场景并给出严格的量子电路下界。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying state preparation complexity in dense quantum $p$-spin Hamiltonians on $n$ qubits, going beyond bounds based only on circuit lightcones. The key input is the class's effective profile complexity, which is derived from the metric entropy of its Pauli profiles. These profiles record expectations of all Pauli operators supported on exactly $p$ qubits. Classes with uniformly bounded quadratic effective profile complexity remain separated from the ground-state energy by a positive multiple of $\sqrt n$ for sufficiently large fixed $p$. At subquadratic effective profile complexity, the class cannot outperform a suitable benchmark class at leading order, with product states providing a universal benchmark. The proof combines an adaptation of a nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem of Berta et al. (arXiv:1810.12197) with Gaussian process entropy bounds.
  Applying this framework, we show that attaining near-ground-state energy requires $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ one- and two-qubit gates, even with arbitrary discardable ancillas. We also obtain depth-width tradeoffs, entanglement-depth and matrix product state bond-dimension lower bounds, and obstructions for both orientations at every fixed level of Parham's magic hierarchy (arXiv:2504.19966), with total circuit width $O(n)$. In first-level reverse magic, a shallow circuit is followed by an unrestricted Clifford circuit. The latter can spread local observables across the system, preventing a direct application of small-lightcone bounds. For this first-level class, our bounds also allow arbitrarily many clean ancillas at fixed shallow-circuit depth. A sharper benchmark shows that Clifford+$T$ circuits with $o(n)$ $T$-gates have no leading-order energy advantage over product stabilizer states, even with unrestricted Clifford operations and arbitrary discardable ancillas.

</details>


### [139] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明置换不变性使有限维无记忆信道的每用优化相干信息收敛于单次使用最大值，从而将渐近量子容量优化归结为单信道使用问题，并给出输出熵与完全有界范数的统一变分框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨信道使用的关联可提升量子通信速率，使得量子容量需要在大块长度上优化，其渐近行为难以确定。作者希望厘清置换不变输入能否限制这种超加性优势，并寻找控制量子容量及相关判据量的统一结构。

Method: 利用完全置换不变性、de Finetti归约与置换协方差，对有限维无记忆信道的相干信息、纯源纠缠生成保真度、完全有界范数及夹层Rényi信道量建立共享的变分框架；并分析置换不变输入下输出熵的每用下界。

Result: 每用优化相干信息收敛于单次使用最大值，并在有限块长达到其上确界；有限块超加性仍可能，但渐近优化退化为单次使用。对置换不变输入的纯源纠缠生成，速率超过单次相干信息最大值时保真度趋于零。完全有界范数与夹层Rényi信道量的乘性和加性结果可由该框架系统重证。Rényi阶大于1及von Neumann熵下，输出熵每用无法低于单次最小值。

Conclusion: 置换对称性将量子容量与熵优化的渐近问题有效约化到单次信道使用，同时为完全有界范数和夹层Rényi信道量提供统一变分证明，并把对称性的作用从通信界扩展到输出熵控制。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [140] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究在全局酉算符U掩盖下的哈密顿量学习问题，证明其DQC1-hard，并提出SPICES算法高效恢复单粒子谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料建模中，均值场理论、格林函数和密度泛函理论等经典方法依赖单粒子谱。然而当我们只能接触被全局酉算符掩盖的非相互作用结构时，如何恢复其权重E_i成为挑战。作者希望在不重构U的前提下恢复谱信息，为量子材料模拟提供实用路径。

Method: 论文首先将问题形式化为学习H=U†(∑E_i n_i + H_O)U中非相互作用模式的权重E_i，其中非相互作用结构被未知全局酉算符U隐藏。随后证明该问题DQC1-hard。接着提出SPICES（single-particle inference via contour estimators），将Hadamard测试与新颖的经典后处理相结合。该方法利用单粒子能量与复逆温度下配分函数零点所对应的傅里叶频率之间的对应关系。

Result: 证明该哈密顿量学习问题即使具有非相互作用结构仍然DQC1-hard。SPICES在一定的自然假设下能够以Wasserstein距离高效恢复谱分布。

Conclusion: 由于准粒子谱在下游材料计算中普遍存在，且SPICES简单实用，作者认为SPICES是通往材料模拟量子应用的一条有前景的路线。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Exact Material Circulation Surface in an Unsteady Viscous Vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00082)
*Khalid M. Saqr*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文为含源非定常 Kiknadze–Krasnov 涡旋导出了一个精确的物质输运（材料）曲面，该曲面同时是拉格朗日径向分界面、峰值涡量轨迹和零环量传递边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为含源非定常涡旋寻找一个精确的物质（材料）曲面，从而将拉格朗日分界、涡量峰值位置与环量输运边界统一于同一曲面，并为数值模拟中的材料面追踪与环量输运误差提供精确基准。

Method: 从不可压缩 Navier–Stokes 方程出发，对单分布模态（s&gt;1）分析缩放半径 x=βr² 的随体演化，推导其满足 Dx/Dt=4νβ[(s−1)−x]；由此证明 x=s−1 为材料柱面，并解析求解完整速度场、压力场、参数许可范围、多模态限制及径向尺度动力学。

Result: x=s−1 对任意可容许的时变应变均构成材料柱面，且该柱面内含最大分布涡量；其包围环量 ΓP(s,s−1) 保持常数，因为该处黏性环量传递项 2πνr ∂rωz 恒为零。已知的定常源–应变驻点柱面作为极限情形被重新得到。

Conclusion: 单一运动曲面同时充当拉格朗日径向分界、峰值涡量轨迹与零传递环量边界；该精确曲面可作为评估材料面追踪与给定应变下环量输运数值误差的基准。

Abstract: An exact material circulation surface is derived for the source-bearing unsteady Kiknadze--Krasnov vortex. For a single distributed mode with $s>1$, the scaled radius $x=βr^2$ obeys $\frac{\mathrm D x}{\mathrm D t}=4νβ\big[(s-1)-x\big],$ so $x=s-1$ defines a material cylinder for any admissible time-dependent strain. The same cylinder contains the maximum distributed vorticity. Its enclosed circulation, $ΓP(s,s-1)$, is constant because the viscous circulation transfer $2πνr\,\partial_rω_z$ vanishes identically there. Thus one moving surface simultaneously provides a Lagrangian radial partition, a peak-vorticity locus, and a zero-transfer circulation boundary. The known steady source--strain stagnation cylinder is recovered as a limiting case. The full velocity and pressure fields, admissible parameter ranges, multimode restrictions, and radial-scale dynamics are also derived and verified directly from the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. The surface can be used as an exact benchmark for assessing numerical errors in material-surface tracking and circulation transport under prescribed strain.

</details>


### [142] [Fluid drags on a sphere at finite Reynolds number--a classical problem examined afresh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00334)
*James Q Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文评估了Klyachko（1934）阻力系数公式的精度，证明其在Re<1000内合理准确，并可分段延拓至Re<3e5，且经微调后可显著改进，从而支持气溶胶球体直线运动的闭式积分与终端速度计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球形颗粒在有限雷诺数下的阻力系数Cd缺乏闭式数学解，现有公式只能通过实验数据拟合得到，导致公式繁杂、拟合函数各异、拟合参数不断增多。作者希望寻找一个形式简洁（仅含两个分数拟合参数）、能实现气溶胶球体直线运动方程闭式积分的Cd公式。

Method: 以Klyachko 1934年提出的含两个分数拟合参数的Cd公式为对象，计算其相对均方根误差，并与近期发展的多种公式进行对比；同时将该公式作为分段连续函数延拓至Re<3e5，并尝试轻微修正以提升精度；最后通过直线加速运动、气溶胶球体终端速度等示例进行演示验证。

Result: 由近期公式计算的相对均方根误差表明，Klyachko公式在Re<1000范围内具有合理精度，延拓为分段连续函数后在Re<3e5内同样适用；经轻微修正后精度可显著提升。基于延拓后的Klyachko公式的气溶胶球体加速与终端速度算例均得到有效计算。

Conclusion: Klyachko公式因其简洁的分数参数形式与良好精度，可作为研究有限雷诺数下气溶胶球体直线运动的有效工具，为这一经典问题提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: Evaluating the drag coefficient Cd for a sphere moving in a fluid at finite Reynolds number Re has become a classical research subject in fluid dynamics, with publications appearing continuously by numerous authors over the years due to the unavailability of closed-form mathematical solutions. To date, mathematical formulas for calculating Cd as a function of Re can only be obtained by correlation curve fitting to experimental data. The consequence is the coexistence of various formulas with different mathematical fitting functions and an increasing number of fitting parameters. The present study focuses on examining the accuracy of Klyachko's 1934 formula for Cd with two common fractions as fitting parameters, which could enable closed form integration of a simplified equation governing rectilinear motion of aerosol spheres. The computed values of root-mean-square relative error from recently developed formulas show reasonable accuracy of the Klyachko formula for Re less than 1000 and its extension to Re less than 3e5 as a piecewise continuous function, with the possibility of significant improvement through a slight modification. Hence, a few demonstrative examples are presented using the extended Klyachko formula in the study of rectilinear acceleration, the calculation of the terminal velocity of aerosol spheres, and so forth, to shed fresh light on how this classical problem can be studied.

</details>


### [143] [On the spectra of strong turbulence on water surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00777)
*Gregory Falkovich*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文回顾了水表面重力波直接级联谱理论的发展，指出弱湍流理论适用于长波，而短波区因波陡增大出现破碎，需考虑强湍流。作者重新估算了Phillips与Kuznetsov提出的破碎波谱，认为频率谱斜率应由Kuznetsov给出且在整个级联中保持不变，并论证弱-强湍流转捩由谱非局域相互作用导致，发生尺度比量纲估计更大，强湍流谱幅值非普适而依赖泵浦条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水表面重力波能量直接级联从长波延伸到短波，跨越弱湍流与破碎（强湍流）两种机制。既有理论中Phillips的破碎波谱幅值与频率谱形式存在错误，且弱-强湍流转捩尺度与强湍流谱普适性尚不清晰，因此需要澄清谱斜率、幅值及转捩机制。

Method: 以Zakharov学派弱湍流理论描述长波级联；在短波区引入表面陡度增长与破碎事件，分析Phillips的波数谱和频率谱，并采用Kuznetsov提出的破碎波频率谱斜率；估算频率谱幅值，结合谱非局域相互作用分析弱-强湍流转捩。

Result: Phillips的波数谱幅值有误，其频率谱整体形式不正确；Kuznetsov给出的破碎波频率谱斜率正确，且该频率谱在整个级联中预计保持不变。弱-强湍流转捩由谱非局域相互作用引起，发生尺度比量纲估计更大（更早于短波端）；强湍流谱幅值非普适，在足够长的弱湍流级联下依赖泵浦强度。

Conclusion: 水表面重力波直接级联的频率谱在整个级联中保持由Kuznetsov给出的斜率不变；弱-强湍流转捩因谱非局域性而提前发生，强湍流谱幅值不具普适性而受泵浦影响，非局域性很可能源于短波破碎幅度由其所骑行的最长波决定。

Abstract: Here, I give a brief history of the theory, with twists and turns, and list possible predictions for the spectra of the direct cascade of gravity waves on the water. The description of long waves is straightforward within the weak-turbulence theory developed by Zakharov and his school. As the energy cascade proceeds to shorter scales, the mean surface steepness grows, and wave-breaking events appear with increasing frequency. Considering surface cusps, Phillips suggested a wavenumber spectrum (with a wrong magnitude, as I argue here) and a frequency spectrum whose whole form is incorrect. The right form for the frequency spectral slope due to breaking waves was suggested by Kuznetsov. Here we estimate the magnitude and argue that the frequency spectrum is expected to continue unchanged through the whole cascade. The transition from weak to strong turbulence in the wavenumber and spatio-temporal spectra is due to spectrally nonlocal interactions and happens earlier (at larger scales) than the dimensional estimate suggests. As a result, the magnitude of the strong-turbulence spectrum is not universal but depends on the pumping for a sufficiently long weak-turbulence cascade. The reason for nonlocality is likely that breaking of shorter waves is determined by the magnitude of the longest wave on whose crest they are running.

</details>


### [144] [Scalar mixing by stagnation points on solid surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00790)
*Gaute Linga,Tanguy Le Borgne,Joris Heyman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究单个障碍物附近分离线上细溶质条带的输运，建立上游滞止点附近标量混合的完整理论，识别出混合时间尺度 t_B ~ Pe^{1/3}，并给出不同阶段的浓度衰减与宽度标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有将多孔介质混合与无穷小流体元变形相联系的拉格朗日框架，通常忽略或简化了与固体表面的相互作用，因此需要研究固体边界对混合过程的影响。

Method: 考虑细溶质条带（带状或片状）在单个一般障碍物附近的输运，特别是沿连接固体边界滞止点的分离线（separatrices）；利用流动与扩散耦合可解析处理的特点，建立上游滞止点附近标量混合的完整理论，并用数值模拟验证。

Result: 识别出混合时间尺度 t_B ~ Pe^{1/3}，控制标量衰减由流体拉伸还是固体边界相互作用主导。t < t_B 时，最大浓度 c_max 按 t^{-5/2} 衰减，宽度 σ 按 t^{1/2} 增长；t > t_B 时，c_max 呈指数衰减，宽度趋于常数 σ ~ s_B ~ Pe^{-1/3}，其中 s_B 为区别于剪切流中的混合长度尺度。

Conclusion: c_max(t) 与 σ(t) 的渐近标度及其对 Pe 的依赖，对无滑移滞止点具有普适性，并且与开放流动中的滞止点在性质上不同。

Abstract: Recent advances have linked mixing in porous media to the deformation of infinitesimal fluid elements. However, these Lagrangian frameworks generally neglect or simplify interactions with solid surfaces. We consider transport of thin solute blobs (strips or sheets) near single generic obstacles, in particular along separation lines (separatrices) which connect to stagnation points at solid boundaries. In this case, we demonstrate that the coupling between flow and diffusion is analytically tractable. We provide a complete theory for scalar mixing near upstream stagnation points, which we validate by numerical simulations. We identify a mixing time scale $t_B \sim Pe^{1/3}$, where $Pe$ is the Péclet number, which controls whether scalar decay is driven by fluid stretching or by interaction with the solid boundary. Asymptotically, for times $t < t_B$, we find that the maximum concentration $c_{\rm max}$ decays as $t^{-5/2}$ and that the width $σ$ grows like $t^{1/2}$. For $t > t_B$, we find exponential decay of $c_{\rm max}$ and constant width $σ\sim s_B \sim Pe^{-1/3}$, where $s_B$ is a mixing length scale distinct from that which appears in shear flows. The asymptotic scalings of $c_{\rm max}(t)$ and $σ(t)$, and their dependence on $Pe$, are universal to no-slip stagnation points and qualitatively different from stagnation points in open flows.

</details>


### [145] [A conservative micro-continuum-cellular automaton method for multispecies biofilm dynamics in complex flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01565)
*Soyoung Kim,Yinuo Noah Yao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种守恒的微观连续-元胞自动机方法，耦合Darcy-Brinkman-Stokes方程、反应输运、悬浮细菌与生物膜动力学，实现多物种生物膜在复杂流动中的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂流动与几何条件下多物种生物膜的动力学模拟需要兼顾流动、传质与生物膜界面演化，并保证多物种生物量在非均匀网格上的守恒性与物种组成保持。

Method: 将Darcy-Brinkman-Stokes方程、反应输运、悬浮细菌和生物膜动力学与两阶段元胞自动机算法耦合，用于生物膜再分布与界面演化；把生物膜视为演化多孔介质，显式考虑供体与受体单元格体积，在部分占据单元格上实现多物种生物量的守恒再分布。

Result: 在扩散主导基准算例中验证方法，包括单物种指状形态与多物种分层；针对矩形凸起上的流动与生长开展网格收敛性研究；并应用于膜曝气生物膜反应器中的反向扩散生物膜。结果表明该框架可再现预期的生物膜形态与分层，且涉及流动、底物输运与生物膜动力学耦合。

Conclusion: 该守恒微观连续-元胞自动机框架为复杂流动与几何中多物种生物膜动力学提供了灵活的计算方法。

Abstract: We develop a conservative micro-continuum-cellular automaton method for simulating multispecies biofilm dynamics in complex flows. The proposed method couples the Darcy-Brinkman-Stokes equations, reactive transport, suspended bacteria, and biofilm dynamics with a two-stage cellular automaton algorithm for biofilm redistribution and interface evolution. While treating biofilms as evolving porous media, we ensure conservative redistribution of multispecies biomass across partially occupied cells. Donor and recipient cell volumes are explicitly accounted for to conserve biomass and preserve species composition on non-uniform meshes. The proposed method is assessed against diffusion-dominated benchmark cases, including single-species fingering and multispecies stratification, and is further evaluated through a mesh-convergence study for flow and growth over a rectangular bump. The framework is then applied to counter-diffusional biofilms in a membrane-aerated biofilm reactor as a canonical example. The results demonstrate that the framework captures the expected biofilm morphology and stratification in systems involving coupled flow, substrate transport, and biofilm dynamics. The proposed method provides a flexible computational approach for simulating multispecies biofilm dynamics in complex flows and geometries.

</details>


### [146] [Flow separation and turbulence production over trapezoidal protrusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01570)
*Shahar Zuckerman,Jibu Tom Jose,Ori Levi,Omri Ram*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过立体粒子图像测速实验，研究十二种梯形凸起物（L/h=1–4，后倾角30°、45°、90°）尾缘附近湍流特性，揭示后倾角影响依赖于前缘分离流与上表面再附的拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后倾角对表面凸起物周围湍流的影响取决于接近尾缘的分离流状态，但该依赖关系缺乏系统实验验证，需要明确几何参数（长高比与后倾角）如何共同决定湍流产生机制。

Method: 采用立体粒子图像测速（stereoscopic PIV）测量十二种梯形凸起物（L/h=1,2,3,4；后倾角30°、45°、90°），基于高度雷诺数约5×10^4，来流边界层厚度与凸起高度相当；并分解面内偏应力生成项，分离雷诺应力与平均应变幅值及其主轴取向的相对贡献。

Result: L/h=1和2时，连通的反向流区从上表面延伸至尾流；L/h=3和4时，上表面再附将两区域分离。长度决定拓扑转变及共同空间位置的湍流水平变化，后倾角影响在近壁区最强：L/h≥2时，较缓后倾面近面湍动能更高，垂直面能量较低但展向占比更大。倾斜面上，该生成项贡献在L/h=1至2之间由上表面再附前由负转正，随后随长度增加主要由张量幅值增大驱动，L/h=4且30°时贡献最大。

Conclusion: 局部湍流生成同时取决于接近尾缘的流动状态与高能剪切层相对于后表面的位置；长高比控制拓扑与整体湍流水平，后倾角则主要在近壁区调节湍动能分布与生成项符号。

Abstract: The influence of rear-face inclination on turbulence around a surface-mounted protrusion depends on the separated flow approaching its trailing edge. This dependence is examined experimentally using stereoscopic particle image velocimetry over twelve trapezoidal protrusions with upper-surface length-to-height ratios $L/h=1,2,3,4$ and rear-face angles of $30^\circ$, $45^\circ$, and $90^\circ$. Measurements were conducted at a height-based Reynolds number of approximately $5\times10^4$, with an incoming boundary-layer thickness comparable to the protrusion height. For $L/h=1$ and 2, a connected reverse-flow region extends over the upper surface into the wake. For $L/h=3$ and 4, upper-surface reattachment separates the two regions. Length governs this change in topology and the variation in turbulence levels at common spatial locations, whereas rear-face inclination has its strongest influence close to the surface. From $L/h=2$ onward, shallower faces have higher near-face turbulent kinetic energy, while vertical faces have lower energy but a larger spanwise fraction. A decomposition of the in-plane deviatoric production separates the effects of Reynolds-stress and mean-strain magnitudes from those of their relative principal-axis orientation. At the inclined faces, this contribution changes from negative to positive between $L/h=1$ and 2, before upper-surface reattachment occurs. Its subsequent increase with length is driven mainly by increasing tensor magnitudes, with the largest contribution at $L/h=4$ and $30^\circ$, where strong production remains close to the rear face. These results show that local turbulence production depends jointly on the flow state approaching the trailing edge and the position of the energetic shear layer relative to the rear surface.

</details>


### [147] [Total Collision Energy model for surface processes in the DSMC method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01586)
*Yevgeniy A. Bondar,Alexandra N. Molchanova*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 将气相互碰总碰撞能量(TCE)方法推广到表面化学建模,提出sTCE模型,由有限速率Arrhenius数据解析求得碰撞过程概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有TCE方法仅用于DSMC中的气相化学,表面化学反应建模缺乏相应处理;且此前模型存在数学缺陷。

Method: 提出表面TCE(sTCE)模型,将碰撞过程概率表示为分子平动能的解析函数,并由有限速率Arrhenius数据推导该概率函数;模型中可考虑表面温度、入射角与内能的影响,并推广至指定反应效率的表述。

Result: 所导出的概率函数克服了以往模型典型的数学缺陷;模型能够纳入表面温度、入射角和内能等效应,并支持指定反应效率的形式。

Conclusion: sTCE为DSMC表面化学提供了一种数学上更稳健、物理因素更完备的建模途径。

Abstract: The total collision energy (TCE) approach originally developed for taking into account gas-phase chemistry in the direct simulation Monte Carlo method is applied to surface chemistry modeling. In the proposed surface TCE (sTCE) model probability of the impact process as a function of the translational energy of the molecule is found analytically from the finite-rate Arrhenius data. This probability function lacks some mathematical drawbacks typical of previous models. Effects of surface temperature, incident angle and internal energy can be taken into account in the sTCE model. Additionally the model is generalized to the specified reaction efficiency formulation.

</details>


### [148] [An unstructured finite-volume Helmholtz method with perfectly matched layers for heterogeneous two-phase acoustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01593)
*Chuanchao Xu,Jun Liu,Jan-Helge Dörsam,Mario Kupnik,Tomislav Maric,Dieter Bothe*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种用于时谐两相声学的单元中心非结构有限体积法：以体积分数表征密度与声学压缩性，配合复坐标拉伸PML与实虚分裂，经多类算例验证，压力与速度重构约二阶收敛，分层透射相对L2误差低至2.657×10⁻⁴，刚球辐射力与Gorkov预测偏差不超过1.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向含异质两相介质的时谐声学问题，需要一种能在非结构网格上稳健处理界面与开放边界的数值格式，并阐明其精度与适用范围，因此构建并量化评估异质Helmholtz-PML有限体积解法。

Method: 采用单元中心非结构有限体积法；以体积分数表示密度和声学压缩性；面分数对相邻平面界面切割取平均且与速度方向无关，沿用单场面密度闭合；用笛卡尔复坐标拉伸构造完美匹配层；通过实虚分裂得到实值分块系统，并以修正的非正交通量与一致的边界贡献进行离散。

Result: 验证涵盖均匀波、带PML截断的分层气液透射、挡板活塞辐射（Kirchhoff远场重构）以及可分辨刚球辐射力。均匀波压力在正交网格及"内部非正交+边界正交单元"网格上约二阶收敛；重构速度在正交网格约二阶、后者约1.7阶。对齐加密下分层算例全域复压力相对L2误差降至2.657×10⁻⁴。可分辨球体辐射力在测试Rayleigh尺寸范围内与Gorkov预测最多相差1.5%。

Conclusion: 结果量化了异质Helmholtz-PML格式的精度与当前局限，表明该单元中心非结构有限体积框架在两相时谐声学中具备可用的二阶级精度，同时仍受PML与异质界面处理等因素限制。

Abstract: This paper presents a cell-centred unstructured finite-volume method for time-harmonic two-phase acoustics. A volume-fraction representation supplies density and acoustic compressibility. Face fractions average available neighbouring planar interface cuts independently of velocity direction, retaining the established one-field face-density closure. Cartesian complex-coordinate stretching provides perfectly matched layers (PMLs). Real-imaginary splitting yields a real-valued block system, discretized with corrected non-orthogonal fluxes and consistent boundary contributions. Verification covers homogeneous waves, layered gas-liquid transmission with PML truncation, baffled-piston radiation with Kirchhoff far-field reconstruction, and resolved rigid-sphere radiation forces. Homogeneous-wave pressure converges at approximately second order on orthogonal meshes and on meshes with non-orthogonal interiors and orthogonal boundary cells. Reconstructed velocity converges at approximately second order on orthogonal meshes and with an observed order of about 1.7 on the latter mesh family. Under aligned refinement, the layered case's relative whole-domain complex-pressure $L_2$ error decreases to $2.657\times10^{-4}$. The resolved-sphere force differs from the Gorkov prediction by at most 1.5% over the tested Rayleigh size range. The results quantify the accuracy and current limitations of the heterogeneous Helmholtz-PML formulation.

</details>


### [149] [Femtosecond laser-induced cavitation seeds liquid-jet breakup beyond the Rayleigh-Plateau stability limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01597)
*Sourabh Singh,Tamanna,S. Sree Harsha,Prashant Kumar Singh*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 利用飞秒激光诱导空化在流动液柱上施加局部脉冲反冲扰动，独立调控波长与振幅，绘制射流破碎的(kR, ε/R)二维图谱，实现经典边界内外的可控Rayleigh-Plateau破碎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Rayleigh-Plateau理论预测液柱仅当kR<1时微小扰动才会增长，kR=1为稳定性边界。如何在实验中解耦扰动的波长与振幅，并突破该线性边界实现可控破碎，尚缺乏系统手段。

Method: 用飞秒激光在流动液柱中诱导空化，产生局域脉冲反冲扰动；扰动波长由激光重复频率设定，振幅由脉冲能量独立设定，从而在(kR, ε/R)平面上二维扫描射流破碎行为。

Result: 低振幅下改变重复频率复现经典色散关系及kR=1截止；增大波长可依次获得单分散液滴链、双分散液滴群及孤立单液滴。低脉冲能量时种子仅放大所施加波数，超过临界能量Ec后种子展宽、同时激发多波数；更高振幅下在kR>1（至kR=1.9）实现确定性破碎，激光同步液滴产率可达0.2 MHz。

Conclusion: 飞秒激光诱导空化可作为在经典线性稳定性边界内外实现可控Rayleigh-Plateau破碎的通用途径。

Abstract: The Rayleigh-Plateau instability predicts that infinitesimal perturbations of a liquid jet grow only for kR < 1, with kR = 1 marking the classical stability boundary. Here, we show that femtosecond-laser-induced cavitation generates a localized, impulsive recoil perturbation on a flowing liquid jet, enabling a two-dimensional map of jet breakup across the (kR, epsilon/R) plane. The perturbation's spacing (wavelength) and strength (amplitude) are set independently by the laser repetition rate and pulse energy, respectively. At low amplitudes, tuning the repetition rate recovers the classical Rayleigh-Plateau dispersion relation and its kR = 1 cutoff, while increasing the wavelength enables controlled generation of monodisperse droplet trains, bidisperse droplet populations, and ultimately isolated single droplets. Along the amplitude axis, low pulse energies produce a seed that amplifies only the imposed wavenumber, whereas above a critical energy Ec, the seed broadens to excite many wavenumbers simultaneously. At still higher amplitudes, we demonstrate deterministic breakup for kR > 1, extending to kR = 1.9, with laser-synchronized droplet production rates up to 0.2 MHz. These results establish localized femtosecond-laser-induced cavitation as a route to controlled Rayleigh-Plateau breakup both within and beyond the classical linear-stability boundary.

</details>


### [150] [On the transmission of floating-point perturbations in flow-dependent filter-width formulations in Large-Eddy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01743)
*Valerio D'Alessandro,Alessio Piccolo,Matteo Falone,Simone Bnà*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该工作研究大涡模拟(LES)中最小二乘(LSQ)滤波宽度的浮点非结合性敏感性：证明其相对于网格度量的扰动对数非扩张但不严格收缩，提出标量max-min压缩与有界调制加以缓解，并在异构CPU/GPU上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构高性能计算架构使数值算法暴露于浮点运算非结合性引起的扰动之下。在LES中，若扰动影响进入亚格子尺度(SGS)模型的滤波宽度，这类数值效应可能变得显著。作者希望揭示LSQ滤波宽度公式中浮点效应的产生、传递及其与已解析流场的耦合机制。

Method: 以LSQ滤波宽度公式为对象分析浮点扰动的产生与传递。在已解析运动学固定的前提下，从理论上刻画其对网格度量扰动的行为（对数非扩张、非严格收缩）。为缓解敏感性，引入方向性网格尺度的标量max-min压缩，并结合基于已解析速度梯度的有界调制。在异构CPU与GPU架构上，针对Re=3900圆柱绕流与Re=1600 Taylor–Green涡进行评测。

Result: 对于固定已解析运动学，LSQ滤波宽度相对网格度量扰动是对数非扩张的，但不严格收缩，因此小扰动可经强方向性滤波宽度响应以极小衰减传递。在圆柱绕流中，相对度量扰动近乎一对一的传递在扰动与剪切层转捩相互作用时可能变得重要；而在正交Taylor–Green涡网格上，该累积路径在结构上不存在，未出现可比的宏观响应。

Conclusion: 所提出的公式通过滤波宽度算子重新路由浮点扰动，在保留原LSQ构造流动相关特性的同时提升鲁棒性。结果表明，在异构计算环境下，浮点敏感性对LES滤波宽度公式具有现实意义。

Abstract: Heterogeneous high--performance computing architectures expose numerical algorithms to perturbations arising from the non--associativity of floating--point arithmetic. In Large--Eddy Simulation (LES), similar numerical effects may become relevant when they affect the filter--width entering the subgrid scale (SGS) model. This work investigates this mechanism for the least--squares (LSQ) based filter--width formulation, focusing on how floating--point effects are generated, transmitted, and coupled with the resolved flow. We show that, for fixed resolved kinematics, the LSQ filter--width is logarithmically non-expansive but not strictly contractive with respect to perturbations of the mesh metrics. Consequently, small disturbances may be transmitted with little attenuation through strongly directional filter-width responses. To mitigate this sensitivity, we introduce a scalar max--min compression of the directional mesh scales together with a bounded modulation based on the resolved velocity gradient.
  The resulting formulation reroutes floating--point perturbations through the filter-width operator, improving robustness while preserving the flow--dependent character of the original LSQ construction. The framework is assessed on heterogeneous CPU and GPU architectures for flow past a circular cylinder at Re=3900 and the Taylor--Green vortex at Re=1600. In the former, nearly one--to--one transmission of relative metric disturbances can become relevant when the transmitted perturbations interact with shear-layer transition. By contrast, on orthogonal Taylor--Green vortex grids, the accumulation pathway is structurally absent and no comparable macroscopic response develops. These results suggest floating--point sensitivity matters for LES filter--width formulations in heterogeneous computing environments.

</details>


### [151] [Direct Modeling of Pore Size Evolution and Microcollapse in Lyophilization by Population Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01851)
*Isaac Stonewall Wheeler,Vivek Narsimhan,Alina A. Alexeenko,Davide Fissore*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文针对冻干一次干燥中无定形辅料接近玻璃化转变温度时的"微塌陷"现象，建立了孔径分布的群体平衡模型，并用矩量法加插值闭合将其计算成本控制在文献标准模型水平，验证了传质阻力表达式的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微塌陷会导致传质阻力下降，但现有建模方法要么回避该温度区间，要么需要通过大量实验经验性地评估阻力下降幅度，缺乏机理性且实验负担重。作者希望直接从机理层面正面处理微塌陷现象。

Method: 建立冻干饼孔径分布的群体平衡模型，描述微塌陷过程中的孔径演化，并将孔径与传质阻力关联；采用矩量法（method of moments）与插值闭合（interpolative closure）处理群体平衡方程，使计算负担与文献标准的干燥模型相当。随后用文献数据分三种方式验证：对传质阻力随产品温度变化的案例，分别拟合高温与低温工况并预测中间工况；用另一组事后测得孔径分布（无微塌陷配方）的数据验证传质阻力表达式的有效性，仅拟合一个参数；对一个配方在多种条件下的多实验联合拟合。

Result: 模型可准确模拟中间温度工况；在无微塌陷配方的数据上，仅拟合一个参数即可获得与事后经验评估相当的产品温度预测精度，验证了传质阻力表达式；多实验联合拟合在较宽条件范围内实现了产品温度的定量一致，表明微塌陷速率常数可能是与具体多孔几何无关的材料参数。

Conclusion: 所提出的群体平衡模型为微塌陷期间的传质阻力提供了机理性描述，且计算代价低、可与标准一次干燥模型兼容；微塌陷速率常数可能具有材料参数性质，为冻干工艺的预测性建模提供了新途径。

Abstract: In the primary drying step of pharmaceutical freeze drying, it is well known that amorphous excipients are prone to "microcollapse" as they approach their glass transition temperature. Existing modeling approaches for this phenomenon generally entail either avoidance of this regime or an experimentally burdensome empirical assessment of the resulting drop in resistance to mass transfer. In this work, we approach the phenomenon head-on by developing a population balance model for the pore size distribution of a freeze-dried cake during microcollapse and relating the pore size to mass transfer resistance. By treating the population balance with the method of moments and an interpolative closure, we keep the model's computational burden on par with the literature-standard model for primary drying.
  For a literature example when mass transfer resistance clearly varies as a function of product temperature during drying, we fit the population balance model to cases with high and low product temperature and show that it can then simulate intermediate cases with equal accuracy. The validity of the expression for mass transfer resistance is demonstrated with another literature data set, where pore size distributions were measured after the fact for a formulation without microcollapse; only a single parameter from this model needs to be fit to those cases to yield predictive accuracy in product temperature on par with post-hoc empirical assessment. Finally, a sophisticated multi-experiment fit for one formulation across a range of conditions yields quantitative agreement in product temperatures, suggesting that the rate constant for microcollapse behavior may be a material parameter and independent of the precise porous geometry.

</details>


### [152] [Rapid generation of relaxed centimeter-sized drops by capillary auto-ejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01857)
*Patric Mueller,Kai Ihrig,Achim Sack,Jonathan E. Kollmer,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究探讨利用毛细自动喷射（水洼跳跃）作为被动机制产生自由漂浮的厘米级液滴，并通过优化支撑腔体形状来最小化喷射后的弛豫时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力实验常需厘米级球形液滴，而落塔实验仅提供数秒微重力，因此液滴必须在极短时间内生成并达到近乎松弛（平衡）状态。

Method: 对填充固定液体积的抛物线截面腔体，通过最小化初始液体表面积求解唯一形状，并采用光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）模拟验证弛豫时间；进一步在腔体中引入浅中心凹陷进行优化。

Result: 最小表面积形状在抛物线族中同时给出最短弛豫时间；引入浅中心凹陷可进一步缩短弛豫时间，尽管其初始液体表面积更大。

Conclusion: 该结果为毛细驱动液滴发生器提供设计准则，可在落塔短时微重力窗口内快速生成已达松弛状态的厘米级液滴。

Abstract: Many microgravity experiments require centimeter-sized spherical liquid drops. In drop-tower experiments, only a few seconds of microgravity are available, so the drops must be generated and reach a nearly relaxed state within a very short time. We investigate capillary auto-ejection (puddle jumping) as a passive mechanism for producing free-floating drops and investigate how the supporting cavity can be shaped to minimize the relaxation time after ejection.
  For cavities with a parabolic cross-section filled with a fixed liquid volume, minimization of the initial liquid surface area yields a unique shape, and smoothed particle hydrodynamics simulations show that this shape also gives the shortest relaxation time within the parabolic family. Introducing a shallow central depression into the cavity reduces the relaxation time further, despite producing a larger initial liquid surface area. These results provide design principles for capillary-driven drop generators that enable the rapid production of relaxed centimeter-sized drops during the short microgravity intervals available in drop towers.

</details>


### [153] [Helical pipes homogenize particle transport in pneumatic conveying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01920)
*Wing To Ku,Patric Mueller,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用 CFD-DEM 模拟研究螺旋管在中等气速下对气力输送颗粒流动的均化作用。螺旋管诱导气固两相旋流，使颗粒在管道截面上持续再分布，将直管中的强非均质输送转变为更均匀状态，颗粒速度分布变窄，从而在中等气速下维持高效输送并抑制过高颗粒速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中等气速气力输送存在强非均质性：慢速颗粒在管底沉积，快速颗粒在管上部输送，导致颗粒速度分布宽、输送不均匀。作者希望在不提高气速（避免能耗与颗粒磨损/破碎增加）的前提下实现颗粒输送的被动均化。

Method: 以螺旋管作为被动均化手段，采用 CFD-DEM（计算流体力学-离散元耦合）方法进行数值模拟，对比直管与螺旋管内的气固两相流动，分析螺旋结构诱导的旋流运动及颗粒在管道横截面上的再分布过程。

Result: 螺旋管诱导气体与颗粒产生旋流运动，使颗粒在管道截面上被连续再分布；直管中观察到的非均质输送在螺旋管中转化为更均匀的输送状态，颗粒速度分布明显变窄。

Conclusion: 螺旋管可作为一种被动手段，在中等气速下维持高效气力输送，同时降低过高的颗粒速度，实现颗粒输送的均化。

Abstract: Pneumatic conveying at moderate gas velocities can result in strongly heterogeneous particle transport, with slow particles accumulating near the bottom of the pipe while faster particles are transported above. Here, we investigate helical pipes as a passive means of homogenizing particle transport without increasing the gas velocity. CFD-DEM simulations show that the helical pipe induces swirling motion of the gas and particles, continuously redistributing particles across the pipe cross-section. This transforms the heterogeneous transport observed in a straight pipe into a more homogeneous state with a narrower particle-velocity distribution. The results demonstrate that helical pipes can maintain efficient pneumatic conveying at moderate gas velocities while reducing excessive particle velocities.

</details>


### [154] [Spiral motion enables simultaneous homogenization and enhanced transport in granular pipe flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02105)
*Wing To Ku,Patric Mueller,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文利用离散元模拟（DEM）揭示螺旋壁面纹理通过诱导颗粒螺旋运动实现颗粒管流均质化，并提出中心线呈螺旋路径的螺旋管设计，可同时提升流动均匀性与平均流速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重力驱动颗粒管流中的密度波会降低输运效率并造成局部固相分数大幅波动。螺旋壁面纹理虽能抑制这类不稳定性，但代价是吞吐量下降，因此需要理解其机理并寻求兼顾均质性与输运效率的设计方案。

Method: 采用离散元模拟（DEM）研究螺旋壁面纹理下的颗粒管流，以平均角速度量化颗粒的螺旋运动，分析螺旋运动、流动均质化与输运性能之间的相关性；并据此设计中心线沿螺旋路径、横截面保持恒定圆形的螺旋管，考察不同设计参数下的流动特性。

Result: 颗粒均质化与螺旋壁面纹理诱导的螺旋运动密切相关，平均角速度与流动均质化及输运性能呈强相关。所提出的螺旋管设计能产生比传统螺旋壁面纹理更强的螺旋运动；在合适设计参数下，可同时增强流动均匀性，并使平均流速超过光滑直管。

Conclusion: 螺旋运动可作为有用的诊断量与设计变量，在所研究参数范围内可同时改善颗粒管流的流动均匀性与输运效率。

Abstract: Density waves in gravity-driven granular pipe flow reduce transport efficiency and produce large fluctuations in the local solid fraction. Helical wall textures suppress these instabilities but at the expense of reduced throughput. Using discrete element simulations (DEM), \RefA{we find that this homogenization is associated with} spiral particle motion induced by the helical wall texture. Quantifying this motion by the mean angular velocity reveals a strong correlation between spiral motion, flow homogenization, and transport performance. Motivated by this insight, we introduce a helical pipe whose centerline follows a helical path while maintaining a constant circular cross section. This design generates stronger spiral motion than conventional helical wall textures. For suitable design parameters, the helical pipe simultaneously enhances flow homogeneity and increases the mean flow velocity beyond that of a plain straight pipe. These results support spiral motion as a useful diagnostic and design variable for simultaneously improving flow homogeneity and transport efficiency within the investigated parameter range.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [High-Value Synthetic Supervision for Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of a Compact Japanese Speech Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00026)
*Sidi Chang,Peiying Zhu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文研究一条可审计的合成数据流水线，将日语护理交接语音直接映射为六字段结构化笔记，并比较全量微调与LoRA在1.47B音频模型上的能力获取效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有领域语音难以采集与再分发，且紧凑模型需要任务特定监督，因此需要一种可审计的合成数据方案来规避隐私与数据获取限制。

Method: 使用182个合成训练与开发片段，对1.47B音频模型进行全量微调与rank-16 LoRA适配；在39个场景种子不相交的合成测试集上评估，采用模型判定的factuality-recall分数。

Result: 未适配模型得分0.0500，全量微调0.8664，LoRA为0.8461；LoRA达到全量微调聚合性能的97.7%，可训练参数约12.4M（约占骨干0.85%）；两者相对同一基座均有大幅配对增益，但全量与LoRA的区间跨零，且优化设置不同，无法声称等价。

Conclusion: 结果为参数高效的能力获取结论而非设备性能结论：紧凑模型可从数百个带来源溯源的合成样本中习得狭窄的音频到结构化转换能力；但未验证临床有效性、真实语音迁移或优于干净云系统的结论，所有语音与目标均为合成、参考由模型提出、评判器未校准。

Abstract: Private domain speech is difficult to collect and redistribute, while compact models need task-specific supervision. We study an auditable synthetic pipeline that maps Japanese care handoffs directly to six-field structured notes. Using 182 synthetic training and development clips, we adapt a 1.47B audio model by full fine-tuning and rank-16 LoRA. On a 39-clip scenario-seed-disjoint synthetic test, an unadapted model obtains a model-judged factuality-recall score of 0.0500, full tuning 0.8664, and LoRA 0.8461. LoRA reaches 97.7% of the full-tuning aggregate as a descriptive ratio while project telemetry reports 12.4M trainable parameters, about 0.85% of the backbone. Both adaptations show large paired gains over the same base; the full-versus-LoRA interval crosses zero, and differing optimization settings preclude an equivalence claim. This is a parameter-efficient capability-acquisition result, not a device-performance result: latency, memory, energy, and real-time factor were not measured. All evaluation speech and targets are synthetic, references are model-proposed, and the judge is uncalibrated. The evidence shows that a compact model can acquire a narrow audio-to-structure transformation from a few hundred provenance-linked synthetic examples; it does not establish clinical validity, real-speech transfer, or superiority to a clean cloud system.

</details>


### [156] [How Robust Are Neural Audio Codecs for African Speech? A Multi-Task Benchmark and the Limits of Perceptual Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00154)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文对七种开源神经音频编解码器在三种非洲语音数据集上进行基准测试，从信号级质量与ASR、ASV下游任务两个维度评估其鲁棒性，并提出四项发现，强调任务感知、领域代表性及适配感知的编解码器评测必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经音频编解码器已支持低比特率语音压缩与分词，但其在带口音及多语言语音上的鲁棒性评估仍不充分，尤其在代表性不足的非洲语音场景下缺乏系统性评测。

Method: 选取七种开源神经编解码器（DAC、EnCodec、FocalCodec、LanguageCodec、SemantiCodec、UniCodec、WavTokenizer），在afrinames、afrispeech dialog、afrispeech multilingual三个非洲语音数据集上，结合NISQA、UTMOS、ViSQOL、STOI、F0-RMSE等信号级指标与ASR、ASV两项下游任务进行基准评测；并采用LoRA（约1–3%参数）在约35小时非洲语音上进行参数高效编解码器适配。

Result: 得出四项发现：①基于参考的结构/可懂度指标（ViSQOL、STOI）与韵律误差（F0-RMSE）比神经MOS预测器（NISQA、UTMOS）更能可靠反映ASR与ASV退化；②可懂度与说话人身份保持在不同架构间差异显著，身份排序依赖ASV后端；③退化呈强领域依赖性，会话对话场景最严重；④退化部分可恢复，LoRA适配可缩小压缩导致的WER差距，使识别性能接近未压缩基线。

Conclusion: 任务感知、领域代表性及适配感知的语音编解码器评测是实现包容性部署的前提，应成为该领域评测的标准实践。

Abstract: Neural audio codecs enable low-bitrate speech compression and tokenization, yet their robustness on accented and multilingual speech remains under-evaluated. We benchmark seven open-source neural codecs (DAC, EnCodec, FocalCodec, LanguageCodec, SemantiCodec, UniCodec, WavTokenizer) on three African speech datasets afrinames, afrispeech dialog, afrispeech multilingual, reporting signal-level quality (NISQA, UTMOS, ViSQOL, STOI, F0-RMSE) alongside two downstream tasks: automatic speech recognition (ASR) and speaker verification (ASV). Our analysis yields four findings. First, signal metrics differ sharply in downstream validity: reference-based structural/intelligibility measures (ViSQOL, STOI) and prosodic error (F0-RMSE) track ASR and ASV degradation far more reliably than neural mean-opinion-score predictors (NISQA, UTMOS). Second, intelligibility and speaker-identity preservation diverge sharply across architectures, and the apparent identity ranking itself depends on the ASV backend. Third, degradation is strongly domain-dependent and largest for conversational dialog. Fourth, the resulting degradation is partly recoverable: parameter-efficient \emph{codec} adaptation (LoRA, $\sim$1--3\% of parameters) on roughly 35 hours of African speech reduces the compression-induced word-error-rate gap, recovering recognition toward the uncompressed baseline (developed in companion work). These results motivate task-aware, domain-representative, and adaptation-aware evaluation of speech codecs as a prerequisite for inclusive deployment.

</details>


### [157] [ProxyMOS: Label-Free Speech Quality Assessment by Multi-Teacher Distillation with Adaptive Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00419)
*Maxim Trokunov,Kirill Borodin,Nikita Vasiliev,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 ProxyMOS：无需新人工标注，将多个公开 MOS 预测器经子集搜索与路由融合，蒸馏出单一 wav2vec 2.0 学生模型，在 URGENT 与新建俄语 TTS 基准 mos260 上超越最优教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工 MOS 采集成本高，且非侵入式 MOS 预测器在训练域外性能急剧下降；因此需要在不新增人工标签的前提下，将已有公开预测器整合为更强的单一模型。

Method: 基准评测 8 个 MOS 预测器；选取信息量最大的 5 个进入子集搜索，采用均匀、相关性加权、误差加权、MSE 优化及自适应逐句路由五种融合规则；用最优路由的四模型集成对 807k 无标注语句打标，训练 wav2vec 2.0 学生模型。

Result: URGENT 上学生 Spearman ρ=0.802，优于最优教师的 0.773；在 mos260（38 种合成条件、4,600 条俄语 TTS 新基准）上逐句 ρ=0.636（教师 0.613），按条件 ρ=0.95，单次前向即匹配自身路由集成；自适应路由是唯一在加入弱预测器时不退化的规则。

Conclusion: ProxyMOS 证明可通过路由式集成与知识蒸馏复用现成 MOS 预测器，获得无新标签下更强且更鲁棒的单模型；已开源模型、ONNX 导出与 mos260 基准。摘要约 950 字符，符合 arXiv 1920 字符限制；数学符号 ρ 可替换为 rho 以规避渲染问题。

Abstract: Human mean opinion scores (MOS) are costly to collect, and non-intrusive MOS predictors degrade sharply outside their training domain. ProxyMOS turns a pool of public MOS predictors into a single stronger model without new human labels. Eight predictors are benchmarked against human ratings; the five most informative enter a subset search under uniform, correlation-weighted, error-weighted, MSE-optimised and adaptive per-utterance routing; and the best routed four-model ensemble labels 807k unlabeled utterances that train a wav2vec 2.0 student. On URGENT the student reaches Spearman $ρ=0.802$ against $0.773$ for the best teacher. On mos260, a new Russian TTS benchmark of 4,600 utterances from 38 synthesis conditions, it reaches $ρ=0.636$ against $0.613$ per utterance and $0.95$ per condition, matching its own routed ensemble in one forward pass. Adaptive routing is the only rule that does not degrade when weak predictors are added. Model, ONNX exports and mos260 are released. It's about 950 characters; arXiv's limit is 1,920. I kept $ρ$ because arXiv renders it on the abstract page. If you'd rather avoid math, replace $ρ=0.802$ with rho = 0.802 and do the same for the other $...$ values.

</details>


### [158] [Collapse, Not Invariance: Diagnosing Auxiliary Objectives in Speech Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00539)
*Ksenia Lysikova,Kirill Borodin,Maxim Maslov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 系统比较了七种用于语音反欺骗的辅助目标函数与交叉熵，在113次实验中发现没有任何辅助目标能在不同架构、语料与随机种子下稳定优于交叉熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音反欺骗系统在生成器、编解码器或信道变化时性能下降，常用补救手段是通过辅助目标塑造嵌入空间，但EER作为纯排序指标无法反映嵌入空间是否真正改善，故需直接检验这些辅助目标的有效性。

Method: 在五个语料上共113次实验，对比七种辅助目标与交叉熵；使用AASIST3三个随机种子加四个预训练检测器，并直接测量24次AASIST3运行的嵌入空间（位移、余弦一致性、归一化后的展布、决策轴结构等）。

Result: 原始增强位移使余弦一致性看似有效，但增益源于更小的空间而非更稳定的空间；按展布归一化后，没有任何配置持续优于交叉熵。所有训练空间均被二分类所预期的单一决策轴主导，其训练集结构无法迁移；四次运行坍缩为近常数输出——位移奖励这种坍缩而EER将其报告为低准确率。

Conclusion: 没有任何辅助目标能在架构、语料与种子之间保持相对交叉熵的优势；以EER评价会掩盖嵌入空间的退化，需直接测量嵌入空间几何。

Abstract: Speech anti-spoofing countermeasures degrade when the generator, codec or channel changes, and a common remedy is an auxiliary objective that shapes the embedding space; whether it does is invisible to EER, a pure ranking metric. We compare seven such objectives with cross-entropy over 113 runs on five corpora, AASIST3 at three seeds plus four pre-trained detectors, and measure the embedding space of the 24 AASIST3 runs directly. Raw augmentation displacement makes cosine consistency look effective, but the gain is a smaller space, not a more stable one: normalised by the spread, no configuration consistently improves on cross-entropy. Every trained space is dominated by the single decision axis expected for two classes, whose training-set structure does not transfer, and four runs collapse to a near-constant output that displacement rewards and EER reports as poor accuracy. No auxiliary objective keeps an advantage over cross-entropy across architectures, corpora and seeds.

</details>


### [159] [PLACE: Positional Latent Adaptation via Conditioned Embeddings for Binaural Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00630)
*Tiernon Riesenmy,You Zhang,Gautam Bhattacharya,Andrea Fanelli*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: PLACE 将预训练 any-to-audio 模型 AudioX 扩展到双耳音频生成，通过感知编码器特征、文本-视频空间线索对齐、条件相关的低秩变换，以及双耳线索监督，实现灵活的多模态空间化音频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练 any-to-audio 模型（如 AudioX）主要针对单声道音频生成，难以从文本、视频及可选音频等任意组合提示中生成具有正确空间一致性（双耳线索）的音频。

Method: 在 AudioX 基础上增加适配器：1) 用 Perception Encoder Core 特征增强视频条件；2) 对齐文本与视频表示以推导空间线索；3) 对生成潜变量施加条件相关的低秩变换；4) 以解码音频的双耳级差（ILD）与时间差（ITD）目标进行监督；在 MRSAudio 上训练。

Result: 在 FAIR-Play 上多数指标优于 ViSAGe；在 BEWO-1M Single Static 测试集上 SpatialCLAP 分数优于 SpatialSonic；主观听测在视频到音频及分布外文本到音频生成中更受偏好。

Conclusion: PLACE 实现了灵活的多模态控制与更好的空间一致性，验证了将单声道预训练模型扩展为双耳生成的有效性。

Abstract: We present PLACE, a method that extends the pretrained any-to-audio model AudioX for binaural generation from arbitrary combinations of text, video, and optional audio prompts. PLACE augments video conditioning with Perception Encoder Core features, aligns text and video representations to derive spatial cues, and applies a conditioning-dependent low-rank transformation to the generated latent. The adapter is supervised via decoded-audio interaural level and time difference objectives. Trained on MRSAudio, PLACE improves most metrics over ViSAGe on FAIR-Play and achieves an improved SpatialCLAP score over SpatialSonic on the BEWO-1M Single Static test split. Listener evaluations favor PLACE for video-to-audio and out-of-distribution text-to-audio generation, demonstrating flexible multimodal control and improved spatial consistency.

</details>


### [160] [On Evaluating Quantum Kernel Robustness for Low-Resource Cross-Corpus Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00649)
*Lisan Al Amin,Lei Zhang,Vandana P. Janeja*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 在仅200个训练样本、4维PCA特征的严苛低资源约束下，比较QSVM、经典SVM与MLP在跨语料合成语音检测中的表现；QSVM在ASVspoof 2019→ADD 2023的严重域偏移下保持有效判别（AUC 76.0%、EER 27.0%），而MLP退化至近随机（AUC≈50%、EER 50.0%），但该优势不具跨方向一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成语音检测对音频安全至关重要，但标注数据稀缺、评测条件偏离训练分布时性能显著下降。作者希望探究量子核方法与轻量神经网络在受限训练数据下的跨语料音频深伪检测能力，并考察量子核归纳偏置在分布偏移下的经验特性。

Method: 以冻结的wav2vec 2.0嵌入为输入，用PCA降至4维以匹配近期量子硬件的比特预算；在统一的200样本训练预算下，对QSVM、经典SVM和MLP三种模型使用同一组降维特征进行训练与比较。数据集包括ASVspoof 2019、ASVspoof 5、ADD 2023和In-the-Wild，重点评测跨语料迁移。

Result: ASVspoof 2019→ADD 2023的严重域偏移下，MLP退化为近随机水平（AUC约50%、EER 50.0%）；QSVM仍具判别力（AUC 76.0%、EER 27.0%）。但该优势不跨迁移方向稳定：以ADD 2023为训练集时，QSVM在3个迁移方向中有2个低于随机，而MLP表现更好。

Conclusion: 量子核方法在严重跨语料偏移与严格低资源条件下可具竞争力，但在近域迁移下并无一致优势。作者将其定位为对分布偏移下量子核归纳偏置的经验刻画，而非量子优越性证据，因为该4比特核可在经典硬件上精确模拟。

Abstract: Synthetic speech detection is critical for audio security, but performance can degrade when labeled data are scarce and evaluation conditions differ from training. This study examines quantum kernel methods and lightweight neural models for cross-corpus audio deepfake detection under limited training data. We compare a Quantum Support Vector Machine (QSVM), a classical support vector machine (SVM), and a multilayer perceptron (MLP), all trained on frozen wav2vec 2.0 embeddings using a strict budget of 200 training samples. To match the qubit budget of near-term quantum hardware, embeddings are reduced to four dimensions using principal component analysis, and all models use the same reduced features. Experiments on ASVspoof 2019, ASVspoof 5, the ADD 2023 Challenge, and the In-the-Wild dataset show that under severe domain shift from ASVspoof 2019 to ADD 2023, the MLP degrades to near-random performance, with an area under the curve of approximately 50% and an equal error rate of 50.0%. In contrast, the QSVM maintains meaningful discrimination, achieving an area under the curve of 76.0% and an equal error rate of 27.0%. This advantage is not consistent across transfer directions. When trained on ADD 2023, the QSVM falls below chance on two of three transfers, while the MLP performs better. These results suggest that quantum kernel methods can be competitive under severe cross-corpus shifts and strict low-resource constraints, but do not provide a consistent advantage under near-domain transfer. We interpret these findings as an empirical characterization of quantum kernel inductive bias under distribution shift, rather than evidence of quantum advantage, since the four-qubit kernel can be simulated exactly on classical hardware.

</details>


### [161] [Balalaika-Longform: A Russian Speech Corpus for Continuous Long-Form Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00658)
*Nikita Vasiliev,Kirill Borodin,Vasilii Kudryavtsev,Maxim Maslov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Balalaika-Longform，一个 189 小时的开放俄语长文本语音语料库，以 30 秒至 15 分钟的连续单元组织，并在相同源录音上对比短窗与长单元微调对长文本 TTS 的连续合成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文本 TTS 需要在长时生成中保留请求的词语，但句子级训练与评测可能掩盖漏词和提前停止的问题，因此需要长单元语料与可保留每次合成尝试的评测协议来暴露这些缺陷。

Method: 构建 Balalaika-Longform 语料库，含 30 秒至 15 分钟的连续长单元及匹配的短窗，在相同源录音上支持微调对比，并保留每次合成尝试的评测。对 CosyVoice3、Qwen3-TTS、VoxCPM2 和 F5-TTS 分别在短窗与长单元视图上微调，在 50 个文本-音色对（微调未见音色）上测试约 75、300 和 1,200 词的连续合成。

Result: 在 1,200 词时，CosyVoice3 的 WER 从短窗微调后的 99.9% 降至长目标下的 47.3%，在带标点、格式匹配的转写下进一步降至 16.6%。配对区间显示长目标对 Qwen3-TTS、VoxCPM2 有利，对 F5-TTS 亦有小幅优势，但后者绝对 WER 仍高于 90%。

Conclusion: 结果在固定连续生成协议下分离出训练序列长度的影响，表明长单元训练目标显著改善长文本连续合成的词语保留能力，且转写格式匹配可进一步大幅降低 WER。

Abstract: Long-form text-to-speech must retain requested words over extended generations, yet sentence-level training and evaluation can hide omissions and early stops. We introduce Balalaika-Longform, an open Russian corpus of 189 hours in continuous units of 30 seconds to 15 minutes. Long units and matched short windows support fine-tuning comparisons on the same source recordings, and the accompanying evaluation retains every synthesis attempt. We fine-tune CosyVoice3, Qwen3-TTS, VoxCPM2 and F5-TTS on either view and test continuous synthesis on 50 text-voice pairs with voices unseen in fine-tuning, at about 75, 300 and 1,200 words. At 1,200 words, CosyVoice3 WER falls from 99.9% after short-window fine-tuning to 47.3% with long targets, and to 16.6% with punctuated, format-matched transcripts; paired intervals favor long targets for Qwen3-TTS and VoxCPM2, and by a small margin for F5-TTS, whose absolute WER stays above 90%. The results isolate the effect of training sequence length under a fixed continuous-generation protocol.

</details>


### [162] [AnchorPrompt: Self-Distilled Soft Prompts for Robust Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00706)
*Pooneh Mousavi,Amir Ivry,Mirco Ravanelli,Cem Subakan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 AnchorPrompt：冻结模型、在解码器输入处插入单块提示向量，通过自蒸馏学习，提升 LALM 抗扰动一致性并减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型对噪声、波形损坏和对抗注入等输入扰动敏感，导致答案不一致与幻觉，需要一种高效且不依赖扰动检测的适配方法。

Method: 保持模型冻结，在音频与问题嵌入之间的解码器输入处学习单块提示向量；在多样音频与文本扰动下自蒸馏训练，可答输入以干净录音的预测为目标，证据不足时以拒答为目标；推理时与扰动无关，无需先验检测，并可零样本迁移至未见失真。

Result: 在三个 LALM 与三个基准上评估：多数条件下答案一致性提升；9 个模型-基准组合中 6 个干净准确率提升，其余最多仅下降 1.2%；严重音频损坏下幻觉减少，干净音频误拒答很少；一致性增益可迁移至选择项排列与混响等未见扰动。

Conclusion: AnchorPrompt 以轻量提示向量适配实现扰动无关的鲁棒性与一致性提升，兼顾干净性能并具备零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) are sensitive to input perturbations, such as noise, waveform corruption, and adversarial injections. We propose AnchorPrompt, an efficient adaptation method that keeps the model frozen and learns a single block of prompt vectors inserted at the decoder input, between the audio and question embeddings. We train these vectors through self-distillation over diverse audio and text perturbations. To improve answer consistency and mitigate hallucination, we use the model's prediction on the clean recording as the target for answerable inputs, and assign a refusal target when the audio lacks sufficient evidence to answer. Furthermore, AnchorPrompt is perturbation-agnostic at inference, requiring no prior detection of perturbations and enabling zero-shot transfer to unseen distortions. We evaluate three LALMs across three benchmarks and show that AnchorPrompt improves answer consistency in most tested conditions. Clean accuracy improves in six of nine model-benchmark pairs, with minimal impact on the remainder of 1.2% at most. Crucially, AnchorPrompt reduces hallucinations under severe audio corruption while keeping false refusals on clean audio rare. Finally, these consistency gains transfer to unseen perturbations, such as choice permutations and reverberation.

</details>


### [163] [Where the Body Keeps the Beat: Structured Motion Conditioning and Music Dynamics Supervision for Dance-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00726)
*Changchang Sun,Lu Cheng,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: Dyna2Music：结构化运动条件+显式潜动力学监督的潜在流匹配D2M框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有D2M方法未显式区分身体部位与频段的运动线索，且标准flow matching缺乏对局部音乐潜动力学的专用监督目标。

Method: 先在AIST++上量化空间划分与频率分离对音乐-运动节拍对齐和运动参考密度的影响；据此将关节速度分解为快/慢分量，分层融合部位级运动能量与预训练关节特征作为条件；提出潜动力学一致性（LDC）辅助目标，对齐单步干净潜估计与参考的相邻帧变化幅度，无额外参数与推理开销；支持变长生成。

Result: 在AIST++与TikTok上，相比代表性D2M基线，节奏对齐与音频质量均提升。

Conclusion: 结构化运动条件与显式潜动力学监督可有效提升D2M的节奏对齐与音频质量。

Abstract: Dance-to-music (D2M) generation aims to synthesize musically plausible soundtracks whose temporal structure aligns with a given dance performance. Representative D2M methods do not explicitly distinguish motion cues across body parts and frequency bands, while standard flow matching lacks a dedicated objective for supervising local music-latent dynamics. To address these issues, we propose Dyna2Music, a latent flow-matching framework that combines structured motion conditioning with explicit supervision of music-latent dynamics. An empirical analysis on AIST++ quantifies how spatial partitioning and frequency separation affect raw music-to-kinematic beat alignment and motion-reference density, informing the conditioning design. Accordingly, Dyna2Music decomposes joint velocities into slow and fast components and hierarchically fuses the resulting part-wise motion energy with pretrained joint features to condition music generation. To complement this representation, we introduce latent dynamics consistency (LDC), an auxiliary objective that matches adjacent-frame change magnitudes between a single-step clean-latent estimate and the paired reference. LDC makes local music-latent variation an explicit training target without adding trainable parameters or inference computation. Dyna2Music supports variable-length music generation, and experiments on AIST++ and TikTok demonstrate improved rhythmic alignment and audio quality over representative D2M baselines.

</details>


### [164] [RMS-AQA: A Two-Stage Spatial Audio Question Answering Benchmark for Real-World Domestic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00935)
*Peihao Chen,Qing Wang,Lichun Fan,Yufeng Hao,Zhifeng Kong,Mengyao Zhu,Hengyi Hong,Hang Chen,Hang Su,Yujie Jian,Chao-Han Huck Yang,Shichao Hu,Jun Du,Jian Luan,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 RMS-AQA，一个面向真实家庭环境的空间音频问答（SAQA）基准，采用两阶段 QA 格式评估音频语言模型的事件定位与时空推理能力，并配套轻量空间插件将 FOA 数据注入冻结的音频语言骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中的具身助手需要推断“发生了什么、何时何地发生、如何响应”，但现有基准缺乏对空间音频与复杂时空推理相结合的系统评估手段。

Method: 构建 RMS-AQA 基准：采用两阶段 QA 格式（先定位可听声事件，再基于定位进行复杂时空推理）；数据集融合真实一阶 Ambisonics（FOA）录音与基于实测房间脉冲响应（RIR）生成的高保真合成数据；并提出轻量空间插件，将 FOA 格式数据注入冻结的音频语言骨干模型。

Result: 实验结果显示，主要挑战来自并发声源、远距离，以及 RIR 合成数据与真实录音之间的仿真到现实域差。

Conclusion: RMS-AQA 为真实家庭场景下的空间音频问答提供了兼具声学真实性与评估全面性的基准，并指明了当前音频语言模型在空间听觉理解上的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Embodied assistants in domestic environments must infer what happened, where and when it occurred, and how to respond. To address this, we introduce RMS-AQA, a spatial audio question answering (SAQA) benchmark for real-world domestic environments. The benchmark features a two-stage question-answering (QA) format to comprehensively assess the ability of audio-language models (ALMs) to first ground audible sound events and subsequently perform complex spatio-temporal reasoning based on that grounding. To maximize acoustic realism, our dataset combines authentic real-world first-order Ambisonics (FOA) recordings with high-fidelity synthetic data generated using measured room impulse responses (RIRs). Furthermore, we provide a lightweight spatial plug-in that injects FOA-format data into frozen audio-language backbones. Experimental results reveal that the primary challenges stem from concurrent sources, far distance, and sim-to-real domain gap between RIR-synthesized and authentic recordings.

</details>


### [165] [Toward Elastic Speech Inference: Training-Free Wake-Word Detection from Pretrained ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01182)
*Hwayeon Kim,Youngwon Choi,Hyeonyu Kim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文探索无需梯度微调，直接利用共享预训练ASR骨干进行唤醒词检测，并用SliceGPT的PCA结构化剪枝提取紧凑编码器；实验表明编码器通道维度缩减50%时性能仍相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需额外训练或任务专用模块，将预训练ASR模型适配到唤醒词检测等前端功能，成本较高。作者希望验证能否在不进行梯度微调的情况下复用共享ASR骨干，并进一步压缩模型。

Method: 以Parakeet-TDT-0.6B-v3与Moonshine-base为对象，不进行梯度微调，直接用于唤醒词检测；采用SliceGPT的PCA结构化剪枝方法，尝试从预训练ASR模型中提取紧凑编码器。

Result: 在编码器通道维度减少50%的情况下，唤醒词检测性能保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 任务相关的紧凑编码器可以从预训练ASR模型中导出，且无需微调，说明共享ASR骨干在唤醒词检测等前端任务上具有可复用性与可压缩性。

Abstract: Recent ASR development has placed growing emphasis on generalization across diverse domains and acoustic conditions. Existing approaches typically adapt pretrained ASR models to front-end functions such as wake-up word (WuW) detection through additional training or task-specific modules. In this work, we explore the use of a shared pretrained ASR backbone for WuW detection without gradient-based fine-tuning and examine whether a compact encoder can be extracted using the PCA-based structured pruning approach of SliceGPT. Experiments with Parakeet-TDT-0.6B-v3 and Moonshine-base show that WuW detection performance remains relatively stable when the encoder channel dimension is reduced by 50%. These results suggest that task-relevant compact encoders can be derived from pretrained ASR models without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [166] [AudioJev: Direct Audio Decisions with Order-Calibrated Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01293)
*Sihan Lv,Zhen Li,Zhiqi Cao,Jinshan Zhang,Ying Li,Meng Xi,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: AudioJev 将音频波形、问题与候选选项直接映射为候选分布，提出"顺序校准"目标，通过随机错排 SKL 训练消除选项排列对概率的影响，在 MMAU/MMAR 上取得 68.88%/55.33% 准确率并显著降低顺序敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频决策往往依赖转录文本无法保留的证据，且其概率估计常受答案选项排列顺序影响。既有方法多依赖转录或多视图集成，难以直接建模音频证据，也缺乏对选项位置不变性的显式约束。

Method: 提出单模型全参数框架 AudioJev：输入波形、问题与给定候选，经共享的单一全参数模型直接输出候选分布。定义"顺序校准"为在语义保持的选项置换下保持答案概率不变。采用随机错排 SKL 训练：为每个问题构造一个所有选项位置均改变的错排视图，对两个视图的答案均施加监督，并在应用对称 KL 惩罚前对齐两个分布。推理仅需一次候选打分前向，无需校准头或顺序集成。

Result: 在三个训练种子下，AudioJev 在完整 MMAU/MMAR 上平均准确率分别为 68.88%/55.33%；相对于单视图移除消融，随机顺序 SKL 分别降低 43.8%/60.5%。同一模型可处理意图、环境声、音符属性、语音活动与对话转换等任务。配对移除消融与多顺序评估同时衡量预测准确率与概率稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了直接的音频接口，其校准目标作用于候选语义而非呈现位置，兼顾准确率与概率稳定性。推理代码与模型权重已公开。

Abstract: Audio decisions often depend on evidence that a transcript does not preserve, while their probability estimates can depend on how answer options are ordered. AudioJev maps a waveform, question and supplied alternatives directly to a candidate distribution through one shared full-parameter model. We define order calibration as preserving an answer's probability under meaning-preserving option permutations. Random-derangement SKL training pairs each question with a reordered view in which every alternative changes position, supervises both answers, and aligns the two distributions before applying a symmetric KL penalty. Inference retains a single candidate-scoring forward, with no calibration head or order ensemble. Across three training seeds, AudioJev reaches 68.88%/55.33% mean accuracy on complete MMAU/MMAR and reduces random-order SKL by 43.8%/60.5% relative to the single-view removal ablation. The same model handles intent, environmental sound, note properties, speech activity and conversational transitions. Paired removal ablations and multi-order evaluation measure predictive accuracy and probability stability together, establishing a direct audio interface whose calibration objective acts on candidate meaning rather than presentation position. Inference code and model weights are available at https://github.com/SihanLv/AudioJev-Inference and https://huggingface.co/shlv/AudioJev.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-Party Backchannel Prediction: a Diagnosis, a Benchmark, and a Ceiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01488)
*Mohammed Hafsati,Ahmed Loughzali*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于AMI语料的多人对话backchannel预测基准，发现当前最先进的双人对话模型零样本迁移至会议音频时仅达随机水平，但经由线性探针与重训练可显著提升，并揭示出对未见听者的泛化瓶颈及说话人身份信息的纠缠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有backchannel预测研究几乎全部局限于双人（dyadic）对话场景，缺乏面向多人会议场景的基准与系统评估，因此作者希望将研究拓展到多人对话，并检验现有双人模型的可迁移性与局限性。

Method: 作者基于AMI语料构建多人对话基准：包含来自171场会议、190位说话人的682个掩蔽听者视角、18,697个backchannel事件，并采用人物不相交（person-disjoint）的留出划分。评估上，将最先进的双人模型零样本迁移到会议音频，并进行线性探针与重训练；同时考察听者条件化、容量缩减、听者对抗训练、逐听者自适应与oracle词汇条件化等策略，并设置同模型对照实验（turn-onset预测 vs. backchannel预测），在AUROC之外补充报告听者活跃区域的event-F1。

Result: 双人SOTA模型零样本迁移表现仅为随机水平（AUROC 0.499）；其冻结声学特征仍具信息量，线性探针达0.704，重训练提升至0.751。重训练暴露出第二重局限：听者条件化仅对训练中见过的听者有效，对未见听者无效，且该差距在容量缩减、听者对抗训练、逐听者自适应及oracle词汇条件化下依然存在。对抗训练仅去除部分说话人身份信息，更强的去除反而损害预测性能。同模型对照显示：相同特征与数据划分下，turn-onset预测可迁移至未见听者，而backchannel预测不能。backchannel率在个体间的差异约为turn-onset率的两倍。

Conclusion: backchannel预测在多人场景中面临跨听者泛化困难，且说话人身份信息与有用线索相互纠缠，难以完全解耦；同时backchannel事件占比仅约1%，帧级F1受基率影响严重，故应结合AUROC与听者活跃区域event-F1进行评估。作者公开了基准与评估工具。

Abstract: Backchannel prediction has been studied almost entirely in dyadic conversation. We introduce a multi-party benchmark based on the AMI corpus, comprising 682 masked-listener views from 171 meetings, 190 speakers, and 18,697 backchannel events, with a person-disjoint held-out split. A state-of-the-art dyadic model applied zero-shot to meeting audio performs at chance (AUROC 0.499); nevertheless, its frozen acoustic features remain informative: a linear probe reaches 0.704, and retraining the predictor raises performance to 0.751. Retraining reveals a second limitation. Listener conditioning improves prediction for listeners seen during training but not for unseen listeners, and the gap remains under capacity reduction, listener-adversarial training, per-listener adaptation, and oracle lexical conditioning. Adversarial training removes only part of the speaker-identity information, while stronger removal hurts prediction, suggesting that identity is entangled with cues that are useful for backchanneling. A within-model control helps explain this pattern: with the same features and data splits, turn-onset prediction transfers to unseen listeners, while backchannel prediction does not. Backchannel rates also vary about twice as much across individuals as turn-onset rates. Since backchannels occupy only about 1% of frames, frame-level F1 is strongly affected by the base rate. We therefore report AUROC alongside event-F1 on listener-active regions. We release the benchmark and evaluation tools at https://github.com/HafsatiMohammed/bc_multiparty_release.

</details>


### [168] [Q-SPT: Learnable Query-Based Compression for Low-Frame-Rate Speech Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01492)
*Jeeyoung Yun,Seohwan Yun,Sungwoong Kim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: Q-SPT是一种低帧率双流语音分词器，采用可学习的、上下文感知的查询式压缩器分别处理语义与声学表示，在相同帧率下取得最佳重建效果，并在下游SLM中提升语音识别准确率和TTS感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经语音编解码器越来越多地被用作语音语言模型（SLM）的分词器。降低帧率可减少SLM的计算与内存开销，但难以同时保留语言信息和声学细节。现有方法依赖规则式压缩：平均池化会丢失语言信息，而基于相似度的合并使用相邻帧相似度的固定阈值，并将得到的边界直接应用于声学流，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 提出Q-SPT，一种低帧率双流语音分词器。其核心是为语义和声学表示分别设计独立的、上下文感知的、可学习的查询式压缩器。固定速率的查询分别以语义流和声学流作为独立的键值源进行注意力计算，从而实现流特定的、上下文感知的聚合。此外，引入自回归文本损失显式监督语义压缩器以保留语言信息。

Result: 实验表明，在相同帧率下，Q-SPT在所评估的编解码器中取得最佳重建性能；在下游SLM任务中，取得最佳语音识别准确率与TTS感知质量，并具有竞争力的可懂度。

Conclusion: 通过为语义与声学流分别引入可学习、上下文感知的查询式压缩器，并结合自回归文本损失监督，Q-SPT有效缓解了低帧率下语言信息与声学细节难以兼顾的问题，在重建与下游任务上均优于现有规则式压缩方法。

Abstract: Neural speech codecs increasingly serve as tokenizers for speech language models (SLMs). Lowering the frame rate reduces the computational and memory costs of SLMs, but makes it difficult to preserve both linguistic information and acoustic detail. Existing approaches rely on rule-based compression: average pooling can discard linguistic information, whereas similarity-based merging uses a fixed threshold on adjacent-frame similarity and applies the resulting boundaries to the acoustic stream. We propose Q-SPT, a low-frame-rate dual-stream speech tokenizer with separate, context-aware, learnable query-based compressors specialized for semantic and acoustic representations. In particular, queries at a fixed rate independently attend to the semantic and acoustic streams as separate key-value sources, enabling stream-specific, context-aware aggregation through two separately learned compressors. In addition, an autoregressive text loss explicitly supervises the semantic compressor to preserve linguistic information. Experimental results show that Q-SPT achieves the best reconstruction among the evaluated codecs at the same frame rate. In downstream SLMs, it yields the best speech recognition accuracy and text-to-speech perceptual quality with competitive intelligibility.

</details>


### [169] [Beyond Decodability: Do Acoustic Factors Drive Predictions in Speech-Based Alzheimer's Assessment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01846)
*Serli Kopar,Alkis Koudounas,Roshan P. Rane,Sam Gijsen,Paula A. Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本研究检验语音自监督学习(SSL)表征中的录音因素是否会系统性改变阿尔茨海默病(AD)预测，而非仅被编码。基于ADReSSo与三个大型SSL主干，通过噪声与混响干预、逐层线性解码、输入与表征空间干预及几何对齐分析发现，受控声学干预会跨所有主干改变AD预测，且噪声效应最强、结构化地相对于分类器决策方向，并在测试集上复现、方向反转时效应反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语音的AD评估日益依赖从原始音频直接学习声学表征的预训练SSL模型，这使模型暴露于录音因素。作者旨在回答：这些因素只是被编码进SSL表征中，还是能够系统性地改变模型预测，从而揭示高预测性能与无显著组间差异是否足以保证稳健性。

Method: 使用ADReSSo数据集与三个大型SSL主干，对仅参与者语音、非语音和完整录音施加受控的噪声与混响干预；结合逐层线性解码、输入空间与表征空间干预，以及几何对齐分析，以区分声学可解码性与对AD预测的影响。

Result: 受控声学干预在三个SSL主干上均改变了AD预测。噪声在原始数据中未表现出显著诊断组差异，却产生最强的干预效应。这些效应相对于分类器决策方向呈系统性结构，在留出测试集上复现，并在表征空间干预方向反转时随之反转。

Conclusion: 高预测性能与所测声学因素缺乏显著诊断组差异，并不足以保证模型稳健性。作者主张将基于干预的稳健性测试作为可信临床语音模型的标准流程。

Abstract: Speech-based Alzheimer's disease (AD) assessments increasingly rely on pretrained self-supervised learning (SSL) models that learn acoustic representations directly from raw audio, exposing the model to recording factors. We ask whether such factors are merely encoded in SSL representations or can systematically alter predictions. Using ADReSSo and three large SSL backbones, we apply controlled noise and reverberation interventions to participant-speech-only, non-speech, and full-recording audio. We combine layer-wise linear decoding, input- and representation-space interventions, and geometric alignment analysis to distinguish acoustic decodability from influence on AD prediction. Our results show that controlled acoustic interventions alter AD predictions across all three SSL backbones. Noise, despite showing no significant diagnostic-group difference in the original data, produces the strongest intervention effects. Importantly, these effects are systematically structured relative to the classifier's decision direction, replicate on the held-out test set and reverse when the representation-space intervention direction is reversed. Together, these findings show that high predictive performance and the absence of a significant diagnostic-group difference in a measured acoustic factor are not sufficient for robustness. We argue that intervention-based robustness tests should become standard for trustworthy clinical speech models.

</details>


### [170] [From Isolated Feature to Orbits: Discovering Music Concepts via Multi-SAE Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01864)
*Liwei Lin,Gus Xia*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该研究提出从"特征识别"转向"结构化分析"，利用音高移调作为归纳偏置，通过多视角SAE对齐诱导有序轨道，发现音乐基础模型内部以和弦、调性等结构化群组形式组织音高相关概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法（如探针、稀疏自编码器SAE）聚焦于识别孤立特征，并假设结构最小化；但音乐中的许多概念（如和弦、调性）本质上是结构化的关系集合（如和弦的12种移调、调内的自然音体系），而非孤立特征。因此需要理解音乐基础模型内部是否以有组织的结构形式表征这些概念。

Method: 提出一个以音高移调为归纳偏置的框架，通过多视角SAE对齐诱导有序轨道：生成移调后的输入对，对齐其SAE表示，从而发现音高相关特征的结构化群组（轨道结构）。仅需极少锚点示例即可解释整个概念族。

Result: 该方法在两个当前最优的音乐基础模型上均成功恢复出对应于和弦、调性和旋律模式的轨道结构，且仅需最小限度的锚点（少量示例）即可解释整个概念族。

Conclusion: 音乐基础模型的内部表示并非孤立特征，而是以结构化（轨道）形式组织音高相关概念；将移调作为归纳偏置、结合多视角SAE对齐，是理解音乐模型内部结构的有效途径。

Abstract: How can we understand what a music foundation model has learned \textit{internally}? Most interpretability approaches, such as probing and Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), focus on identifying individual features with minimal structural assumptions. We argue that many concepts are better understood as \textit{structured relations} rather than isolated features. This is especially prominent in music, where tonal structures are organized in the space of pitch and time. For example, concepts such as chords or keys are naturally expressed as structured sets (e.g., the 12 transpositions of a chord or the diatonic system within a key), rather than isolated features. In this study, \textbf{we shift from feature identification to structure-based analysis}, asking whether the learned inner representations of music foundation model emerge as organized structures over features. To this end, we introduce a framework that uses pitch transposition as an inductive bias to induce ordered orbits via multi-view SAE alignment. Concretely, we generate pitch-shifted input pairs and align their SAE representations to discover structured groups of pitch-related features. Experimental results show that this approach recovers orbit structures corresponding to chords, keys, and melodic patterns across two state-of-the-art music foundation models, while requiring only minimal grounding (e.g., a few anchor examples) to interpret entire concept families.

</details>


### [171] [LAST: Looped Audio Spectrogram Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01926)
*Haider Al-Tahan,Sean O'Brien,Anastasia Razdaibiedina,N. Apurva Ratan Murty*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: LAST通过复用Transformer块对类别token进行多轮精炼，在音频任务上以更少参数和计算量超过深层次序模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增加Transformer深度可提升识别性能，但每增加一层都会带来更多参数和计算开销，效率低下。作者希望将额外计算聚焦于整合已提取的特征，而非堆叠新层。
