Method: 提出Looped Audio Spectrogram Transformer（LAST）：先处理全部token，然后复用同一组块在固定的音频特征上仅精炼类别token，使后续轮次计算代价很低。

Result: 在AudioSet上，十轮LAST达到0.345 mAP，相对十二层顺序Transformer提升2.1%，同时参数减少49.4%、乘加运算减少42%、实测吞吐量提高9.8%。轮数从2增至10仅增加1.2%计算量即提升精度。对时间掩蔽及多种听觉增强更鲁棒，在音乐、环境与事件声音分类任务上泛化更好。

Conclusion: 复用块精炼类别token是一种高效替代深层堆叠的方案，可在更低参数与计算成本下提升性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Increasing depth of transformer models improves recognition, but it comes at a substantial cost. Each additional layer requires more parameters, which makes the process computationally inefficient. We ask whether additional processing can focus on integrating features already computed. Looped Audio Spectrogram Transformer (LAST) first processes all tokens, then reuses the same blocks to refine only the class token over fixed audio features, thereby making later passes inexpensive. On AudioSet, ten-pass LAST achieves 0.345 mean average precision, exceeding a twelve-layer sequential transformer by 2.1% relative with 49.4% fewer parameters, 42% fewer multiply-accumulate operations, and 9.8% higher measured throughput. Across separately trained models, increasing the pass count from two to ten improves accuracy while adding only 1.2% computation. Further evaluations show improved robustness to temporal masking and various other auditory augmentations, with better generalization on classification tasks with music, environmental, and event sounds.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [When Intent Arrives Late: A Benchmark for Full-Duplex Speech Models under Delayed Intent Revelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00272)
*Yang Xiao,Tianyi Peng,Hanyu Meng,Ting Dang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出LateIntent-Bench匹配对基准与Premature Response Rate指标，揭示原生全双工语音模型在意图延迟揭示下出现早响应与安全性选择性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型可在用户说完前响应，行为同时取决于最终话语与意图信息到达时机；现有基准只评估轮次机制，安全评估则假设生成前已观测完整请求，无法刻画延迟意图揭示下的时序风险。

Method: LateIntent-Bench采用匹配对设计：共享模糊前缀后接良性或有害续言，用受控停顿延迟分支可区分时刻；定义Premature Response Rate (PRR)衡量响应起始是否早于意图揭示；联合评估有害与良性参与以区分安全选择性变化与整体响应性损失。共3,136个会话，测试四种原生全双工模型，并做意图揭示后插入1.5s静音的干预实验。

Result: 四种模型呈现不同响应时序模式：三种在保持良性响应性的同时有害参与增加，一种对两类分支参与均下降；意图揭示后插入1.5s静音使PRR接近无停顿基线，并显著降低有害参与变化。

Conclusion: 仅评估完整指定请求无法捕捉全双工模型涌现的时序行为模式，必须将延迟意图揭示纳入评估体系；代码将公开。

Abstract: Native full-duplex speech models can respond before a user finishes speaking, making the behavior of the model depend on both the final utterance and when intent-defining information arrives. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate turn-taking mechanics, whereas safety evaluations typically assume that the complete request is observed prior to response generation. We introduce LateIntent-Bench, a matched-pair benchmark for delayed intent revelation. A shared ambiguous prefix precedes either a benign or a harmful continuation, using a controlled pause to delay when the branches become distinguishable. We define the Premature Response Rate (PRR) to measure whether response onset precedes the revelation of intent. Joint evaluation of harmful and benign engagement distinguishes changes in safety selectivity from general losses in responsiveness. Across 3,136 sessions, four native full-duplex models exhibit distinct response-timing patterns under delayed intent. Three models show increased harmful engagement while maintaining benign responsiveness, whereas one loses engagement with both branches. Inserting a 1.5s silence after intent revelation keeps PRR near the no-pause baseline and substantially reduces changes in harmful engagement. These results demonstrate that evaluating fully specified requests alone fails to capture emerging timing patterns, highlighting the necessity of assessing delayed intent revelation in full-duplex models. The code will be publicly released soon.

</details>


### [173] [Multi-agent Auditory Scene Analysis: Improved Localization Speed and Robustness by Multi-beamformed Speech Quality Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00538)
*Caleb Rascon*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种新的优化机制，用一组覆盖多个位置的音质估计序列替代单一音质估计，从而简化搜索空间，使实时听觉场景分析器（ASA）优化更快、更准确、更稳定且复杂度更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时听觉场景分析器（ASA）需完成声源定位、分离与分类。此前将其建模为多智能体系统，通过智能体间通信形成反馈回路以修正局部误差、提升鲁棒性并降低局部复杂度。但该方案的优化速度很慢，可能原因是仅依赖无参考音质估计器给出的逐窗单一音质估计，这些估计在相邻窗间波动大，导致搜索空间难以优化。

Method: 提出新的优化机制：不再依赖单一音质估计序列，而是使用在一系列位置上获得的多组音质估计集合，从而更清晰地刻画搜索空间并简化优化过程。将该机制集成到多智能体ASA中，用于实时校正语音源定位误差。

Result: 所提ASA优化时间显著缩短，精度更高，在真实声学场景下校正更高级别的定位误差时更稳定，且整体复杂度低于此前方法。唯一代价是音质估计智能体的响应时间增加，但完整ASA仍可实时运行。

Conclusion: 以多组位置音质估计替代单一估计可有效改善ASA优化性能；结果再次证明将ASA建模为多智能体系统的优势。

Abstract: A real-time auditory scene analyzer (ASA) aims to carry out the tasks of locating, separating and classifying the sound sources present in a given acoustic environment. Recently, an effort has been made into modelling an ASA as a multi-agent system, with each one of its agents performing one of the aforementioned tasks and communicating their results to the rest of their peer agents. These communication routes are used as feedback loops to fix local errors at a global level, providing robustness while reducing local complexity. An example of the benefits of this approach is the optimization of speech quality by correcting in real-time the estimated location of the speech source of interest. However, their optimization speed has been shown to be considerably slow. One possible reason is that it solely relies on a series of single quality estimations (provided by a reference-free quality estimator model) that vary considerably from one window to the next, which results in a difficult search space to optimize. In this work, a new optimization mechanism is proposed that instead relies on a series of sets of quality estimations over a range of locations, providing a clearer view of the search space, simplifying its optimization. The proposed ASA now has a considerably smaller optimization time, is more accurate, and is more stable when being evaluated in real-life acoustic scenarios to correct higher levels of localization errors, all while being less complex than previous efforts. The only trade-off is that there is an increase in the response time of the quality estimation agent, but the complete ASA is still able to run in real-time. The performance shown in this work again shows the benefits of modelling an ASA as a multi-agent system.

</details>


### [174] [End-to-End Historical Music Restoration in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00607)
*Steven Cho,Junghyun Koo,Raphael Lafargue,Tushar Dhyani,Eloi Moliner,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一个监督式端到端管弦乐历史音乐修复(HMR)基准，通过更忠实地模拟历史录音退化链合成配对数据，并用潜空间流匹配模型完成修复，性能超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史音乐修复(HMR)研究几乎只关注超分辨率或独奏作品等受限问题，对多乐器的管弦乐历史音乐修复这一通用任务探索不足。主要原因在于该领域针对早期20世纪录音，缺乏退化前的真实配对数据，使修复成为无监督且更具挑战的任务。

Method: 探索合成退化函数与端到端生成式深度学习修复方法，构建监督式端到端管弦乐HMR基准；比以往工作更忠实地模拟历史录音的退化链，将管弦乐修复转化为可处理的监督问题；在合成配对上训练潜空间流匹配(latent flow-matching)模型。

Result: 在客观侵入式(intrusive)、非侵入式(non-intrusive)以及主观评价上，该方法均优于现有HMR基线；同时整理并发布了一个9.3小时、免版权、非配对的古典音乐历史录音测试集，以及代码和音频演示。

Conclusion: 通过忠实的退化链模拟将无监督的管弦乐历史音乐修复转化为监督问题，并借助潜空间流匹配模型取得了当前最优表现；所发布的数据集、代码与演示为管弦乐HMR的后续研究提供了基准与资源。

Abstract: Historical music restoration (HMR) has almost exclusively focused on constrained problems such as Super-Resolution or the restoration of solo pieces, under-exploring the general task of restoring orchestral historical music, which has multiple instruments. This under-exploration is largely because the HMR domain, early-20th-century recordings, has no pre-degradation ground-truth pairs, making the restoration task unsupervised and more challenging. This paper presents a supervised end-to-end orchestral HMR benchmark by exploring both the synthetic degradation functions and the end-to-end generative deep-learning restoration methods. We simulate the historical recording degradation chain more faithfully than prior work, which makes orchestral restoration into a tractable supervised problem. A latent flow-matching model trained on the resulting synthetic pairs outperforms existing HMR baselines on intrusive, non-intrusive, and subjective evaluations. We also curate and release a 9.3-hour license-free, unpaired, historical classical-music test set, along with code and audio demos.

</details>


### [175] [Interpretable Destination-Aware synthesizer Modulation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00642)
*David Liu,Giulio Cengarle,David Cooper,Mark Vinton,Haici Yang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出可解释的目的地感知调制与波形恢复流程，从干净音频重建可迁移至现代合成器的调制参数，通过可微分合成器与感知损失/对抗训练提升感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成器编程困难，尤其是调制配置（LFO 对音高、滤波截止、振荡器电平的时变控制）。已有工作虽能从干净音频通过曲线匹配重建调制，但恢复的参数往往无法迁移到现代合成器。

Method: 模仿音乐人重建音色的流程：先识别被调制的目的地；再恢复每个目的地对应的 LFO 形状；并估计振荡器波形以匹配源音色。训练依托可微分合成器，加入噪声振荡器与更多调制选项；在感知质量上做优化，探索多种感知损失与对抗训练，提出 Gammatone 损失与 CQT 判别器。

Result: 客观与主观评测表明：正确预测调制目的地至关重要；模型在面向调制的逆合成任务上表现优异；Gammatone 损失与 CQT 判别器在提升感知质量上显著优于传统 MSS 损失。

Conclusion: 目的地感知的可解释调制/波形恢复流程能有效完成调制聚焦的逆合成，并可迁移至现代合成器；感知导向的损失与对抗设计对音质提升关键。论文提供主观测试音频样本。

Abstract: Synthesizer programming is a challenging task, particularly in configuring modulation: the time-varying control of parameters such as pitch, filter cutoff, and oscillator level by modulators such as Low-Frequency Oscillators (LFOs). Prior work has shown that modulation can be reconstructed from the clean audio through curve matching, yet the recovered parameters are often untransferable to modern synthesizers. We present an interpretable destination-aware modulation and waveform recovery pipeline that mirrors how musicians often recreate sounds on a synthesizer. First, the system identifies the modulated destinations; then it recovers the LFO shape associated with each destination, and estimates the oscillator waveform to match the source timbre. The training is supported by a differentiable synthesizer that incorporates a noise oscillator, and more modulation options than previous work. We carefully train the models for optimal perceptual quality, with extensive exploration of perceptual losses and adversarial training. Through objective and subjective evaluation, we show that predicting the destinations correctly is essential, our model excels in the modulation focused inverse synthesis tasks, and our Gammatone loss and CQT discriminator significantly outperform the traditionally-used MSS loss in improving perceptual quality. We provide audio samples from the subjective test.

</details>


### [176] [Silence-the-Mimic: Accelerating Imperceptible Perturbation Generation Against Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00662)
*Runqiu Xu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于心理声学掩蔽约束的频域对抗扰动方法（STM），用于快速保护说话人身份，防止VC/TTS模型进行未授权声音克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络驱动的语音转换（VC）与文本到语音（TTS）模型发展迅速，仅需少量数据即可实现逼真的声音克隆，带来了说话人身份被未授权克隆的隐私与安全风险。现有的不可感知对抗保护方法依赖对超参数高度敏感的质量控制损失，且因迭代优化过程导致计算开销大。

Method: 提出一种快速保护方法，在频域中生成满足心理声学掩蔽（psychoacoustic masking）约束的感知受限扰动。该方法在对抗训练过程中严格强制感知界限，从而无需迭代式的质量平衡调参，并显著降低计算成本。

Result: 在多个先进VC和TTS模型上的实验表明，STM在保护性能上达到与现有白盒基线相当或更优的水平，同时具有显著更好的感知质量，并实现最高45.3倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，结合感知约束的频域扰动是一种实用且有效的防声音克隆保护范式。

Abstract: Deep neural network-based Voice Conversion (VC) and Text-to-Speech (TTS) models have rapidly advanced, enabling realistic voice cloning with minimal input data. Such capabilities raise serious concerns over unauthorized cloning of speaker identities and the associated privacy and security risks. Current imperceptible adversarial protection methods rely on quality control losses that are highly sensitive to hyperparameter tuning and computationally expensive due to lengthy optimization. To address these limitations, we propose a fast protection method that generates perceptually constrained perturbations in the frequency domain under a psychoacoustic masking-based constraint. Our approach strictly enforces perceptibility bounds during adversarial training, eliminating the need for iterative quality balancing and significantly reducing computational cost. Experiments on multiple state-of-the-art VC and TTS models show that STM achieves competitive or superior protection performance with substantially better perceptual quality and up to $45.3\times$ speedup over existing white-box baselines. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of frequency-domain perturbations with perceptual constraints as a practical paradigm for protecting against voice cloning.

</details>


### [177] [Frequency-Weighted Soft-Constrained Spatially Selective Active Noise Control for Open-Fitting Hearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00721)
*Tong Xiao,Reinhild Roden,Matthias Blau,Simon Doclo*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出频率相关加权的软约束空间选择性主动噪声控制方法，在保持低延迟时域控制的同时，用LTASS和oracle加权实现更优的语音可懂度与音质折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的软约束SSANC仅用单一标量折中参数在所有频率上平衡降噪与目标语音保留，缺乏频率选择性，难以兼顾语音可懂度与失真。

Method: 将软约束SSANC推广为对目标语音保留误差进行频率相关加权，保持低延迟时域主动控制；比较基于SII、MIRS、LTASS及信号相关oracle的加权与标量加权，并在多说话人场景下用实测声学冲激响应评估。

Result: LTASS与oracle加权带来最大语音可懂度提升，并在显著更低失真水平下获得与SII、MIRS加权相当的语音质量提升；oracle加权整体感知折中最佳，LTASS加权在不需干净目标语音信号的条件下可达到相近的感知折中。

Conclusion: 频率相关加权优于标量加权，LTASS加权因无需参考干净语音而更具实用价值。

Abstract: Recently, soft-constrained spatially selective active noise control (SSANC) for open-fitting hearables has been proposed to enable a flexible trade-off between noise reduction and desired-speech preservation using a scalar trade-off parameter across all frequencies. This paper generalizes soft-constrained SSANC by introducing frequency-dependent weighting of the desired-speech preservation error while maintaining low-delay time-domain active control. Frequency-dependent weightings based on the speech intelligibility index (SII), the modified intermediate reference system (MIRS), the long-term average speech spectrum (LTASS), and a signal-dependent oracle are compared with scalar weighting using measured acoustic impulse responses in a multi-speaker scenario. The results show that LTASS and oracle weightings provide the largest speech-intelligibility improvements and comparable speech-quality improvements at substantially lower distortion levels than those obtained with SII and MIRS weightings. While the oracle weighting provides the best overall perceptual trade-off, LTASS weighting provides a comparable perceptual trade-off without requiring the clean desired-speech signal.

</details>


### [178] [Articulatory Source-Filter TTS: Physically Grounded Control through Vocal Tract Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00735)
*Jesuraj Bandekar,Shinji Watanabe,Prasanta Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于发音运动学的可解释语音合成（TTS）架构，通过声学-发音反演模型为大规模语料生成运动学伪轨迹，进而分别驱动声道滤波器和声门源，并利用最优传输条件流匹配模块精修源信号，在保持可懂度与自然度的同时实现源-滤波器解耦与细粒度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经TTS系统虽合成质量高，但呈"黑箱"特性，对声道滤波器的控制缺乏可解释性。作者希望引入发音运动学，实现显式、可解释的源-滤波器分离与控制。

Method: 1) 构建由发音运动学驱动的源-滤波器TTS架构；2) 采用大规模预训练表征增强的声学-发音反演（AAI）模型，为大规模TTS语料生成运动学伪轨迹；3) 用这些轨迹条件化滤波器响应，用预测的基频与能量轮廓参数化声门源；4) 源与滤波器独立预测，源经最优传输条件流匹配（OT-CFM）模块精修后与滤波器重组为最终频谱。

Result: 在可懂度与自然度上与同等规模基线相当，仅以适度的频谱保真度代价换取显式控制能力。评测显示源-滤波器实现清晰解耦，支持稳定的韵律缩放与跨说话人源/滤波器重组（F0保持源说话人特性，声道特性随滤波器说话人）。直接操控发音轨迹可实现口音修改等细粒度控制。

Conclusion: 该工作为可解释语音合成提供了新方向：以发音运动学为中间表征，实现源-滤波器解耦与细粒度可控合成，在质量与控制性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Modern neural text-to-speech (TTS) systems achieve remarkable acoustic fidelity but act as black boxes, offering little interpretable control over the vocal tract filter. We propose a controllable source-filter TTS architecture grounded in articulatory kinematics. An Acoustic-to-Articulatory Inversion (AAI) model, enhanced by large-scale pretrained representations, generates kinematic pseudo-trajectories for a large TTS corpus. These trajectories condition the filter response, while predicted pitch and energy contours parameterise the glottal source. Source and filter are predicted independently, and the source is refined by an Optimal Transport Conditional Flow Matching (OT-CFM) module before recombination into the final spectrogram. Our model achieves intelligibility and naturalness competitive with similarly sized baselines, with only a modest spectral fidelity cost in exchange for explicit control. Evaluations reveal clear source-filter disentanglement, enabling stable prosodic scaling and cross-speaker source/filter recombination, where F0 remains tied to the source speaker while vocal-tract characteristics follow the filter speaker. Finally, direct manipulation of articulatory trajectories enables fine-grained control, such as accent modification, offering a new direction for interpretable speech synthesis. Audio samples: https://coding-phoenix-12.github.io/ArticulatorySFTTS/

</details>


### [179] [MAV-C: A Framework for the Joint Objective Estimation of Audio-Visual Complexity in Immersive Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00754)
*Luca Resti,Amelia Gully,Michael McLoughlin,Gavin Kearney,Alena Denisova*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出无参考音视频联合复杂度度量MAV-C，融合熵类音频特征与视觉特征输出连续复杂度分数CAV∈[0,1]，在合成与游戏数据集上验证其有效性与模态独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复杂度估计多针对单一模态，缺乏对音视频联合复杂度的无参考客观度量，且难以区分复杂度来源是音频还是视觉维度。

Method: 以熵类音频特征（时间、频谱、空间）与视觉特征（Sobel梯度幅值、色度唯一性、光流）为输入，经参数化融合阶段生成联合复杂度分数CAV(t)∈[0,1]；在合成语料SYN与SAFEPLAY-X的60段游戏语料GAM上验证，含单轴特征扫描、OFAT敏感性分析与跨模态不敏感性评估。

Result: SYN上音频分CA与视觉分CV对相反模态变化不敏感（CoV<0.003），CAV覆盖[0.00,0.90]，单轴扫描单调（Spearman高达0.995）；GAM上CV在内容类别间差异显著（Kruskal-Wallis p=0.021）而CA不显著，二者不相关（r=0.03）；OFAT识别出以模态平衡wa与视觉正则v为主的两层参数层级。

Conclusion: MAV-C可作为无参考音视频联合复杂度度量，两子分数作用于独立信号维度；当前仅为客观验证，完整主观校准留作未来工作。

Abstract: We present the Motion-Aware Audio-Visual Complexity Metric (MAV-C), a reference-free framework for the joint objective estimation of audio-visual complexity. The metric combines entropy-based audio features (temporal, spectral, and spatial) with visual features (Sobel gradient magnitude, chromatic uniqueness, and optical flow) via a parametric fusion stage, producing a continuous joint complexity score CAV (t) [0,1]. We validate MAV-C on two datasets: a controlled synthetic corpus (SYN) of stimuli with known signal characteristics and a naturalistic gameplay corpus (GAM) of 60 clips drawn from the SAFEPLAY-X dataset. On SYN, the metric exhibits strong validity: the audio score CA and visual score CV are each insensitive to changes in the opposite modality (CoV < 0.003), the joint score CAV spans [0.00,0.90] across all parameter combinations, and single-axis feature sweeps produce monotone trajectories (Spearman up to 0.995). On GAM, CV differs significantly across content categories (Kruskal-Wallis p = 0.021) while CA does not, and the two sub-scores are uncorrelated (r = 0.03), confirming they operate on independent signal dimensions. OFAT sensitivity analysis identifies a two-tier parameter hierarchy, with modality balance (wa) and visual regularization (v) as most significant tunable parameters. Full subjective calibration is planned as future work.

</details>


### [180] [ParaCalib: Semantically Calibrated Paralinguistic Modeling for Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01103)
*Yuxin Li,Yifei Li,Yi-Wen Chao,Xiangyu Zhang,Eng Siong Chng,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出ParaCalib框架，利用音频-语言模型与LLM将副语言行为在语句级语义语境下进行语义校准，映射为结构化SC-Para表示，在DAIC-WOZ和MODMA上取得最高Macro-F1（71.9%和90.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抑郁检测方法将声学线索视为与语境无关的标记，难以区分声音形式与其依赖语境的交际功能，导致副语言行为解释缺乏语义校准。

Method: ParaCalib使用音频-语言模型（ALM）生成语境化的声音描述，并通过基于LLM的副语言状态提取器（PSE）将这些描述映射到结构化的"语义校准副语言（SC-Para）"表示中，从而在可比较的状态空间内刻画副语言行为。

Result: 在所评估方法中取得最高平均Macro-F1，DAIC-WOZ达71.9%，MODMA达90.5%；受控分析显示在固定字幕级声学描述下，改变伴随语义语境会改变推断出的抑郁相关副语言证据；探索性分析发现与抑郁标签相关的副语言状态呈多种反复出现的配置，而非单一主导状态。

Conclusion: 语义校准对副语言抑郁检测具有关键作用，副语言状态与抑郁的关联体现为多配置模式而非单一主导状态。

Abstract: Vocal behavior provides important signals for speech-based depression detection, but its interpretation often depends on what is being said and how it functions in context. However, existing methods typically treat acoustic cues as context-independent markers, making it difficult to distinguish vocal form from its context-dependent communicative function. We propose ParaCalib, a framework that semantically calibrates paralinguistic behavior by interpreting vocal patterns relative to utterance-level semantic context and inferred communicative function and representing them in a comparable state space. Concretely, ParaCalib uses an Audio-Language Model (ALM) to generate contextualized vocal descriptions and an LLM-based Paralinguistic State Extractor (PSE) to map these descriptions into a structured Semantically Calibrated Paralinguistic (SC-Para) representation. ParaCalib achieves the highest mean Macro-F1 among the evaluated methods, reaching 71.9\% on DAIC-WOZ and 90.5\% on MODMA. Our controlled analysis provides direct evidence of semantic calibration at the PSE stage: under a fixed caption-level acoustic description, varying the accompanying semantic context changes the inferred depression-related paralinguistic evidence. Our exploratory analysis further identifies recurring configurations of paralinguistic states associated with depression labels rather than a single uniformly dominant state.

</details>


### [181] [FedCFM: Federated Continual Domain Generalization for Fake Speech Detection via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01242)
*Yingjian Yu,Haiyan Guo,Tianshun Wang,Xinzhou Xu,Zirui Ge,Chi Liu,Ziheng Liu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出FedCFM——一种基于条件流匹配（CFM）的联邦持续域泛化框架，用于虚假语音检测；客户端各训练CFM生成器建模伪造类型特定嵌入分布，跨客户端生成器交换以合成未见伪造类型嵌入，通过生成式回放与知识蒸馏持续更新分类器，无需共享原始语音数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假语音检测（FSD）模型在真实部署中对泛化能力要求高。现有方法存在两类局限：一是多数据集协同训练依赖固定训练集，无法适应新出现的伪造类型；二是虽已探索持续学习，但多数方法忽视单个设备上的数据存储受限问题，实用性受限。因此需要一种在分布式客户端间、面对多样且演化攻击、无需共享原始语音数据、且适应存储受限场景的协作方案。

Method: 提出FedCFM，联邦持续域泛化框架，核心采用条件流匹配（CFM）。每个客户端训练一个CFM生成器以建模伪造类型特定的嵌入分布；通过跨客户端生成器交换，合成未见伪造类型的嵌入；借助生成式回放与知识蒸馏实现分类器的持续更新。整个过程不共享原始语音数据。

Result: 在相同训练数据集下，FedCFM取得比所评估的集中式和联邦域泛化基线更低的EER，展现出强跨域泛化能力。代码将于 https://github.com/jspycpp/FedCFM 发布。

Conclusion: FedCFM通过联邦持续域泛化与条件流匹配生成器的交换与回放，在保护数据隐私、适应存储受限场景的同时，有效提升虚假语音检测对未见及新兴伪造类型的跨域泛化性能。

Abstract: The generalization ability of Fake Speech Detection (FSD) models is crucial for real-world deployment. Existing multi-dataset co-training methods rely on fixed training sets and cannot adapt to emerging spoofing types. Although con-tinual learning has been explored, many approaches overlook limited data storage at individual devices, thereby restricting practical applicability. To address this, we propose FedCFM, a Federated continual domain generalization framework via Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) for collaboration without sharing raw speech data across distributed clients facing diverse and evolving spoofing attacks. Each client trains a CFM-based generator to model spoof-type-specific embedding distributions, and cross-client generator exchange enables synthesis of unseen spoof-type embeddings for continual classifier updating through generative replay and knowledge distillation. With the same training datasets, FedCFM achieves lower EER than the eval-uated centralized and federated domain generalization baselines, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Code will be released on https://github.com/jspycpp/FedCFM.

</details>


### [182] [A Federated Deepfake Speech Detection Method Based on Layer-Wise Center-Guided Weighting Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01259)
*Yingjian Yu,Haiyan Guo,Tianshun Wang,Zirui Ge,Chi Liu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出联邦深度伪造语音检测方法FedDSD，结合FedProx与逐层中心引导加权聚合(L-CGWA)，在不共享原始音频的前提下实现去中心化协同训练，检测性能接近集中式训练并具跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的语音合成技术使深度伪造语音日益多样，对声纹认证构成威胁。集中式深度伪造语音检测(DSD)虽有效，但计算资源消耗大且存在隐私问题，因此需要一种去中心化、保护隐私的协同训练方案。

Method: 提出联邦DSD(FedDSD)框架：各客户端在本地数据上训练模型，采用FedProx算法缓解数据异构性，并仅上传模型参数至中央服务器。进一步提出逐层中心引导加权聚合(L-CGWA)策略，依据各客户端到参考中心的距离逐层调整其聚合贡献，以刻画客户端间与层间差异，提升聚合鲁棒性。

Result: FedDSD训练所得模型的等错误率(EER)与集中式协同训练相当，同时显著优于在单一语料上训练的模型；且在多类跨域数据集上表现出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: FedDSD在保护数据隐私、降低集中式训练资源需求的同时，实现了与集中式训练相当的检测性能与良好的跨域泛化，为去中心化深度伪造语音检测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The advancement of deep learning-based speech synthesis has significantly increased the diversity of deepfake speech, posing threats to voice authentication. While centralized training is effective for deepfake speech detection (DSD), it requires considerable computational resources and raises privacy concerns. To address these issues, we propose a Federated DSD (FedDSD) method that enables collaborative model training across decentralized speech datasets without sharing raw audio. Specifically, each client trains a local model using the FedProx algorithm to mitigate the effects of data heterogeneity and uploads model parameters to a central server. To improve global model aggregation, we further propose a layer-wise center-guided weighting aggregation (L-CGWA) strategy that adjusts each client's contribution per layer based on its distance to a reference center, capturing inter-client and inter-layer discrepancies and enhancing the robustness of model aggregation. Experimental results demonstrate that models trained under the proposed FedDSD method achieve equal error rates (EERs) comparable to those obtained via centralized co-training, while significantly out-performing models trained on individual corpora. Furthermore, the proposed FedDSD method demonstrates robust generalization capabilities across diverse cross-domain datasets.

</details>


### [183] [PADP: Perceptual Audio Data Perturbation for Probing Perception Awareness in Audio Quality Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01405)
*Guanxin Jiang,Andreas Brendel,Pablo M. Delgado,Jürgen Herre*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一系列音频变换方法（PADP），在保持人耳感知质量不变的前提下大幅改变波形，并将其用作音频质量模型的感知压力测试，揭示了SOTA客观质量模型与基础模型在感知对齐上的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有客观音频质量模型声称具备感知动机，但其是否真正契合人类听觉感知尚缺乏系统检验。论文借助人耳对某些细粒度信号变化不敏感的特性，构造"感知上不可察觉但波形变化显著"的扰动，以此作为压力测试手段，检验模型的感知鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Perceptual Audio Data Perturbation（PADP），利用人类听觉系统对特定细粒度信号变化的低敏感性，在保持感知音频质量与内容的前提下显著改变波形。通过受控听音测试评估PADP的可听性并选取参数，确保对非关键与关键测试项均达到透明或近透明质量；随后用这些变换探测SOTA感知驱动型客观音频质量模型及基础模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 受控听音测试表明PADP可实现对关键与非关键项目的透明或近透明感知质量；测试结果揭示模型响应与人类听觉感知之间存在错位，说明这些模型对部分所提变换缺乏感知意识，鲁棒性不足。

Conclusion: PADP可作为有效的感知压力测试工具，暴露当前客观音频质量模型与基础模型在感知对齐方面的局限，为后续构建更贴合人类听觉感知的模型提供了评测手段与研究依据。

Abstract: This paper presents a collection of audio transforms that introduce perceptually irrelevant distortions and demonstrates their use as perceptual stress tests for audio quality models. We refer to these methods as Perceptual Audio Data Perturbation (PADP). PADP exploits the insensitivity of the human auditory system to certain fine-grained signal variations to substantially alter the waveform while preserving perceived audio quality and content. The audibility of PADP and the selection of its parameters are evaluated through controlled listening tests, ensuring that the transformations achieve transparent or near-transparent quality for both non-critical and critical items. We further probe the robustness of state-of-the-art (SOTA) perception-motivated objective audio quality models and foundation models. The results reveal a misalignment between model responses and human auditory perception, highlighting the limited perceptual awareness of these models for certain proposed transforms.

</details>


### [184] [Code-Switching Spoken Language Identification as Multi-Label Set Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01450)
*Shunsuke Mitsumori,Matthew Wiesner,Shigeo Morishima,Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出语码转换感知的语种识别（CS-LID），将其建模为多标签语言集合预测任务，并提出直接输出语句所含语言的集合生成器，与原子对分类和基于分数的分类基线进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语音语料库用单语LID过滤器筛选，但语码转换（CS）语音会漏过这些过滤器，因此需要CS感知的LID（CS-LID）。

Method: 将语句级CS-LID形式化为多标签语言集预测；提出集合生成器，直接输出一条语句中的语言；并将其与原子对分类基线和基于分数的分类基线进行对比。

Result: Oracle Top-k 是最强基线，但阈值方法失败，因为没有单一阈值能把CS语音与单语语音分开。集合生成器无需假设语言数量，能在未见语言对上预测正确的语言数量，但在精确集合准确率上仍低于oracle Top-k。

Conclusion: 分析指出稳健CS-LID的关键障碍：oracle基数、阈值不稳定性、CS训练数据中的语言偏置，以及合成到真实的差距。

Abstract: Code-switched (CS) speech leaks through the monolingual language identification (LID) filters used to curate massive speech corpora, calling for CS-aware LID (CS-LID). We formulate utterance-level CS-LID as multi-label language-set prediction and propose a set generator that directly outputs the languages in an utterance, comparing it against atomic-pair and score-based classification baselines. Oracle Top-k is the strongest baseline, but thresholding fails because no single threshold separates CS from monolingual speech. Our set generator predicts the correct language count on unseen pairs without assuming the number of languages, but underperforms oracle Top-k in exact set accuracy. Our analysis identifies the key obstacles to robust CS-LID: oracle cardinality, threshold instability, language bias in CS training data, and the synthetic-to-real gap.

</details>


### [185] [Teaching LLMs to Hear Who Spoke What: Metadata-Supervised Pretraining for Encoder-Free Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01695)
*Mohan Shi,Ruchao Fan,Sunit Sivasankaran,Keqi Deng,Jinyu Li*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向无编码器语音大模型的元数据监督预训练（MSP）策略，结合说话人感知话语组合（SAUC）与随机跨度掩码，验证无编码器架构在多说话人场景下可媲美甚至超越使用大规模预训练语音编码器的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于编码器的语音大模型（Speech-LLMs）通常采用预训练语音编码器，其偏向语言内容而可能丢失对说话人区分与副语言理解至关重要的细粒度声学线索。无编码器方案直接将梅尔频谱特征映射进LLM输入空间，可保留低层声学信息，但其系统化预训练策略尚缺乏探索，难以弥补缺少大规模预训练语音编码器的不足。

Method: 提出元数据监督预训练（MSP），利用说话人身份、情感等语音属性赋予模型说话人区分与副语言能力；引入说话人感知话语组合（SAUC）增强说话人区分能力；并采用随机跨度掩码对预训练进行正则化。模型为无编码器架构，将梅尔频谱经轻量嵌入层直接映射至LLM输入空间。

Result: 在同等训练数据条件下，无编码器模型优于随机初始化的基于编码器对照模型；在有限元数据标注数据下，其表现可与在更大规模语料上预训练语音编码器的模型相竞争，并在多个设定中超越后者。主评估任务为多说话人对话中的联合ASR与说话人日志，辅以副语言语音理解任务实验。

Conclusion: 结果表明无编码器架构在构建原生多模态大语言模型方面具有潜力，能够通过元数据监督预训练获得多样的语音能力，为摆脱对大规模预训练语音编码器的依赖提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Encoder-based speech large language models (Speech-LLMs) commonly employ pretrained speech encoders that prioritize linguistic content but may discard fine-grained acoustic cues essential for speaker discrimination and paralinguistic understanding. Encoder-free Speech-LLMs instead map Mel-spectrogram features directly into the LLM input space through lightweight embedding layers, enabling the LLM to learn from low-level acoustic features. However, systematic pretraining strategies for encoder-free Speech-LLMs remain underexplored, limiting their ability to compensate for the absence of large-scale pretrained speech encoders. We propose metadata-supervised pretraining (MSP), which leverages speech attributes such as speaker identity and emotion to develop speaker-discriminative and paralinguistic capabilities. We further introduce speaker-aware utterance composition (SAUC) to strengthen speaker discrimination and apply random span masking to regularize pretraining. We primarily evaluate our approach on joint ASR and speaker diarization in multi-speaker conversations, complemented by experiments on paralinguistic speech-understanding tasks. Under matched training-data conditions, our encoder-free model outperforms its randomly initialized encoder-based counterpart. With limited metadata-annotated data, it is competitive with models using speech encoders pretrained on substantially larger corpora, outperforming them in several settings. These results demonstrate the potential of encoder-free architectures for building native multimodal LLMs that acquire diverse speech capabilities.

</details>


### [186] [Shared-State Local Translations for Training-Free Voice Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01952)
*Yangyang Qu,Michele Panariello,Massimiliano Todisco,Nicholas Evans*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: StateVC：用共享状态（states）与状态级均值漂移实现无训练单样本语音转换，无需帧级对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单样本无训练语音转换中，源与参考语句语言内容不同，无法假定可靠的帧级对应关系，因此需要一种不依赖显式帧匹配的转换方法。

Method: 对源与参考语句的池化帧级WavLM表示拟合一个针对该语句对的特定高斯混合模型，联合定义一组共享局部区域（states）；在每个state内估计原始WavLM空间中的源到参考均值漂移；再用源帧后验概率组合各state的漂移，使不同帧获得不同的局部更新。

Result: 在LibriSpeech单样本协议上，WER 8.01%、CER 3.22%，均为所评估系统中最低；SIM为0.9512；在所评估系统中平均感知说话人相似度最高，在无训练系统中平均自然度最高。

Conclusion: 通过共享状态的构建与状态级均值漂移组合，StateVC在无显式帧匹配条件下实现了高质量的单样本无训练语音转换，在内容保真与说话人相似度等指标上表现领先。

Abstract: In one-shot training-free voice conversion (VC), the source and reference utterances may contain different linguistic content, so reliable frame-level correspondence between them cannot be assumed. We propose StateVC, which jointly defines a common set of local regions from pooled frame-level WavLM representations of the source and reference utterances; we refer to these regions as states. These shared states are obtained by fitting a pair-specific Gaussian mixture model to the pooled representations, without explicit source--reference frame matching. Within each state, StateVC estimates a source-to-reference mean shift in the original WavLM space. Source-frame posterior probabilities then combine the state-specific shifts so that different frames can receive different local updates. For the LibriSpeech one-shot protocol, StateVC achieves the lowest word error rate (WER) and character error rate (CER) among the evaluated systems, at 8.01% and 3.22%, respectively, with a speaker similarity (SIM) of 0.9512. It also achieves the highest mean perceived speaker similarity among the evaluated systems and the highest mean naturalness among the evaluated training-free systems.

</details>


### [187] [Multi-sample Synthetic Supervision for Accent Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01961)
*Yangyang Qu,Michele Panariello,Massimiliano Todisco,Nicholas Evans*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出多样本合成监督框架，通过联合评估候选波形的口音实现度与源保持度来构造口音转换目标，并提供离散语音码作为口音条件自回归适配的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口音转换需改变口音同时保持说话人身份和语言内容，但平行录音稀缺；利用语音合成提供监督时，生成目标在口音实现和源保持方面质量参差不齐。

Method: 针对每个源-口音条件，构造多个候选转换目标，联合评估其口音实现度与源保持度进行筛选；选中的候选提供离散语音码（表征语言内容、韵律和说话风格）作为口音条件自回归适配的目标。

Result: 相比每个训练样本仅用一个生成目标，目标口音分类准确率提升2.49个百分点，说话人相似度几乎不变，词错误率仅增加0.29个百分点；在六种目标口音上取得7.81% WER，并在评测系统中获得最高的平均主观听感评分。

Conclusion: 多候选联合筛选的合成监督可有效提升口音转换的目标口音准确度，同时基本保持说话人相似度和内容保真度，并带来更优的主观听感。

Abstract: Accent conversion (AC) requires changing accent while preserving speaker identity and linguistic content, yet parallel recordings are scarce. Speech synthesis provides an alternative source of supervision, but generated targets vary in accent realization and source preservation. We propose a multi-sample synthetic supervision framework that constructs conversion targets by jointly assessing these properties across candidate waveforms for each source--accent condition. Selected candidates provide associated discrete speech codes, representing linguistic content, prosody, and speaking style, as targets for accent-conditioned autoregressive adaptation. Compared with using one generated target per training example, our method improves target-accent classification accuracy by 2.49 percentage points, while speaker similarity remains nearly unchanged, and word error rate increases by 0.29 percentage points. Across six target accents, our method achieves 7.81% WER and the highest mean listening ratings among evaluated systems.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Finite Element Simulation of Microwave Technologies for PowderBased Volumetric Additive Manufacturing Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00842)
*Brendan Mackey,Levi Smith,Bosco Yu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过有限元模拟探索微波驱动的体积增材制造（VAM）系统，兼容陶瓷、金属或复合粉末原料，利用热晶格实现选择性加热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAM系统受限于光聚合树脂，通用性差，难以覆盖陶瓷、金属等工业材料，亟需开发与粉末原料兼容的新型VAM方法。

Method: 采用有限元模拟，研究三种微波基VAM装置：线性谐振器、交叉干涉仪和边界加热器；通过精确控制输入频率、功率和波导横模来构建热晶格。

Result: 可工程化热晶格以选择性加热原料区域；晶格单元尺寸约1 mm，所需输入功率约100 kW。

Conclusion: 随着微波精密控制技术进步，此类装置有望实现光学方法无法覆盖材料的VAM路径。

Abstract: Volumetric additive manufacturing (VAM) is a promising field of advanced manufacturing which reduces production times of conventional 3D printing while retaining application to complex geometries. Current VAM systems are limited to photo-polymerizing resins, which reduces the general applicability across industry. This work presents exploratory finite element simulations of microwave-based VAM systems compatible with ceramic, metal, or composite powder feedstock. The simulations explore three devices -- a linear resonator, a cross interferometer, and a boundary heater -- each with potential uses in manufacturing processes for one or multiple of the considered feedstock materials. We find that with precise control of input frequency, power, and waveguide transverse modes, thermal lattices can be engineered to selectively heat regions of the feedstock, allowing direct application to VAM. Resultant lattices have cellular dimensions on the order of 1 mm, and require input powers on the order of 100 kW. With advances in microwave technologies allowing for this precision, such devices could open pathways to VAM with materials currently outside the scope of optical methods.

</details>


### [189] [Upper Bounds on Efficiency of Pixel Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01107)
*Albert Salmi,Philipp del Hougne,Leo Lahti,Ville Viikari*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用半定松弛计算像素天线总效率基 bounds 上限的方法，并与遗传算法实现解进行对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素天线的像素状态为二值变量，其总效率最大化属于组合优化问题，难以求解，因此需要一种能够给出性能理论上界的方法来评估可实现设计极限。

Method: 采用半定松弛（semidefinite relaxation）技术，将带有二值像素状态的总效率最大化优化问题进行松弛，从而获得该问题的效率上界。

Result: 针对不同像素数量的天线计算了总效率上界，并将其与通过遗传算法获得的可实现解进行了比较。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效给出像素天线总效率的理论界限，为评估实际优化算法所得设计的性能提供参照基准。

Abstract: This letter presents a method to compute fundamental bounds on the total efficiency of pixel antennas. Using semidefinite relaxation, we obtain an upper bound on the optimization problem of maximizing the antenna's total efficiency subject to binary-valued pixel states. The bounds are demonstrated for antennas with varying numbers of pixels and compared with realizable solutions obtained using the genetic algorithm.

</details>


### [190] [Correlation Imaging via Hybrid Entanglement between Microwave Photons and Surface Acoustic Wave Phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01252)
*Yu-Yuan Chen,Ling-An Wu,Yu-xi Liu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于超导量子电路中微波光子-声子混合纠缠的关联成像方法，利用光子与声子五个数量级的波长差，实现跨尺度量子传感：可将宏观物体轮廓缩小10^-8~10^-6倍成像到微观声子芯片，或将微观物体轮廓放大10^4~10^6倍成像到宏观光子读出器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物理场量子传感需要在不同量子场之间建立有效的空间关联。微波光子与微波表面声波声子频率可匹配但波长相差悬殊，为"探测场与读出场分离"提供了独特资源，现有方法缺乏此类跨尺度关联手段。

Method: 基于超导量子电路产生微波光子与微波表面声波声子的混合纠缠；在微波频率匹配条件下，利用光子与声子约五个数量级的波长差异构成的量子纠缠资源；将自由空间长波长微波与片上短波长表面声波接口，构建关联成像方案。

Result: 实现两种具有巨大空间缩放能力的成像模式：一是将宏观物体轮廓去放大（缩小）10^-8~10^-6倍映射到微观声子芯片上；二是反过来将微观物体轮廓放大10^4~10^6倍映射到宏观光子读出器件中。

Conclusion: 通过将自由空间长波长微波与片上短波长表面声波耦合，该关联成像方案为跨尺度量子传感提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Establishing effective spatial correlations between distinct quantum fields is essential for multi-physics quantum sensing.~We here propose a correlation imaging approach based on the hybrid entanglement between microwave photons and microwave surface acoustic wave phonons generated via a superconducting quantum circuit.~At matched microwave frequencies, the five order-of-magnitude wavelength disparity between photons and phonons leads to a unique quantum entanglement resource, which allows the optimal field for probing an object to be distinct from that used for readout.~This resource enables two imaging modalities with giant spatial scaling: a $10^{-8}-10^{-6}$-fold demagnification of macroscopic object profiles onto microscopic phononic chips, and conversely, a $10^{4}-10^{6}$-fold magnification of microscopic object profiles into macroscopic photonic readout devices. By interfacing free-space long-wavelength microwaves with on-chip short-wavelength surface acoustic waves, our correlation imaging approach provides a general framework for cross-scale quantum sensing.

</details>


### [191] [Determination of van der Waals thin film thickness via characteristic X-ray photon yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01309)
*Guoshun Luo,Shichao Zhao,Xinyu Liu,Suguo Chen,Yue Wang,Ping Zhang,Zhigang Lu,Shaomeng Wang,Sunchao Huang,Yubin Gong*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用特征X射线产额无损测定范德华薄膜厚度的方法，建立了六阶多项式模型关联薄膜厚度与特征X射线产额，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟和实验验证了该方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华薄膜厚度的准确测定通常依赖破坏性或复杂手段，缺乏简便无损的表征方法；而特征X射线产生在X射线源和光-物质相互作用研究中具有重要作用，作者由此提出将特征X射线产生用于薄膜厚度的无损测定。

Method: 建立一个六阶多项式模型，将薄膜厚度与特征X射线产额相关联；利用该模型既能预测任意厚度石墨薄膜的X射线产额，也能由特征X射线产额反推薄膜厚度；通过蒙特卡洛模拟对比预测产额，并通过实验验证该方法的可行性与可靠性。

Result: 模型预测的石墨薄膜X射线产额与蒙特卡洛模拟结果吻合；实验验证了通过X射线光子产额表征提取石墨薄膜厚度的可靠性和可行性。

Conclusion: 特征X射线产生可用于范德华薄膜厚度的无损测定，为范德华薄膜厚度表征提供了一种新的无损方法。

Abstract: Characteristic X ray generation is essential for producing X ray sources and for investigating light matter interactions. Here, we demonstrate that characteristic X ray generation can also be employed to determine the thickness of van der Waals thin films in a non destructive manner. Specifically, we establish a sixth order polynomial model that relates film thickness to the characteristic X ray yield. The model can both predict the X ray yield of graphite films at arbitrary thicknesses and infer the film thickness from a corresponding characteristic X ray yield. We validate the model by comparing its predicted X ray yields for graphite films with those obtained from Monte Carlo simulations. Additionally, we experimentally validated the reliability and feasibility of the proposed method for extracting graphite film thickness via X ray photon yield characterization. Our results provide a non destructive approach for determining the thickness of van der Waals thin films.

</details>
